|| śrīḥ ||
|| jñāpakasaṃgrahaḥ ||
prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
aiuṇ |1| akāro vivṛta upadiṣṭaḥ prākriyādaśāyāṃ cetyatra "a a' (sū.8-4-68) iti pratyāpattivacanaṃ jñāpakam | vivṛtiḥ vṛṣaśikharinivāsaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ śritānāmabhimataphaladāne kalpavṛkṣātiśāyī | aharaharupayātaiḥ sevyamāno'rthibṛndaiḥ kalayatu kuśalaṃ naḥ ko'pi kāruṇyarāśiḥ || 1 || anekatantraniṣṇātān nityaṃ śiṣyahitodyatān | mahānubhāvadhaureyān namāmi śirasā gurūn || 2|| nāgeśabhaṭṭasudhiyā baddho jñāpakasaṃgrahaḥ | pārthasārathinā pūrvamāndhralipyāṃ prakāśitaḥ || 3 || prājñenājñāpitastena tasya vyākhyāṃ samārabhe | śabdaiḥ parimitāṃ nārthaiḥ viduṣāṃ cittarañjanīm || 4 || vyājenānena bhāṣyādigranthāloḍaḍḍatdh;najā mateḥ | śuddhiḥ saṃjāyate yā sā kṛterasyāḥ paraṃ phalam || 5 || tatrabhavān padavākyapramāṇapārāvārīṇo nāgeśabhaṭṭaḥ paribhāṣāvyatiriktāni mahābhāṣye tatra tatra sthitāni jñāpakasiddhavacanāni saṃkalayya jñāpakasaṃgrahanāmānaṃ granthaṃ kartumārabhamāṇaḥ prathamaṃ vacanamāha---akāro vivṛta iti | "aiuṇ' iti sūtre akāro vivṛtatayopadeṣṭavyaḥ ākāragrahaṇārtham iti vārtikakāreṇokte kiṃ punaridaṃ sūtrakāreṇaiva kṛtasyopadeśasya vārtikakāreṇa prayojanamucyate, utākṛta eva vivṛtopadeśo vārtikakṛtā kartavyatvenopanyasta ityākṣepe sati bhāṣyakāra āha---"vivṛtasyopadiśyamānasya prayojanamanvākhyāyate | kathaṃ jñāyate yadayaṃ "a a' ityakārasya vivṛtasya saṃvṛtatāpratyāpattiṃ śāsti' iti | asyāyamāśayaḥ--svabhāvataḥ saṃvṛtasyākārasya prakriyādaśāyāṃ vivṛtatvamāpannasya prayogakāle punaḥ saṃvṛtatvaprāptiḥ "a a' iti sūtreṇa pratipādyate | idaṃ ca sūtra śāstrānte vidyate | atastatsūtrātprāktaneṣu prakrayiādaśāpareṣu sūtreṣūpadiśyamānaḥ akāraḥ vivṛta ityavagamyate | tathā ca vivṛtatayā upadiśyamānasya akārasyeva vārtikakṛtā prayojanamākhyāyata iti viṣaye "a a' iti sūtraṃ noktaviṣaye jñāpakam, tasya prayojanāntarasadbhāvāt | tathā hi---"atikhaṭvaḥ, atimālaḥ' ityādau "gostriyorupasarjanasya' (sū 1-2-48) iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ ākārasya ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvaḥ sthāninaḥ ākārasya vivṛtatvāt tadāntaryāt vivṛto bhavet iti sa saṃvṛta eva yathā syādityevamarthaṃ "a a' iti sūtramiti kathaṃ tajjñāpakaṃ bhavet iti cet--na | yadi hi loke vede vā kutrāpi vivṛto'kāro bhavet tadā ākārasya sthāne bhavan ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvaḥ sthānita āntaryāt vivṛto bavet | na hi vivṛto'kāraḥ kutrāpyasti | api tu saṃvṛta evāsti | tathā ca saṃvṛta eva akāro bhaviṣyati iti "a a' iti sūtraṃ na atikhaṭva ityādau ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvasya saṃvṛtatvaphalakam | tataśca śāstrānte prakriyāsiddhyanantaramakārasya saṃvṛtatvaṃ vidadhatsūtraṃ pūrvasūtreṣu vivṛto'kāra evopadiṣṭaḥ pratyetavya iti jñāpayati | tataścopadiṣṭasyaiva vivṛtasya prayojanamanvākhyāyate vārtikakāreṇeti siddham | pratyāpattiḥ---svabhāvataḥ saṃvṛtasya vivṛtatvaṃ prāptasya punaḥ svasvabhāvabhūtasaṃvṛtatvaprāptiḥ | "prakriyādaśāyāmakāro vivṛta upadiṣṭa ityatra a a iti pratyāpattivacanaṃ jñāpakamityanvayaḥ | jhrjñāṭa ṛlṛk || 2 || prakṛtivadanukaraṇaṃ bhavati | "kṣiyaḥ'(sū.6-4-59) ityādāviyaṅnirdeśāt | jhrvi.ṭa ṛlṛk iti sūtre lṛkāropadeśaprayojanakathanāvasare aśaktijānukaraṇamekaṃ prayojanamuktaṃ bhāṣye | tathāhi - kayācit brāhmaṇyā ṛtaka iti prayoktavye aśaktyā yo lṛtaka iti prayogaḥ tasya yat
brāhmaṇylṛtaka ityāha ityanukaraṇaṃ tadaśaktijānukaraṇamityucyate | tatra brāhmaṇīalṛtaka iti sthite lṛkārasyāctvāt tasmin parataḥ "iko yaṇaci' (sū.6-1-77) iti sūtreṇa īkārasya yaṇi brāhmaṇylṛtaka iti bhavati | yadi tu ṛlṛksūtre lṛkāro nopadiśyeta ṛk ityevocyeta tadā lṛkārasyāctvā bhāvāt iko yaṇacīti sūtraṃ na pravarteta | ato lṛkāropadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ ityuktvā tasya pratyākhyānaṃ kṛtam | tathāhi "prakṛtivadanukaraṇaṃ bhavati' iti paribhāṣā bhavati | tasyāśca, anukaraṇaṃ prakṛtivat anukāryatulyaṃ bhavati, anukārye yatkāryaṃ bhavati anukaraṇe'pi tadbhavati ityarthaḥ | atra cānukārya ṛtakaśabdaḥ anukaraṇamlṛtakaśabdaḥ | anukārya ṛtaśabde parataḥ ṛkārasyāctvāt "iko yaṇaci (sū.6-1-77) iti sūtraṃ yathā pravartate tathā anukaraṇe lṛtakaśabde'pi tatsūtraṃ pravartate iti yaṇādeśasiddhayā lṛkāropadeśo māstu iti bhāvaḥ | "prakṛtivadanukaraṇaṃ bhavati' ityatra jñāpakaṃ tu "kṣiyodīrghāt'(sū.7-2-46) ityādāviyaṅ‌nirdeśaḥ | tathā hi sūtrasthaḥ "kṣi' śabdaḥ dhātupāṭhasthasya kṣi ityasyānukaraṇam | anukaraṇaśabdasya kriyāvācitvābhāvena dhātutvābhāvāt "aciśnudhātubhruvām'(sū.6-4-77) iti sūtreṇeyaṅ durlabhaḥ | iyaṅ tu nirdiṣṭaḥ pāṇininā | tenedaṃ jñāyate yat prakṛtivadanukaraṇaṃbhavatīti | tathā ca kṣidhātorbhavan iyaṅ tadanukaraṇāt kṣiśabdādapi bhavitumarhatīti svāṃśe caritārthatāḥ | pacantu pacantu iti dvivāraṃ kenacidukte tasyānukaraṇaṃ "dviḥ pacantu' iti | asya pacantuśabdasya kriyāsādhanādyavācakatvāktiṅantatvaṃ nāstīti "tiṅṅatiṅaḥ'(sū.8-1-27)iti sūtreṇa atiṅantāt paraṃ tiṅantamanudāttamityarthakena vidhīyamānaḥ nighātasvaraḥ na syāt | prakṛtivadanukaraṇaṃ bhavatīti vacane svīkṛte tu anukārye pacantu iti tiṅante nighātavat anukaraṇe pacantuśabde'pi sa svaraḥ setsyatīti | jhrjñādṛṭa eoṅ || 3 || gonaugrahaṇāt jñāpakāt nāvyapavṛktasyāvayavasya tadvidhiriti | jhrvidṛṭa ṭhaeoṃṅ' iti sūtre ya ete ākarādiṣkārasadṛśā avayavāḥ, ṛkāralṛkārayo rephalakārasadṛśāvavayavau, sandhyakṣareṣu e o ai au ityeteṣu akārekārokārasadṛśā avayavāḥ tatra kevalavarṇānāmakāradīnāṃ vidhīyamānaṃ kāryaṃ bhavati na veti vicāre, yadi varṇāvayavān varṇagrahaṇena gṛhītvā tatravarṇakāryaṃ kriyeta tadā agne indram vāyo udakamityādau ekārottarabhāgasya ikārasya ikāragrahaṇena grahaṇāt tasya indramiti savarṇa-ikāraparakatvāt okārasthasya ukārasya savarṇokāraparakatvācca "akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ' (sū.6-1-101) iti dīrghaḥ prāpnotīti doṣamāśaṅkya tatparihārāya idaṃ vacanamupāttam "nāvyapavṛktasyāvayasya tadvidhiriti |' avyapavṛktasya yo'vayavastasyeti vyadhikaraṇe ṣaṣṭhyau | avyapavṛktasya---abhinnabuddhiviṣayasya ekatvaviṣayakabuddhigrāhyasya ekamityākārakabuddhiviṣayasya samudāyasya yo'vayavaḥ tasya tadvidhiḥ---pṛthagbhūtavarṇavidhirna bhavatīti uktavacanasyārthaḥ | evaṃ ca "e ityakṣaramekaṃ' iti buddhigṛhīte e iti samudāye'vayavaḥ ya ikārastasya, samudāyāghaṭakasya ikārasya yo vidhiḥ savarṇadīrghādiḥ sa na bhavatīti agne indramityādau savarṇadīrghāpattidoṣo netyarthaḥ | uktavacane jñāpakamāha gonaugrahaṇāditi | ayamāśayaḥ--"godhdyaco'saṅkhyāparimāṇāśvāderyat'(sū.5-1-39) iti sūtrasthaṃ gograhaṇam, "naudhdyacaṣṭhan'(sū.4-4-7) iti sūtrasthaṃ naugrahaṇaṃ coktavacane jñāpakam | tatra prathamasūtrasya gośabdāt acdvayayuktācca śabdāt tasya nimittaṃ saṃyoga utpāto vetyarthe yadbhavati ityarthaḥ | gavyaṃ dhanya ityudāharaṇam | tatra gośabde ya okāraḥ akāraukarasamudāyarūpaḥ tadavayavayoḥ a u anayoḥ
pṛthagbhūtākāragrahaṇena ukāragrahaṇena ca grahaṇe sati gośabdasya dhdyackatvāt dhdyaca ityanenaiva grahaṇe siddhe gośabdagrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ sat samudāyāvayavasya varṇagrahaṇena grahaṇaṃ neti jñāpayati | evaṃ dvitīyasūtrasya nauśabdāt dhdyackaśabdācca taratītyarthe ṭhan bhavatītyarthaḥ | nāvikaḥ ghaṭika ityudāharaṇam | tatra nauśabde aukārasya varṇadvayasamudāyarūpatvena dhdyackatvaṃ astīti dhdyaca ityanenaiva tasya grahaṇe siddhe naugrahaṇamapi vyarthaṃ sat uktavacanaṃ jñāpayati| jñāpite ca tasmin okāraaukārayoḥ varṇadvayarūpatayā grahaṇābhāvāt dhdyackatvaṃ nāstīti pṛthak gonaugrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu agne indramityādau savarṇadīrghābhāva iti pūrvoktameveti | jhrjñādṛṭa varṇaukadeśānāṃ varṇagrahaṇenāgrahaṇamiti pakṣe kṣubhnādiṣu tṛpnotiśabdapāṭhāt ṛvarṇānnasya ṇatvaṃ jñāpyate | jhrvidṛṭa ṛlṛksūtre ākārādisandhyakṣaraikadeśabhūtānāṃ pṛthagbhūtavarṇagrahaṇenāgrahaṇamiti pakṣe mātṝṇāmityatra ṇatvaṃ na sidhyāti | mātṛ nām iti sthite ṛkārātparasya nakārasya ṇatvavidhāyakasūtrābhāvāt | grahaṇapakṣe tu ṛkāramadhyastharephātparatvamādāya "raṣābhyāṃ no ṇaḥ' (sū.8-4-1) iti sūtreṇa ṇatvaṃ bhaviṣyati ityākṣepe agrahaṇapakṣe'pi ṛvarṇātparasya nasya ṇatvaṃ jñāpakāt sādhyate | tathā hi---kṣūbhnādigaṇe nṛnamanaśabdaḥ paṭhitaḥ | tatprayojanaṃ tu "kṣubhnādiṣu ca'(8-4-39) iti sūtreṇa ṛkārātparasya nasya ṇatvaniṣedhaḥ | yadi tu ṛvarṇātparasyanasya ṇatvaṃ prāpakasūtrābhāvāt na bhavet tarhi ṇatvaprāptyabhāvāt tanniṣedhaphalakaḥ kṣubhnādigaṇe pāṭhaḥ vyarthaḥ syāt | na ca nārnamaniḥ ityatra rephātparasya nasya ṇatvaniṣedhārthaṃ tasya tadgaṇe pāṭha iti vācyam | evamapi tṛpnotiśabdasya tadgaṇe pāṭhasyoktavacanajñāpakatvāt | tṛpnotiśabde ṛvarṇātparasya nasya ṇatvābhāve prāptyabhāvānniṣedho vyartharasan jñāpayatyeva ṛvarṇānnasya ṇatvaṃ bhavatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa actadhdyāpyapratyāhāreṣvitāṃ na grahaṇam | "uṇādayaḥ'(sū.3-3-1) ityādijñāpakāt | (sū 5) jhrvi.ṭa ac pratyāhāre tadhdyāpyeṣu ak ik ityādipratyāhāreṣu ca madhyavartināmitsaṃjñakānāṃ varṇānāṃ ṇakārakakāradīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ na bhavati | uṇādaya iti nirdeśāt| ajādipratyāhāreṇa yadi madhyavartināṃ itāmapi grahaṇam tadā ṇakārasyāpcactvāt "uṇ' śabde aci ṇakāre pare ikaḥ ukārasya yaṇi vṇādayaḥ iti nirdeṣṭavyaṃ bhavet | yato'ckāryaṃ yaṇaṃ vinā uṇādaya iti nirdiṣṭam ataḥ pratyāhāreṣvitāṃ na grahaṇamitiṃ jñāpyate ityarthaḥ | tena śrīkānta ityādau īkārasya na yaṇ | atra halādipratyāhāramadhyavartināmapi itāṃ grahaṇaṃ neṣyate | evaṃ sthite actadvayāpyapratyāhāreṣviti kutaḥ proktamiti na jānīmaḥ | ata eva kaumudyām "pratyāhāreṣvitāṃ na grahaṇam' ityevoktam | vastutastu acpratyāhāre itāṃ grahaṇaṃ na ityeva jñāpakasiddhaṃ vacanam, na tatra tadhdyāpya ityasya praveśaḥ | na ca agādipratyāhāre itaḥ grahaṇāpattiriti vācyam | halṣu uccāraṇārthākāroccāraṇenoccāraṇārthavarṇahīnahalāṃ halghaṭitapratyāhāre grahaṇābhāvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | ata eva "pratyāhāre'nubandhānāṃ kathamajgrahaṇeṣu na' iti ślokavārtike "ajgrahaṇeṣu' ityevoktam iti sudhībhirākalanīyam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ kaiyaṭe || jhrjñā.ṭa hayavaraṭ || 5 || "halo yamāṃ yami'(sū.8-4-64) "jharo jhari'(sū.8-4-65) vibhāṣānuvṛttau "śaro'ci'(sū.8-4-49) iti dvitvaniṣedho jñāpakaḥ | vyākhyānaṃ vā śaraṇam | jhrvi.ṭa hayavaraṭsūtre antaḥ sthānāṃ yaralavānāmaṇsūpadeśaḥ kimartha iti pṛṣṭvā "sam̐yyantā' ityatra sam yantā iti sthite makārasya "mo'nusvāra' (sū.8-3-23) ityanusvāre tasya parasavarṇe ca kṛte sam̐y yantā ityatra pūrvayakārasya yargrahaṇena grahaṇāt "anaci ca'
(sū.8-4-47) iti dvitve triyakāraṃ rūpaṃ siddhyati | antaḥ sthānāmaṇsūpadeśābhāve yakārasyāṇtvābhāvāt saḥ aṇuditsūtreṇa savarṇānunāsikayakāragrāhako na bhavatīti pūrvasyānunāsikayakārasya yargrahaṇenāgrahaṇāt dvitvaṃ na bhavet | ato dvitvasiddhyarthaṃ yakāradīnāmupadeśaḥ | nanu mābhūdatra dvitvam | na ca sati dvitve triyakāraṃ dvitvābhāve dviyakāramiti rūpabhedāt dvitvamāvaśyakamiti vācyam | dvitve satyapi yakāratraye madhyayakārasya "halo yamāṃ yami lopaḥ' (sū.8-4-64) iti sūtreṇa lopāt dviyakārasyaiva rūpasya siddheḥ iti cet-syādetadevam yadi "halo yamāṃ yamīṃ' ti lopo nityaḥ syāt | sa tu vaikalpikaḥ | tathā ca dvitve sati yakāralopapakṣe dviyakāram, lopābhāvapakṣe triyakāramiti rūpabhedasiddhaye dvitvamāvaśyakam | tacca pūrvoktarītyā antarasthānāmaṇsūpadeśe satyeva ghaṭata iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | tadupara "halo yamāṃ yami'(sū.8-4-64) iti lopo vaikalpika iti kathaṃ jñāyate iti śaṅkāyāmāha vārtikakāraḥ--- "anuvartate vibhāṣā śare'ci yadvārayatyayaṃ dvitvam' iti | asyārthaḥ--"jharo jhari savarṇe' (sū.8-4-65) iti sūtre "jhayo ho'nyatarasyām' (sū.8-4-62) iti sūtrāt anyatarasyāmityanuvartate ityavaśyaṃ vācyam | anyathā "śaro'ci' (sū.8-4-49) iti dvitvaniṣedho vyarthaḥ syāt | "śaro'ci' ityanena hi aco rahābhyāṃ dve' (sū.8-4-46) iti sūtraprāptaṃ dvitvaṃ niṣidhyate | atreyamāśaṅkā-astvatra dvitvam | dvitve kṛte catūrṣṣu iti sthite "jharo iri savarṇe' iti prathamaṣakārasya lope iṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ siddhyatyeva | ataḥ "śaro'ci'(sū.8-4-49) iti sūtraṃ vyarthamiti | tatredaṃ samādhānaṃ vaktavyam yat "jharo jhari ' iti sūtre vikalpānuvṛttiṃ jñāpayatīdaṃ sūtram | tathā ca dvitve kṛte lopasya vaikalpikatvāt lopapakṣe ekaṣakāraṃ lopābhāvapakṣe dviṣakāramiti rūpadvayaṃ bhavet | asmākaṃ tu caturṣu tu caturṣu iti ekaṣakārameva rūpamiṣyate | ataḥ "śaro'ci' iti dvitvaniṣedhakaṃ sūtramiti sūtrasārthakyamiti | itthaṃ ca "śaro'ci' sūtreṇa "halo yamāṃ yami'(8-4-64) iti sūtre'pi anyatarasyāmityanuvartata iti siddhyati | tataśca sam̐yantā ityatra yakārasya dvitve halo yamāmiti lopapakṣe dviyakāraṃ lopābhāvapakṣe triyakāramiti bhedasiddhaye antaḥ sthānāmaṇsūpadeśa iti praghaṭṭakārthaḥ | tadetat sarvaṃ manasikṛtya "halo yamāṃ yami' "jharo jhari' iti sūtradvayamupādāya tataḥ vibhāṣānuvṛttau iti kathitavān | nan tatrevopasaṃhārabhāṣye---"tadetadatyantasaṃdigdhamācāryāṇāṃ vartate vibhāṣānuvartate na veti' iti kathitam | tathā ca kathametadityāśaṅkyāha--vyākhyānaṃ vā śaraṇamiti | sarvatra ca sandigdhasthale iyaṃ paribhāṣopatiṣṭhate "vyākhyānato viśeṣapratipattirna hi saṃdehādalakṣaṇa'miti |tathā ca pūrvauktasūtrayoḥ vibhāṣānuvartate iti vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | ato na ko'pi doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñādṛṭa laṇ || 6 || "ḍhralopa' ityatra pūrveṇāṇgrahaṇaṃ na pareṇa | "acaśca (sū.1-2-28) ityupasthityaiva siddhe aṇgrahaṇāt | jhrvidṛṭa ṭhalaṇ' sūtre evaṃ vicāraḥ pravṛttaḥ | ṇakārarūpaḥ it dviranubadhyate "aiuṇ' ityatra "laṇ' ityatra ca | tatra aṇ iṇ pratyāhārayoḥ sandeho bhavatiṃ kiṃ pūrveṇa ṇakāreṇāyaṃ pratyāhāra uta pareṇeti | asmin sandehe vārtikakāraḥ yatra yatrāṇgrahaṇamiṇgrahaṇaṃ ca vartate tatra sarvatra jñāpakamahimnā nirṇayaḥ kartuṃ śakya ityuktavān | tāniṃsthalāni saṅkalayyopanyasyati--"ḍhralopa' ityādinā | "ḍhralope pūrvasya dīrghe'ṇaḥ'(sū.6-3-211) iti sūtre aṇ kiṃ pūrvaṇakāreṇa pratyāhāraḥ uta pareṇa laṇsūtrasthaṇakāreṇeti saṃdehaḥ | pūrveṇāṇgrahaṇaṃ na pareṇeti nirṇayaḥ | kiṃ tatra ḍhralopasūtre'ṇgrahaṇaṃ vyartham | "acaśca (sū.1-2-28) iti paribhāṣāsūtreṇa hrasvadīrdhaplutaśabdairyatrājvidhīyate tatra aca iti ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ padamupatiṣṭhata ityarthakena aca iti padopasthityaiva aco ataḥ aṇgrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | ātṛḍha ityādau dīrghābhāvaḥ prayojanaṃ iti | nanvevamapi "ke'ṇaḥ'(sū.7-4-13) iti sūtre aṇ pūrveṇa pareṇa vā iti saṃdeha ityatrāha--- jhrjñādṛṭa ata eva "ke'ṇa' (sū. 7-4-13) ityatrāṇ pūrveṇaiva | jhrvidṛṭa ata eveti | aca ityupasthityā siddhatvādevetyarthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ--"ke'ṇaḥ' iti sūtraṇa kapratyaye parataḥ aṇo hrasvo vidhīyate | jñakā kumārikā ityudāharaṇam | tatrāṇ yadi pareṇa pratyāhāraḥ tadā laṇ paryantavarṇeṣu acāmeva ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvayogyatā vartata iti acāmeva hrasvo vidhitsito bhavet | tathā sati aca iti ṣaṣṭhyantapadopasthityaiva aco lābhe aṇgrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ bhavet | ato jñāpayati aṇ atra sūtre pūrveṇaiveti | prayojanaṃ tu gokā nauketyādau hrasvābhāvaḥ | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavat | nanvevamapi "aṇo'pragṛhyasyānunāsikaḥ' (sū.8-4-57) iti sūtre aṇ pūrveṇa pareṇa veti saṃdeha ityatrāha-- jhrjñā.ṭa aṇo'pragṛhyasyetyatrāpi aca iti vaktavye aṇgrahaṇātpūrveṇaiva | jhrvi.ṭa aṇo'pragṛhyasya iti | "aṇo'pragṛhyasya' iti sūtreṇāvasāne apragṛhyasyāṇo'nunāsiko vidhīyate | tatrāṇ yadi pareṇa pratyāhāraḥ tadā aca ityeva brūyāt | na ca hayavaralānāmapi anunāsikavidhānārthaṃ aṇa ityuktamiti vācyam | apragṛhyapadena nañivayuktanyāyena pragṛhyabhinnasya pragṛhyasadṛśasya ca grahaṇāt aca eva pragṛhyasaṃjñāsadbhāvāt pragṛhyabhinnaḥ actvena tatsadṛśaḥ ajeva apragṛhyāṇpadena grahītuṃ śakyeteti aca iti vaktavye aṇa ityuktervyarthatvāt | tathācātra aṇ pūrveṇaiveti aṇgrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati | tathā sati a i ueteṣāmeva grahaṇamtra vivakṣitam | tacca aca ityukte na sidhyediti aṇa ityuktamiti svāṃśecāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu kartṛ ityādau ṛkārasyānunāsikatvaṃ na bhavatīti | nanvevamapi "uraṇ raparaḥ'(sū.1-1-51) iti sūtre aṇ pūrveṇa pareṇa veti sandeha ityatrāha--- jhrjñā.ṭa urajrapara iti | ṛkārasthāne hayavaralānāmasambhavāt aca eva ṛsthānikatvasambhavāt uraj iti vaktavye uraṇ ityuktirvyarthā | na ca ac aṇ ityanayoḥ samānamātrāvattvāt lāghavagauravacarcā nāstīti vācyam | aṇ padena ajupasthāpitākṣaradhikākṣaraṇāmapyupasthitiriti upasthitikṛtagauravasya aṇ padoccāraṇe sadbhāvāt | tataśca uraj iti vaktavye uraṇ ityuktiḥ aṇpadaṃ pūrveṇa ṇakāreṇa pratyāhāra iti jñāpayati | tathā ca ajityukte ṛkārādīnāmapi grahaṇaṃ bhavet | tadhdyāvṛttyarthaṃ aṇ ityuktamiti svāśe cāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃtu mātṝṇāmityādau ṛkārasthāne bhavan dīrghaḥ raparo na bhavatīti | nanvevamapi "aṇuditsavarṇasya cāpratyayaḥ' (sū.1-1-69) iti sūtre aṇ pūrveṇa pareṇa sandeha ityata āha--- jhrjñā.ṭa aṇudidityatra pareṇa "urṛt' iti taparakaraṇāt jñāpakāt | jhrvi.ṭa aṇudiditi | savarṇagrāhake "aṇuditsavarṇasya cāpratyayaḥ' (sū.1-1-69) iti sūtre aṇ pareṇaiva ṇakāreṇa, na pūrveṇa| atra ca "urṛt' (7-4-7) iti sūtre taparakaraṇaṃ jñāpakam | tathā hi acīkṛtadityādau kṝtadhātoścurādeḥ luṅi caṅi dvitvādau
acīkṝt a at ityatraupadhāyā ṛvarṇasya sthāne ṛtsyādvā caṅūpare ṇau ityarthakena "urṛt' (sū.7-4-7) iti sutreṇa ṝkārasya ṛkāre acīkṛtat iti rūpam | atra ṝkārasya sthāneṛkāra eva yathā syāt ṝkāre mā bhūdityevamarthaṃ taparatvaṃ kriyate | yadi cāṇuditsūtre pūrveṇa ṇakāreṇāṇgrahaṇaṃ syāt tadā ṛkāro'naṇtvāt bhinnakālasya ṝkārasya grāhaka eva na bhavatīti ṛkāra eva ādeśo bhaviṣyati na ṝkāra iti kiṃ taparatvena | kṛtaṃ ca tat jñāpakaṃ pareṇāṇgrahaṇasya | tataśca ṛkārasyāpyaṇtvāt sa savarmasya ṝkārasyāpiṃ grāhaka iti kadācit ṝkāro'pi bhavet sa mā bhūt ṛkāra eva bhavatu ityetadarthaṃ taparakāraṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyat | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu ṛkāra aṣṭādaśānāṃ saṃjñeti | nanu urṛt iti sūtre taparakaraṇābhāve'pi ṛkāro vidhīyamānaḥ apratyaya ityukteḥ savarṇaṃ na grahīṣyatīti taparakaraṇaṃ vyarthamiti cet atrāha-udyotakāraḥ---"taparatvābhāve ādeśāntaranivṛttyarthatvena svarūpābhyanujñānārthatvenāpūrvabodhyatvābhāvena cāvidheyatvāt' iti | nanvevamapi "iṇaḥ ṣaḥ'(sū.8-3-39) ityādāviṇgrahaṇeṣu pūrveṇa pareṇa vā ṇakāreṇa pratyāhāra iti sandeha ityat āha--- jhrjñā.ṭa iṇ pareṇaiva | "aciśnudhāt' (sū 6-4-77) iti sūtre iṇa ityeva siddhe yvorityukterjñāpakat | jhrvi.ṭa iṇ pareṇaiva iti | "aci śnudhātubhruvām' iti sutre yvoḥ ityanena ivarṇovarṇayoḥ grahaṇam | yadi ca iṇ pratyāhāraḥ pūrveṇa ṇakāreṇa tadā iṇ ityanenāpi iuvarṇayoreva grahaṇam | tataśca yvoḥ iti pade yakāravakārayorekā mātrā okārasya dve mātre visargasyārghamātrā iti āhatya sārdhastistro mātrā bhavanti | iṇaḥ ityatra tu ikārasyaikā ṇkārasyārdhaṃ akārasyaikā visargasyārdhamiti tistro mātrā iti ardhamātrālaghavaṃ vartate | ato lāghavāt iṇaḥ iti vaktavye yvoḥ ityuktiḥ iṇpareṇa ṇakāreṇeti jñāpayati | tathā ca iṇaḥ ityukte ṛkārādīnāmapi grahaṇaṃ bhavediti tadvāraṇāya yvorityuktamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu "iṇaḥ ṣaḥ' (su 8-3-39) ityādau okārasyāpi grahāt goṣūktamityādau ṣatvasiddhiriti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhavṛddhirādaic' || 1-1-1 || "asthidadhisakthyakṣṇāmanaṅudāttaḥ'(su.7-1-75) ityudāttagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ "abhedakā guṇāḥ' iti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaviddhirādaic' iti sūtre ākārasya taparakaraṇaṃ kartavyam, savarṇārthamiti vārtikakāreṇoktam | tasya guṇānāṃ bhedakatvāt yadraguṇasyoccāraṇaṃ tadraguṇasyaiva saṃjñāprasaṅge guṇāntarayuktānāmapi saṃjñāpraptyarthaṃ taparakāraṇamityarthaḥ | tadupari guṇāḥ ānunāsikyodāttādayaḥ bhedakā abhedākāśceti pakṣadvayamupanyasya, ubhayamidaṃ guṇeṣūktaṃ bhedakā iti kiṃ punaratra nyayyam ityāśaṅkya abhedakā guṇā ityeva nyāyyam | kuta etat | yadayaṃ "asthidadhisaktthyakṣṇāmanaṅudāttaḥ' ityudāttagrahaṇaṃ karoti | yadi bhedakā guṇāḥ syuḥ udāttamevoccārayet ityuktaṃ bhāṣyakṛtā | ayamabhiprāyaḥ | udāttānaṅuccāraṇenaiva guṇasyodāttatvasya anudāttādibhyo bhedakatvāt anudāttādyanaṅvyāvṛttirbhaviṣyatīti tathā anuccārya yata udātta iti pṛthakpadamupāttam tena jñāyate yat guṇā abhedakā iti | tataścodāttatayoccāraṇekṛte'pi udāttatvasya itarabhedakatvābhāvāt anudāttasyāpyanaṅo grahaṇaṃ syāditi tadvyāvṛttyarthamudāttamiti svāṃśe caritārthatvam | tataśca ākārasya nāntarīyakayatkiñcidguṇavattayā uccāraṇe kṛte'pi guṇasyābhedakatayā guṇāntarayuktasyāpyākārasya grahaṇasiddhau tatphalakaṃ vṛddhirādaic iti sūtre taparakāraṇamiti na yujyata iti prakaraṇārthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa iko guṇa || 1-1-2 || "ato halādeḥ' (sū.7-2-7) ityatrākāragrahaṇaṃ
ṇiśvipratiṣedhavacanaṃ ca jñāpakaṃ na sicyantaraṅgaṃ bhavatīti | apavādatvātsiddhe iyamekadeśyuktiḥ | jhrviṭa iko guṇavṛddhī iti sūtre vṛddhigrahaṇaṃ viṅati ca iti sutre'nuvṛttyartham | anyathā tena sūtreṇa guṇamātraṃ niṣidhyeta | tataśca nyanuvīt nyadhuvit ityādau "sici vṛddhiḥ parasmaipadeṣu' (sū.7-2-1) iti sūtreṇa vṛddhiḥ syāt | vṛddhigrahaṇe kṛte ca viṅati cetyanena vṛddherapi niṣedhānna doṣaḥ ityuktvā, tadupari naitadasti prayojanam antaraṅgatvādatrovaṅādeśe kṛte ukārasyānte'vidyamānatvādvṛddhirna bhaviṣyati | atastatra vṛddhyabhāvaphalakaṃ iko guma iti sūtre vṛddhigrahaṇaṃ māratu iti pūrvapakṣaṃ prāpayya, "na sicyantaraṅgaṃ bhavati | yadayaṃ ato ilāderlaghoḥ ityakāragrahaṇaṃ karoti' iti bhāṣyakṛtā uktam | ayaṃ bāvaḥ |"ato halāderlaghoḥ (sū.7-2-7) iti sūtreṇa halāderlaghorakārasya iḍaḍḍatdh;ādau parasmaipadapare sici vṛddhirvikalpena vidhīyate | akhādīt akhadīt ityudāharaṇam | atra akārasyetyanuktau akoṣīt amoṣīt ityādau a-kuṣ-īt ityavakthāyāṃ halādairlaghorukārasyāpi vṛddhirbhavet | sā mā bhūdityevamarthaṃ akārasyetyuktamiti vaktavyam | yadi tu siti parataḥ antaraṅgaṃ kāryaṃ bhavet tadā sicparasmaipadobhayasāpekṣavṛddhyapekṣayā antaraṅgatvāt laghūpadhaguṇe kṛte okārasya laghutvābhāvādeva vṛddhiḥ na bhaviṣyati iti akāragrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | vyarthaṃ ca tat "na sicyantaraṅga bhavati' iti jñāpayati | tataścāntaraṅgaguṇāpravṛttyā paratvādukārasya vṛddhirbhavediti tadvāraṇāya akāragrahaṇamiti svāṃśecaritārthatā | evaṃ "hmyantakṣaṇaśvasajāgṛṇiśvyeditām (sū.7-2-5) iti sūtrasthaṃ ṇiśvigrahaṇamapyuktaṃvacane jñāpakam | tathā hi hamayāntasya kṣaṇāderṇyantasya śvayatereditaśca vṛddhiḥ na syādiḍaḍḍatdh;ādau sici iti tasya sūtrasyārthaḥ | aunayīt aśvayīt ityudāharaṇam | yadi sicyantaraṅgaṃ bhavet tarhi antaraṅgatvāt guṇe kṛte iṣṭarūpasiddhyā ṇiśvigrahaṇamakartavyaṃ syāt | kṛtaṃ ca tat na sicyantaraṅgaṃ bhavatīti jñāpayati tataśvāntaraṅgaguṇāpravṛttyā vṛddhiprāptau tatpratiṣedhārthaṃ ṇiśvigrahaṇam | itthaṃ ca nyanuvīt ityādau sici antaraṅgasyovaṅaḥ apravṛttyā vṛddhiprāptau satyāṃ viṅati ceti sūtreṇa iglakṣaṇavṛddherniṣedhasiddhaye sici vṛddhiriglakṣaṇā āstheyeti tadarthaṃ "iko guṇavṛddhī' iti sutre vṛddhigrahaṇamiti sthitam | vastutastu antaraṅgaguṇasyāpavādabhūtayā vṛddhyā bādhanāta vṛddhiprāptau tanniṣedhārtha ṇiśvigrahaṇamiti tasya uktavacanajñāpakatvaṃ na sambhavatītyāha-apavādatvātsiddhi iti | jhrjñādṛṭa ṭhakṅiti ca' (sū.1-1-5) "trasigṛdhidhṛṣikṣipeḥ knuḥ' (sū.3-2-140) "iko jhal' (sū.1-2-9) iti knusanoḥ kittvāt jñāpakāt upadhālakṣaṇasyāpi guṇasya pratiṣedho'yamiti tannimittagrahaṇaṃ na kāryam | jhrvidṛṭa ṭhakṅiti ca' iti sutre "kṅiti pratiṣedhe tannimittagrahaṇaṃ upadhāroravīrtytham' iti vārtikakāreṇoktam | kṅiti ca iti sūtre "iko guṇavṛddhī' iti sūtramanuvartate | tatra yadi kṅitītyetat iko viśeṣaṇam tadā kiti ṅiti ca parataḥ yaḥ ik tasya guṇavṛddhī na staḥ iti sūtrārthaḥ syāt | tathā sati ciṃta stutam ityādāveva ikaḥ kitparakatvamastīti guṇaniṣedho labhyeta | bhinna bhinnavān ityādau tu bhid ta ityatra upadhāyāḥ ikārasya kitparakatvaṃ nāsti dakāreṇa vyavadhānāditi guṇaniṣedho na labhyeta | ataḥ tannimitta iti padamadhyāhṛtya sūtrārtho varṇanīyaḥ | tadā kṅinnimitte ye guṇavṛddhī prāpnutaḥ te na bhavata iti sūtrārthaḥ | tataśca upadhāguṇasyāpi kṅinnimittakatvāt tasya pratiṣedhalābhādiṣṭasiddhiḥ | ataḥ upadhāyāḥ apīkaḥ guṇavṛddhipratiṣedhārthaṃ tannimittagrahaṇam | evaṃ rudhātoryaṅlugantasya laṭi prathamapuruṣaikavacane "roru īti' ityavasthāyāṃ "sārvadhātukārdhadhātukāyoḥ' iti tibnimittake ukārasya guṇo'vādeśe ca
roravīti iti rūpam | tannimittagrahaṇābhāve yaṅi avyavahitapūrvaḥ yaḥ tannimittakatvaṃ guṇasya nāstīti tasya na pratiṣedha itīṣṭarūpasiddhiḥ | tasmāt bhinna ityādau guṇapratiṣedhārthaṃ roravīti ityādau guṇapratiṣedhābhāvārthaṃ ca kiṅati ceti sūtre tannimittagrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiti vārtikābhiprāyaḥ | bhāṣyakārastu upadhāyā ikaḥ jñāpakena guṇapratiṣedhaṃ siṣādhayiṣuḥ tannimittagrahaṇaṃ pratyācakhyau | tadāha-trasigṛdhītyādi | "trasigṛdhidhṛṣikṣipeḥ knuḥ' (sū.3-2-140) iti sutreṇa trasyādibhyaḥ knu pratyayo vidhīyate | tatra kakāraitsaṃjñakaḥ | tatprayojanaṃ tu gṛdhnuḥityādau kṅiti ceti guṇaniṣedhaḥ | yadi ca kṅitparakasyaiveko guṇo'nena niṣidhyeta tadā dhakāreṇa vyavadhānāt ṛkārasya knuparakatvaṃ nāstīti guṇaniṣedho na prarvatsyati | tadarthaṃ kriyamāṇastu kakārānubandhovyarthaḥ sanū bhavatyupadhālakṣaṇasya guṇasya vyavahitaviṣayasyāpi kṅitītyanena pratiṣedhaḥ iti jñāpayati | tathā "halantācca (sū.1-2-10) iti sūtreṇa iksamīpaddhalaḥ paro jhalādiḥ san kit syādityucyate | jughukṣati ityādau ukārasya upadhāyāḥ guṇābhāvaḥ prayojanam | upadhāyāḥ kitparakatvābhāvāt guṇaniṣedho na bhaviṣyatīti prayojanābhāvāt kidgrahaṇamanarthakaṃ sat upadhālakṣaṇasya guṇasya pratiṣedha iti jñāpayatītyarthaḥ | tathā ca bhinna ityādāvanena jñāpakenaiva guṇapratiṣedhe labdhe tannimittagrahaṇamanāvasyakamiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa yāsuṭo ṅidvacanāt jñāpakāt ṅidādeśānāṃ ṅiti yat kāryaṃ tanna | tena acinavamityādau na niṣedha iti vārtikamatam (1-1-5) jhrvi.ṭa nanu cidhātorlaṅi mipi amādeśe a-ci-nu-am iti sthite laṅādeśasya amaḥ sthānivadbhavena ṅittvāt kṅiti ceti guṇaniṣedhaḥ syādtyāśaṅkyāha--yāsuṭa iti | "yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣūdātto ṅicca' (sū.3-4-103) iti sūtraṇa yāsuṭo ṅitvavidhānaṃ vyartham | bhūyās-ti ityatra liṅādeśasya tipaḥ sthānivadbhāvena ṅittvāt yāsuṭaśca yadāgamanyāyena ṅittvasiddheḥ | tathā ca ṅidvidhānaṃ "ṅidādeśā ṅito na bhavantī' ti jñāpayati | tataśca ṅito liṅa ādeśo|pi tip ṅit na bhavatīti yāsuṭo ṅittvaṃ durlabhamiti tasya ṅidvidhānam sārthakam | jñāpanaprayoḍaḍḍatdh;janaṃ tu acinavamityādau ādeśasya ṅittvābhāvāt guṇaniṣedho na bhavatīti | bhāṣyakāreṇa "sārvadhātukamapit' (sū.1-1-4) iti sutre apiditi yogaṃ vibhajya ṅiditi cānurvatya cāśritya ṅicca pinna, piñca ṅinna bhavatīti vākyārthadvayaṃ varṇitam | asmin pakṣe yāsuṭo ṅidvacanaṃ na jñāpakam, api tu mṛjyādityādāvaprāptaṅittavavidhāyakam | tadetat manasikṛtyāha--- iti vārtikamatamiti | jhrjñādṛṭa ṭhaīdūdeddvivacanam' (sū.1-1-11) "plutapragṛhyā' (sū.3-1-125) iti jñāpakāt siddhaḥ plutaḥ svarasandhiṣu | yadvā pluta iti kṛtameva āśrayātsiddhatvaṃ yathā rorutve | jhrvidṛṭa īdūdediti taparakaraṇena atatkālavyāvartanāt atatkālānāṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñā na prāpnotītyāśaṅkya pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ kartavyāyāṃ plutasyāsiddhatvāt tatkālāḥ dvimātrā īkārādayo bhavantīti pragṛhyasaṃjñā bhaviṣyatīti samādhāya paścādākṣeparūpeṇa bhāṣyakārairuktam -"siddhaḥ plutaḥ svarasandhiṣu | yadayaṃ "plutaḥ prakṛtyā' iti plutasya prakṛtibhāvaṃ śāsti | kathaṃ kṛtvā jñāpakam | sato hi kāryiṇaḥ kāryeṇa bhavitavyam' iti | "plutapragṛhyā' iti sutreṇa plutasya prakṛtibhāvo vidhīyate | yadi svarasandhau plutaḥ asiddhaḥ syāt tadāplutasyaivābhāvāt plutaṃ kāryitvena āśritya tasya prakṛtibhāvavidhānaṃ na yujyate | tenānumīyate yatsvarasandhau plutasyāsiddhatvaṃ na bhavatīti tadāśayaḥ | tadāha--pluteti | sāmānyena svarasandhiprakāraṇaviṣayaṃ siddhatvaṃ na jñāpyate, kiṃtu prakṛte prakṛtibhāva evetyāśayenāha--yadveti | prakṛta
eveti pāṭhaḥ sādhuḥ | pluta ityāśrayāt siddhatvaṃ prakṛta evetyanvayaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa niāta ekāc (sū.1-1-14) idamevaikagrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati varṇagrahaṇe jātigrahaṇamiti | altvādyāśrayānekagrahaṇamiti tadarthaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhanipāta ekājanāṅ' iti sūtre ekagrahaṇaṃ māstu | na ca tadabhāve aco nipātaviśeṣaṇatvāt tadantavidhau ajantasya nipātasya pragṛhyasaṃjñā iti sūtrarthe predaṃ brahma ityādau praaidam ityavasthāyāṃ pra ityajantasya nipātasya pragṛhyatve sandhikāryaṃ na syāditi vācyam | nipātasya ajviśeṣaṇatvamāśritya nipātarūpo yo'c tasya pragṛhyasaṃjñā iti sūtrārthaṅgīkāre pra iti nipātasya ajrūpatvābhāvena pragṛhyatvāprasakteḥ | tataśca ekagrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkya bhāṣyakāra āha--- "evaṃ tarhi siddhe sati yadajgrahaṇe kriyamāṇe ekagrahaṇaṃ karoti tajjñāpayatyācāryaḥ "anyatra varṇagrahaṇe jātigrahaṇaṃ bhavatīti' iti | varṇagrahaṇe sati jātyāśrayānekavarṇagrahaṇamiti ac ityanena actvāśrayānekavarṇasamudāyaghahaṇaṃ syāt | tataśva titauśabdaikadeśa a u śabdānkaraṇe nipātasaṃjñake au akarodityādau au iti samudāye pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ prakṛtibhāvādyaṇādeśo na syāt | ataḥ tadvāraṇāya ekagrahaṇam | kṛte ca tasmin tatsārmathyāt eko yo'c tadrarūpasya nipātasyaiva pragṛhyatvamiti na doṣa iti | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu bhāṣye uktam--- "dambheīlgrahaṇasya jātivācakatvātsiddham iti yaduktaṃ tadupapannaṃ bhavati' iti | tasyāyamarthaḥ--dambheḥ sani "dambha icca' (sū.7-4-56) iti sūtreṇa dhātoracaḥ itve dimbh sa iti sthite "halantācca' (sū.1-2-10) iti sūtreṇa sanaḥ kittvāt tasmin pare dhātūpadhāyā nakārasya "aniditām' (sū.6-4-24) iti lope bhaṣbhāvādau dhipsati iti rūpam | tatra "halantācca' (sū.1-2-10) iti sūtrasya iksamīpāddhalaḥ paraḥ jhalādiḥ san kit syādityarthaḥ | dinbh sa iti daśāyāṃ iksamīpo yo hal nakāraḥ tataḥ paraḥ san na bhavati, yataśca bhakāratparaḥ san sa ca bhakāro neksamīpaḥ nakāreṇa vyavadhānāditi kittvābhāve nalopo durlabhaḥ syāt | varṇagrahaṇe jātigrahaṇamityaṅgīkāre tu haltvajātyāśrayānekasyeksamīpatvaṃ tataḥ paratvaṃ sanaḥ iti tasya kittve tasiman pare dhātunakāralope ca dhipsatīti siddhiḥ prayojanamiti || jhrjñā.ṭa bahugaṇa (sū.1-1-23) || "nadīpaurṇamāsyāgra' (sū.5-4-110) ityatra na nadīsaṃjñakagrahaṇam, paurṇamāsyāgrahāyaṇīgrahaṇasārmathyāt | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhabahugaṇavatuḍaḍḍatdh;ti saṅkhyā' (sū.1-1-23) iti sūtre saṅkhyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | bahugaṇavatuḍaḍḍatdh;tayaḥ saṅkhyāsaṃjñāḥ bhavanti saṃkhyā ca saṃkhyāsaṃjñā bhavatīti vaktavyam | anyathā kṛtrimākṛtrimayoḥ kṛtrime kāryasampratyaya iti paribhāṣayā saṃkhyāśabdaghaṭiteṣu vidhisūtreṣu saṃkhyetyanena kṛtrimāyāḥ saṃkhyāyāḥ bahugaṇādereva grahaṇaṃ syāt na tvekādikāyāḥ saṃkhyāyā iti vārtikakāreṇokte tadupari--- "yadi tarhi kṛtrimākṛtrimayoḥ kṛtrime saṃpratyayo bhavati "nadīpaurṇamāsyāgrahāyaṇībhyaḥ' atrāpi prasajyeta ' iti bhāṣye ākṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ | kṛtrimākṛtrimanyāyāṅgīkāre "nadīpaurṇamāsī' iti sūtre nadī ityanena "yū stryākhyau nadī' (sū.1-4-3) iti sūtrabodhitanadīsaṃjñakānāṃ ī ū varṇāntānāmeva grahaṇaṃ syāt na tu nadī iti śabdasyeti tadarthaḥ | asminnākṣepe samādhānabhāṣyam ---- "paurṇamāsyāgrahāyaṇīgrahaṇasārmathyānna bhaviṣyati' iti | yadi śāstrabodhitanadīsaṃjñakaśabdasya nadī ityanena grahaṇaṃ syāt tadā paurṇamāsī āgrahāyaṇī ityanayorapi nityastrīliṅgaīkārāntatvena nadīsaṃjñakatvāt nadī ityanenaiva grahaṇe sidve pṛthak tayoḥ sutre nirdeśo'narthakaḥ syāt | kṛtaśca sa nirdeśaḥ "nadīpaurṇamāsī' iti sutre nadīśabdaḥ na kṛtrimanadīsaṃjñakavācī | api tu nadīśabdaparaḥ | iti jñāpayati, ato na doṣa iti tadāśayaḥ |
tadetadāha---nadītyādinā | tadanantaraṃ "nadīpaurṇamāsī' iti sutre nadīśabdasya lokaprasiddhanadīparatve nadīviśeṣavācināṃ gaṅgā yamunā ityādīnāmapi tatsūtre nadīpadena grahaṇaṃ syādityāśaṅkya--- "ācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati tadviśeṣebhyo na bhavatīti | yadayaṃ vipāṭśabdaṃ śaratprabhṛtiṣu paṭhati' iti bhāṣye uktam | "nadīpaurṇamāsī' iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ ṭac nadīviśeṣavācigaṅgādiśabdebhyo na bhavati | yadi bhavet tadā śaratprabhṛtiṣu vipāṭśabdapāṭho vyartho bhavet | prativipāśamityādau "avyayībhāve śaratprabhṛtibhyaḥ' (sū.5-4-107) iti sūtreṇa ṭacsiddhirhi tatprayojanam | tatra "nadīpaurṇamāsī' iti sūtre nadīśabdena nadīviśeṣavācivipāṭśabdasyāpi grahaṇāt tenaiva ṭacsiddheḥ śaratprabhṛtigaṇe vipāṭśabdapāṭho vyarthaḥ san tatsutre nadīśabdaḥ na nadīviśeṣapara iti jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | tadetadāha--- jhrjñā.ṭa śaradādiṣu viṣāṭśabdapāṭhāt jñāpakānna viśeṣagrahaṇam | iti | jhrjñā.ṭa saṃkhyādeśeṣu ekādisaṃkhyāgrahaṇam | kanvidhau "atiśadantāyāḥ' (sū.5-1-22) iti pratiṣedhāt jñāpakāt | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhabahuguṇavatuḍaḍḍatdh;ti' (sū.1-2-31) iti sūtre ekādikāyāḥ saṃkhyāyāḥ api saṃkhyāsaṃjñāsiddhyarthaṃ saṃkhyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiti vārtikaṃ pratyākhyātuṃ jñāpakena saṃkhyāpradeśeṣu ekādisaṅūkhyāyāḥ grahaṇaṃ samarthitam | tadāha-saṃkhyādeśeṣu iti | saṃkhyādeśeṣu saṃkhyā iti śabdaghaṭiteṣu vidhisūtreṣu ekādikāyāḥ saṅkhyāyā api grahaṇaṃ bhavati | tatra pramāṇamāha-kanvidhāviti | ayamarthaḥ- "saṃkhyāyāḥ atiśadantāyāḥ kan' (sū.5-1-22) iti sūtraṃ saṃkhyāyāḥ ārhīye'rthe kanaṃ vidadhat tyantāyāḥ śadantāyāśca na kan yathā sāptatikaḥ, śadantāyāḥ cātvāriṃśatka iti | ubhayatra "tena krītam' (sū.5-1-37) iti ṭhañ | yadi vidhisūtreṣu saṃkhyā ityanena akṛtrimāyā ekādisaṃkhyāyā grahaṇaṃ na syāt tadā viṃśati-catvāriṃśadādeḥ saṃkhyāgrahaṇenāgrahaṇāttataḥ kano'prasaktyā atiśadantāyā iti niṣedho'navasaraḥ syāt | ataḥ sa niṣedhaḥ ekādikāyā api saṃkhyāpradeśeṣu grahaṇaṃ jñāpayatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhaṣṇāntā ṣaṭ' (sū.1-1-24) || aṣṭano dīrghagrahaṇaṃ ṣaṭtvajñāpakam | ākārāntasya maḍaḍḍatdh;rtham (nuḍaḍḍatdh;rtham) | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaṣṇāntā ṣaṭ' iti sūtre upadeśagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | upadeśeṣakārāntā nakārāntā ca saṃkhyā ṣaṭsaṃjñā bhavatīti vaktavyam | anyathā śatāni sahastrāṇītyādau numiṃ kṛte nakārāntatvāt ṣaṭsaṃjñāyāṃ "ṣaḍḍḍatdh;bhyo luk' (sū.7-1-22) iti jasśasorlugāpatteḥ | aṣṭānāmityatra aṣṭan ām iti sthite paratvānnityatvācca "aṣṭana ā vibhaktau' (sū.7-2-84) iti nakārasya ātve kṛte nakārāntatvābhāvena ṣaṭsaṃjñāyā aprāptau "ṣaṭcarturbhyaśca' (sū.7-2-55) iti nuḍho'nupapatteśca ityākṣipya, śatāni sahastrāṇītyādau sannipātaparibhāṣayā ṣaṭsaṃjñāṃ vārayitvā aṣṭānāmityatra ātve kṛte'pi ṣaṭsaṃjñā jñāpakamahimnā sādhitā bhāṣyavārtikayoḥ | tadāha-aṣṭano dīrghagrahaṇamiti | "aṣṭano dīrghat' (sū.6-1-182) iti sūtreṇa dīrghāntādaṣṭanśabdāt parasyāḥ śasādivibhakterudāttatvaṃ vidhīyate | tatra ca dīrghagrahaṇam aṣṭasu ityatra supa udāttatvanivṛttyarthaṃ kriyate | yadi ca kṛtātvasya ṣaṭsaṃjñā na syāt tadā aṣṭasu ityatra "aṣṭana' (sū.6-1-182) iti sūtrāpekṣayā paratvāt "jhalyupottamam' (sū.6-1-180) iti sutereṇa ṣaṭtricaturbhyo yā jhalādivibhaktistadante pade upottamamudāttaṃ syādityarthakena upottamasvareṇa ṭakārottarākāra udātta iti iṣṭasvarasiddhyā kiṃ dīrghagrahaṇena | cāpavādatvāt aṣṭasu ityatra "jhalyupottamam' iti svaraṃ bādhitvā "aṣṭanaḥ' iti sūtravihito vibhaktisvara eva syāditi vācyam | ātvapakṣe aṣṭāsu ityatra bhavadrītyā kṛtātvasya
ṣaṭsaṃjñā'bhāvena upottamasvarāpravṛttyā tatra "aṣṭana' iti sūtrasya cāritārthyona tasyāpavādatvāsambhavāt | tataśca paratvāt aṣṭasu ityatra upottamasvara eva bhaviṣyatīti vibhaktisvarāprasaktyā tadvāraṇārthaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ dīrdhagrahaṇaṃ vyarthameva| kṛtaṃ ca tat kṛtātvasyāpyaṣṭanśabdasya ṣaṭsaṃjñāṃ jñāpayati | tataśca kṛtātvasthale'pi "jhalyupottamam' iti ṣaṭsvare prāpta eva "aṣṭano dīrghāt' iti sūtrārambhāt yena nāprāptinyāyena aṣṭana iti sūtramevāpavādatvātpravartate tathā aṣṭasu ityatrādīrghapakṣe'pi apavādatvāt vibhakteḥ svaraḥ syāditi tadvāraṇārthaṃ dīrghagrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiti svāṃśe caritārthatā | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu aṣṭānāmityatra kṛtātvasyāpi ṣaṭsaṃjñāstīti "ṣaṭcaturbhyaśca' (sū 8-1-55) iti nuṭsiddhiriti tātparyārthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa sarvādīni (sū. 1-1-28) || sarvanāmasaṃjñāyām ata eva nipātannna ṇatvam| purāṇapurātanau satatasaṃtatau ca pṛṣodarāditvātsiddhau | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhasarvādīni sarvanāmāni' iti sūtre sarvanāmaśabde "pūrvapadātsaṃjñāyāmagaḥ' (sū.8-403) iti ṇatvaṃ prāpnotītyāśaṅkya, nipātanāṇṇatvābhāva iti samādhāya, tadupari nipātanādaṇatvaṃ bhavatu yathāpraptaṃ ca ṇatvamapyastu, nipātanasya hi tadrūpasādhutvapratipādane tātparyam, na tu rūpāntaranivṛttāvityākṣipya, nāprāpte ṇatve nipātanamārabyate tadbādhakaṃ bhaviṣyati iti samāhiṃta bhāṣye | tadāha-sarvanāmasaṃjñāyāmiti | tanu sam-tata iti sthite makārasya lopena satataśabdaḥ "aparasparāḥ kriyāsātatye' (sū.6-1-144) iti nipātanāt sādhuritīṣyate | nāprāpte alope ārabhyamāṇamidaṃ lopanipātanaṃ alopasya bādhakaṃ bhavet | tataśca saṃtatamiti rūpaṃ na sidhyet | tathā "purāṇaprokteṣu' (sū.4-3-104) ityatra nipātitena purāṇaśabdena purātanaśabdasya bādhaḥ prāpnoti | tataśca purātanaśabdasya sādhutvaṃ na sidhyediti āśaṅkyāha-purāṇapurātanāviti | evaṃ ca saṃtatasatatayoḥ purāṇapurātanayośca pṛṣodarādigaṇe pāṭhādeva sādhutvam, na tu nipātanāt | ato noktadoṣāvasara iti bhāvaḥ | paribhāṣenduśekhare "bādhakānyeva nipātanāni' iti paribhāṣā paṭhyate | jhrjñā.ṭa pūrvaparā (sū.1-1-34) || pūrvaṃ gaṇapāṭhaḥ paścātsūtrapāṭhaḥ | "pūrvādibhyo vā' (sū.8-1-16) iti navagrahaṇāt jñāpakāt | sūtrapāṭhe navaiva hi pūrvādīni | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhapūrvaparāvaradakṣiṇottarāparadharāṇi vyavasthāyāmasaṃjñāyām ' iti sūtre kimarthaṃ pratipadaṃ pūrvādīni jasi vibhāṣārthaṃ paṭhitāni, yāvatā gaṇa evarthaviśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭāni nityaṃ sarvanāma - saṃjñārthaṃ paṭhyante | tatra pūrvādīni navetyevaṃ sūtraṃ paṭhitavyam | prāptavabhiāṣā ceyaṃ bhaviṣyati ityāśaṅkya, bhavedevaṃ yadi gaṇapāṭhaḥ pūrvaḥ syāt paścācca pāṇinisūtrapāṭhaḥ syāt | na tvetadasti, pramāṇābhāvāditi samādhāya, pāṇinisūtrāpekṣayā gaṇapāṭhasya prākkālikatvaṃ jñāpakena samarthitaṃ bhāṣye--- "evaṃ tarhyācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati sa pūrvaḥ pāṭhaḥ ayaṃ punaḥ pāṭha iti | yadayaṃ pūrvādibhyo navabhyo veti navagrahaṇaṃ karoti | navaiva hi pūrvādīni ' iti| tadāha-pūrvaṃ gaṇapāṭha iti | pūrvādibhyo navabhyo veti sūtre navagrahaṇaṃ navādhikatyadādinirāsārthaṃ kriyate | yadi ca niyatasaṃniveśo gaṇapāṭhaḥ pūrvaḥ tadā pūrvādibhya ityanena tyadādīnāmapi grahaṇaṃ syāditi tadvyavacchedāya navagrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ bhavati | yadā tu pāṇinisūtraṃ gaṇapāṭhātpūrvaṃ tadā pāṇinisūtre pūrvādīnāṃ navānāmeva pūrvaparetyādibhiḥ tribhiḥ sūtrairviśiṣya nirdeśāt navādhikānāṃ tyadādīnāṃ pūrvādibhya ityanena grahaṇaprasaktyabhāvāt nabagrahamaṃ vyarthaṃ syāditi gaṇapāṭhasya prācīnatvamanumīyata iti bhāvaḥ| sūtrapāṭha iti | pāṇinisūtrapāṭha ityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhapūrvatrāsiddham' (sū.8-2-1) ityāderjñāpakāt pūrvādīnāṃ dvyādiparyudāsena paryudāso na | jhrvi.ṭa
tatraiva sūtre pūrvādīni navetyevaṃ lāghavena paṭhitavye kimiti pūrvaparāvaretyādinā viśiṣya trisūtrīpāṭhaḥ kriyata ityāśaṅkāyāḥ samādhānamuktam--- "idaṃ tarhi prayojanam--dvyādiparyudāsena paryudāso mā bhūditi' iti bhāṣye | tasyāśayamevaṃ kaiyaṭa āha--tyadādīni paṭhitvā gaṇe kaiścit pūrvādīni paṭhitāni | tatra "kiṃsarvanāmabakahubhyo'dvyādibhyaḥ' (sū.5-3-2) iti pūrvāṃdibhyastasilādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti | tatra pūnaḥ sarvanāmasaṃjñayā tasilādirvidhīyate | tena dhdyādiparyudāso bādhyata iti | tadanantaraṃ samādhānamidaṃ dūṣayannāha bhāṣyakāraḥ--- "etadapi nāsti prayojanam | ācāryapravṛtirjñāpayati naiṣāṃ dvyādiparyu- dāsena paryudāso bhavatīti | yadayaṃ pūrvatrāsiddhamiti nipātanaṃ karoti' iti | yadi ca dvyādiparyuṃdāsena paryudāsāt pūrvādibhyastasilādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ syāt tadā "pūrvatra' iti tralantatayā pūrvaśabdanirdeśo'pramāṇaṃ syāt | nirdeśamahimnā pūrvādīnāṃ dvyādiparyudāsena paryudāso neti jñāpyata ityarthaḥ | tadetatsarvaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--pūrvatrāsiddhamityāderjñāpakāditi | ādipadena ca--"jaśbhāvāditi ceduttaratra ḍhasyābhāvādapavādaprasaṅgaḥ' iti vārtikasthaṃ uttaratra iti nipātanaṃ parāmṛśyate | jhrjñā.ṭa "kṛnmejantaḥ " (sū.1-1-39) kṛdantaṃ yanmāntamiti pakṣe praśānśabdasya svarādiṣu pāṭhāt jñāpakānna pratyayalakṣaṇenāvyayasaṃjñā | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhakṛnmejanta' iti sūtrasya dvedhā vyākhyānaṃ kṛtam-kṛdyo mānta ejantaśca tadantamavyayaṃ syādityekaṃ vyākhyānam | kṛdantaṃ yanmāntaṃ tadavyayasaṃjñakaṃ syāditi dvitīyaṃ vyākhyānam | tatra dvitīyavyākhyāne prapūrvakatamudhātoḥ kvipi, tasmin lupte'pipratyayalakṣaṇena pratām ityasya kṛdantatvaṃ śrutyā makārāntatvaṃ ca vartata ityavyayasaṃjñā syādityāśaṅkya bhāṣyakṛtā uktam---- "ācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati na pratyayalakṣaṇenāvyayasaṃjña bhavatīti | yadayaṃ praśānśabdaṃ svarādiṣu paṭhati' iti | yadi pratyayalakṣaṇena kṛdantatvamāśrityāvyayasaṃjñā bhavet tarhi praśān śabde'pi natvasyāsiddhatvāt māntatvaṃ, pratyayalakṣaṇena kṛdantatvaṃ cāstīti "kṛnmejanta' iti sūtreṇaivāvyayatve siddhe avyayasaṃjñārthaṃ svarādigaṇe praśānśabdapāṭho na kartavyaḥ syāt | kṛtaśca sa na pratyayalakṣaṇenāvyayasaṃjñā bhavatīti jñāpayatītyarthaḥ | tadetadāha--kṛdantamityādinā | jhrjñā.ṭa tisṛcatastrorṅīṃbabhāve "na tisṛ' (sū.6-4-4) iti nāmi dīrghapratiṣedho jñāpakaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhakṛnmejantaḥ' (sū.1-1-39) iti sūtre bhāṣye saṃnipātaparibhāṣāṃ paṭhitvā tasyā bahūni prayoṃjanāni parigaṇitāni | tanmadhye "tisṛcatasṛ ṅībvidhe.' ityapi ekaṃ prayojanam | tricaturśabdayoḥ jaśśasoḥ parataḥ striyāṃ tisṛcatastrādeśayoḥ kṛtayoḥ ṛkārāntatvāt "ṛnnebhyo ṅīp' (sū.4-1-5) iti ṅīp prāpnoti | sa ca saṃnipātaparibhāṣayā na bhavati | vibhaktisaṃnipātaṃ nimittīkṛtya jātau "tisṛcatasṛ' ādeśau tadvighātakasya ṅīpo nimitte na bhavataḥ | ṅīpi sati tena vyavadhānāt vibhaktisaṃnipātasya hāneḥ iti tadāśayaḥ | etacca prayojanaṃ jñāpakamahimnā ṅīvabhāvasādhanapūrvakaṃ khaṇḍaḍḍatdh;yāmāsa bhāṣyakāraḥ--- "etadapi nāsti prayojanam | ācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati na tisṛcatasṛbhāve kṛte hīp bhavatīti | dīrghaniṣedhaḥ tayorṅīp na bhavatīti jñāpayati | tataśca jaśśasorapi jñāpakenaiva ṅīpniṣedhe siddhe tistraḥ catastraḥ ityatra ṅībabhāvaḥ na saṃnipātaparibhāṣāprayojanamiti bhāvaḥ | tadetadāha--tisṛcatastrorṅībabhāva ityādinā | nanu saṃnipātaparibhāṣāsvīkāre yā sā ityādau ṭāp na syāt | yattacchabdābhyāṃ sau parataḥ "tyadādīnāmaḥ' iti dakārasyātve pararūpe ca adantatvāt
"ajādyataṣṭāp' iti ṭāp vaktavyaḥ | sa ca na saṃbhavati | vibhaktisaṃnipātanimittakasya atvasya tadvighātakaṭāpaṃ prati nimittatvāsaṃbhavādityāśaṅkyāha-- jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhana yāsayoḥ' (sū.7-3-45) | jhrvi.ṭa suḍaḍḍatdh;napuṃsakasyeti sutre bhāṣye--- "asamarthasamāsaścāyaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ anapuṃsakasyeti | na hi natro naṃpusakena sārmathyam | kena tarhi | bhavatinā | na bhavati napuṃsakasyeti' ityuktam | anenāsamarthasamāsaṃ svīkṛtya prasajyapratiṣeghaparatayā sūtraṃ vyākhyātam | tatra yadi a ityavyayaṃ kiṃcit pratiṣedhārthakaṃ bhavet tadā a napuṃsakasyeti pṛthakpadatvaṃ svīkṛtya napuṃsakasya na bhavatīti prasajyapratiṣedhasya asamarthasamāstvaṃ vināpi lābhāt asamarthasamāsatvakalpanā kliṣṭā nāśrayaṇīyā syāt | āśritā ca sā pratiṣedhārthakam a ityavyayaṃ nāstīti sūcayati | ut' iti śāstraṃ nimittaṃ yasya ukārasya tasmātparasya visargasya na ṣatvaṃ bhavatīti mātuḥ karotītyatra na ṣatvāpattiḥ | bhrātuṣputraśabde tu kaskādigaṇe pāṭhāt ṣatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | sūtravihitāpavādarūpakapratyaye'pi sthānivadbhāvena aṇi sati yatkāryaṃ tatprāpnotītyāśaṅkya, apavāde utsargaprayuktaṃ kāryaṃ na bhavati | tathā ca kapratyaye'pavāde utsargabhūtāṇpratyayaprayuktakāryaṃ na bhavatīti na doṣaḥ | tatra jñāpakaṃ śapaḥ apavādabhūteṣu śyannādiṣu śitkaraṇam | yadyapavāde sthānivadbhavena utsargakṛtakāryaṃ syāt tadā yan ityasyāpi sthānivadbhāvena śapi (sū.1-1-49) iti sūtrāt ṣaṣṭhī ityanurvatya tacca tṛtīyayā vipariṇamayya nirdaṣṭisyetyadhyāhṛtya ṣaṣṭhyā nirdiṣṭasya sthāne ya ādeśaḥ sa sthānivat iti vyākhayeyam | kapratyayaśca na jñāpakam | tathā hi- "āhasthaḥ' iti sūtraṃ hi jhalādau pare āhaḥ thakāradeśaṃ vidhatte | āttha ityudāharaṇam | yadi cātra sthānivadbhāvena brūtvamhaḥ āśritya tataḥ parasya thalaḥ īṭ syāt tadā thalaḥ īkārādeḥ jhalāditvaṃ nāstīti tasmin pare thatvaṃ kathaṃ bhavet | ataḥ jhali pare vidhīyamānaṃ thatvaṃ nāherīṭ bhavatīti jñāpayati | nanu "āhasthaḥ' iti sūtreṇa bhūtapūrvo yo jhalādistasmin pare thakāro vidhīyate | thala īḍaḍḍatdh;āgame sati adhunā jhalāditvābhāve'pa pūrvaṃ jhalāditvamastīti tadādāya thatvaṃ bhaviṣyatīti vairyathyābhāvātkathamasya jñāpakatvamiti cet--yadi bhūtapūrvagatirāśrīyeta tadā ṇaladīnāmapi sthānyavasthāmādāya bhūtapūrvajhalāditvamastīti pañcasvapi parataḥ āhasthakāro bhavatīti labhyeta | evaṃ cet āhādeśa ityanuktvā "brūvaḥ pañcānāmādita ātho bruvaḥ' (sū.3-4-84) iti āthādeśameva vidadhyāt āhastha iti pṛthak sūtraṃ ca na kuryāt | yataḥ pṛthaksūtraṃ kṛtaṃ tena jñāyate bhūtapūrvagatiratra nāśrīyata iti | tataśca pañcānāṃ madhye thal eka eva jhalādiḥ | tasya cedīṭ bhavet jhalāditvaṃ tasya bhagnamiti jhalādau parato vidhīyamānaṃ thatvamanavakāśaṃ syāt | ato jñāpayati nāherīḍḍḍatdh; | tadetatsarvaṃ manasikṛtyāha--āthādeśe kartavya iti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhagotaḥ' (sū.8-1-90) "otaḥ' (6-1-93) iti ca taparakaraṇasārmathyāt citraguścatragumityādau na ṇicvātve | jhrvi.ṭa sthānivatsūtropari doṣaparigaṇanasandarbhe gośabde ṇittvamātvaṃ ca prāpnotītyāśaṅkya taparakaraṇasārmathyānna ṇittvātve ityākare uktam | tadāha--gota iti | ayamāśayaḥ--yadyādeśaḥ sthānivatsyāt tadā citragurityatra citrā gāvo yasyeti bahuvrīhau "gostriyorupasarjanasya' (sū.1-2-48) iti okārasya hrasve ukāre sati tasya sthānidbhāvena okāratvāt gośabdātparatvaṃ su iti sarvanāmasthānasya vartata iti tasya "goto ṇiṃt' (sū.7-1-90) iti ṇittve tasmin parataḥ "aco ñṇiti' (sū.7-1-15) iti ukārasya vṛddhiḥ prāpnoti | evaṃ catrigumityatra ukārasya sthānivadbhāvena okāratvāt tataḥ amaḥ aci pare pūrvaparayoḥ ākāradeśaḥ "auto'mśasoḥ' (sū 6-1-93) iti sūtreṇa prāpnoti ityāśaṅkā | samādhānaṃ tu - sūtradvaye'pi ota iti taparakaraṇāt alvidhitvāt analvidhāviti niṣeghācca sthāniṃvadbhāvo neti | taparakaraṇasya phalaṃ hi gu ityatrokāravyāvṛttiḥ | yadi tatrāpi sthānivadbhāvena sūtraṃ pravarteta tarhi taparakaraṇamanarthakaṃ syāditi | jhrjhā.ṭa ṭhadvirvacane'ci' (sū.1-1-59) || ajgrahaṇaṃ tu jñāpakaṃ rūpasthānivadbhāvasya | jeghrīyata iti hi tatpratyudāharaṇam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "dvirvacane'ci' (sū.1-1-59) iti sūtrasya dvitvanimitte'ci pare'jādeśaḥ sthānivadityarthaḥ | tatra ca kāryātideśa iti pakṣe cakratuḥ cakruḥ ityādau ābyāsarūpaṃ na sidhyati | kṛ atuḥ ityatra ṛkārasya yaṇi rephe k r atuḥ iti sthite ekāco vidhīyamānaṃdvitvamanackasya aprāptaṃ sthānivadbhāvena prapyamāṇamapi kāryātideśāt yaṇyuktasyaiva prāpnoti | tataśca k r k r atuḥ iti sthite halādiśeṣādau ckratuḥ ityeva rūpaṃ sidhyet | akārayuktābhyāsaviśiṣṭaṃ cakraturiti rūpaṃ na sidhyet ityāśaṅkyāha-ajgrahaṇaṃ tu iti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ--"dvirvacane'ci' iti sūtreṇa ajādeśaḥ sthānivadityatideśaḥ sthānini sati yatkāryaṃ bhavati tadādeśe'pi bhavatītyevaṃrūpaṃḥ kāryātideśa iti na mantavyam | api tu sthānini sati yadrūpaṃ tadādeśe'pi bhavatītyevaṃrūpaḥ rūpātideśa iti | tathā ca kṛ atuḥ ityatra yaṇādeśe kṛte tasya sthānirūpātideśāt kṛ iti ṛkāraviśiṣṭasya dvitve cakraturiti rūpaṃ sidhyatīti na doṣaḥ | rūpātideśe jñāpakaṃ tu aci iti ajgrahaṇam | tasya hi prayojanaṃ jeghrīyata ityādau sthānivattvābhāvaḥ
| ghradhātoryaṅi "ī ghrādhmoḥ' (sū.7-4-31) iti sūtreṇa dhātorākārasya īkāre ghrī ityasya dvitve halādiśeṣe cutve jaśtve "guṇo yaṅlukoḥ' (sū.8-4-72) iti abhyāsasya guṇe ca jeghrīyata iti rūpam | tatra yaṅaḥ dvitvanimittatvāt tasmin pare ākārādeśasya īkārasya sthānivadbhāvena rūpātideśe ghrā ityasya dvitve jaghrāyata ityeva rūpaṃ syāt iti ajgrahaṇaṃ kṛtam | kṛte ca tasmin dvitvanimitte'ci parata eva ajādeśasya sthānivadbhāvaḥ | yaṅastu dvitvanimittasyāpyactvaṃ nāstīti tasmin parata īkārasya sthānivattvena ākārarūpātideśo nāstīti ghrī ityasya dvitve jeghrīyata itīṣṭarūpasiddhiḥ| tatra yadi kāryātideśapakṣaḥ tadā ghrī ityasya dvitvarūpaṃ kāryamevātidiśyate iti ākārarūpātideśābhāvāt sthānivattve'pi na doṣa iti kimajgrahaṇena | kṛtaṃ cājgrahaṇaṃ dvirvacane'cīti sūtrai kriyamāṇaḥ sthānivattvātideśaḥ rūpātideśa iti jñāpayatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhaoḥ pūyaṇji' vacanaṃ jñāpakaṃ ṇau sthānivadbhāvasya | jhrvi.ṭa nanu avannāvayiṣati avacukṣāvayiṣatītyādau nukṣudhātvorṇijantayoḥ sun bhavati | tatra ca ṇici parato na ityatrokārasya "aco ñṇiti' (sū 7-2-115) iti vṛddhau āvādeśe ca nāv i sa iti sthite āvādeśasya sthānivadbhāvena aukārarūpātiṃdeśe nauśabdasya dvitve nau nau isa ityatra ābhyasahrasve āvādeśe ca nunāvayiṣati iti rūpam | tatra sthānivadbhāvo durlabhaḥ | "dvirvacane'ci' ityanena dvitvanimitte'ci parata eva ajādeśasya sthānivadbhāva ucyate | atra ca ṇicaḥ (sū 7-4-80) iti sūtraṃ jñāpakam | tasya sūtrasya hi sani pare yadaṅgaṃ tadavayavābhyāsokārasyetvaṃ syāt avarṇaparakeṣa pavargayaṇjakāreṣu parata ityarthaḥ | yiyaviṣati ityudāharaṇam | tatra puyaṇji ukārabhāvādeva ikārādeśāpravṛtteḥ kiṃ puyaṇjigrahaṇena | kṛtaṃ ca tat ṇau sthānivadbhāvo bhavatīti jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhapratyayasya luk' (sū.1-1-61)|| pratyayasyetyetatsārmathyāt lope khaṇḍaḍḍatdh;yan āha bhāṣyakāraḥ--- "etadapi nāsti prayojanam | ācāryapravṛttirjñapayatiṃ lukślulupaḥ sarvādeśā bhavantīti | yadayaṃ "lugvā duhadihalihaguhāmātmanepade dantye' iti lope prakṛte lukaṃ śāsti' iti | "lugvā duha' (sū.7-3-73) iti sūtreṇa duhādīnāṃ ksasya dantye taṅi pare lugvā vidhīyate | aduggha adhukṣatetyādikamudāharaṇam | tatra sūtre "ghorlopo leṭi vā' (sū.7-3-70) iti sūtrallopa ityanuvṛttyaiva siddhe luggrahaṇam luggrahaṇena vidhīyamānamadarśanaṃ sarvādeśa iti jñāpayati | lopasya tu antyādeśatvam | lugapi yadāntyādeśaḥ syāt tadā lopapadānuvṛttyaiveṣṭasiddheḥ kiṃ luggrahaṇena| kṛtaṃ tu luggrahaṇaṃ sarvādeśatvasya jñāpakamiti bhāvaḥ | tadetadāha--pratyayasyetyetatsārmathyādityādinā | idaṃ vārtikamatena | bhāṣyamatena tvāha--"lugvā duha' iti jñāpakācceti | bhavatītyucyate | tatra pratyayasya luki sati pratyayanimittakaṃ kāryaṃ na prāpnoti, luko lopatvābāvāt | tathā ca pañca sapta ityādau "ṣaḍḍḍatdh;bhyo luk' (sū.7-1-22) iti sūtreṇa jaśśasorluki pañcan lumatāṅgasyeti niṣedhāditi | lumatā luk ityādiśabdena lupte pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ aṅgakāryaṃ neti niṣedhaḥ pratyayalakṣaṇaprāptāveva yujyate | aprāptaniṣedhāyogāt | lukśabdena lupte pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ ca
