prakalpayati, "tasmādityuttarasya' (sū.1-1-67) iti paribhāṣā parasya ṣaṣṭhīṃ prakalpayatīti svīkāre yatra vidhisūstre saptamī pañcamī cetyubhayoḥ śravaṇaṃ tatra pūrvasya parasya ca ṣaṣṭhīprakalpanaṃ syāt | tataśca tatrobhayoḥ kāryaṃ prāpnoti | yathā "tāsyanudāttenṅidadupadeśāllasārvadhātuke'nudāttam' (sū.6-1-186) iti sūtre tāsyādibhya iti pañcamī lasārvadātuke iti saptamyāḥ ṣaṣṭhīṃ prakalpayet | tathā lasarvadhātuka ityeṣā saptamī tāsyādibya iti pañcamyāḥ ṣaṣṭhīṃ prarakalpayet | tathā lasarvadātuka ityeṣā saptamī tāsyādibhya iti pañcamyāḥ ṣaṣṭhīṃ prakalpayet | tatrobhayoḥ kāryamanudāttatvaṃ prāpnoti ityāśaṅkyāha--anayoḥ sūtrayoriti | yadyubhayanirdeśāt parasparaprakalpane sati tāsyādibhyo lasārvadhātukāsya lasārvadhātuke tāsyādīnāmanudāttatvaṃ bhavatīti vākyārthaḥ syāt tadā "ādraguṇaḥ' ityādāvapyacītyanuvṛtteḥ ubhayapraklṛptimāśritya aci avarṇasya, avarṇātparasyācaśca guṇa iti vyākhyānena pūrvaparayorguṇasiddhyā "ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ' (sū.6-1-75) iti vyarthaṃ syāt | yataśca tatsūtramārabdham ataḥ pañcamīsaptamyorubhayoḥ prakalpanaṃ nāśrīyate | ubhayata āśrayaṇe pañcamīnirdaiśasya balīyastvāt pañcamyā eva ṣaṣṭhīprakalpakatvāt parasya guṇaḥ syāditi pūrvaparayorguṇasiddhyarthaṃ "ekaḥ pūrvaparayo' riti sūtram | tataśca tāsyanudāttet ityādāvapi pañcamī saptamyāḥ ṣaṣṭhīṃ prakalpayatīti tāsyādibhyaḥ parasya lasārvadhātukasyaivānudāttatvaṃ na pūrvaparayordvayoriti na doṣa ityāśayaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa yena vidhiḥ (sū.1-1-72)|| "nedamadasorakoḥ' (sū.7-1-11) ityatrākorityuktyā tadekadeśabhūtaṃ tadragrahaṇena gṛhyata iti jñāpyate | jhrvidṛṭa nanu yena vidhiriti sutre viśeṣaṇaṃ tasya tadantasya ca saṃjñā syāditi vyākhyāne'pi akajvataḥ sarvaka ityasmāt parasya jasaḥ śī na prāpnoti | tathā hi sarva jas iti sthite akaci sarvaka jas ityatra sarvaka ityasya sarvaśabdatvābhāvāt sarvaśabdāntatvābhāvācca sarvanāmnaḥ prātipadikāt ityanena vā sarvanāmāntāt prātipadikāt ityanena vā ubhayathāpi grahaṇābhāvāt "jasaḥ śī' (sū.7-1-17) iti sūtrāpravṛtteḥ | evaṃ bhinatti ityatra bhid ityasyaiva dhātutvena śnamvataḥ bhinad ityasya śabdānatarasya dhātutvābhāvāt "dhātoḥ' (sū.6-1-162) iti vidhīyamāno'ntodāntasvaro na syāt ityāśaṅkyāha--nedamadasorakorityādinā | "nedamadasorakoḥ' (sū.7-1-11) iti sūtraṃ kakārarahitayoridamadasoḥ parasya bhisaḥ es na syāditiṃ pratipādayati | tatra kakārarahitayorityanena imaka amuka iti akajviśiṣṭayoḥ
idamadasorvyāvṛttiriṣyate | tatra idamadasorviṣaye ucyamānaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ tadibhannayoḥ akajviśiṣṭayoḥ kathaṃ prāpnuyāt | ataḥ akoḥ ityanuktāvapi śabdabhedādeva vyāvṛttisiddheḥ akoriti vyarthaṃ sat tadekadeśabhūtaṃ tadgrahaṇena gṛhyata iti jñāpayati | tathā ca idamadas śabdamadhyapātī akac tadavayavatvāt idamadsagrahaṇena gṛhyeteti tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ akoriti sārthaṃkam | evaṃ sarvakaśabdasyāpi sarvaśabdena grahaṇāt sarvanāmatvamastīti tataḥ parasya jasaḥ śībhāve sarvake iti rūpaṃ sidhyati | evaṃ bhinad ityatra bhid ityasya avayavatvenāgataḥ śnam bhidragrahaṇena gṛhyata iti bhinad ityasyāpi dhātutvāt "dhātoḥ' iti vihito'ntodātto'pi sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | idameva "tanmadhyapatitastadgrahaṇena gṛhyate' ityapi paṭhanti | aktaparimāṇānāmiti | paricchinnaparimāṇānāmityarthaḥ | tattvamiti | dhātvāditvamityarthaḥ | jhrjña.ṭa ṭhapūrvādiniḥ' (sū.5-2-86) "sapūrvācca' (5-2-87) iti yogavibhāgo vyapadeśivadbhāvo'prātipadikenetyasyāḥ jñāpakaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "yena vidhistadantasya' (sū. 1-1-72) iti sūtre viśeṣaṇaṃ tadantasya saṃjñā syādityetāvanmānnoktau "roṇī' (sū.4-2-78) iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamāno'ṇ roṇīśabdāntāt ajakaroṇī ityata eva bhavet na tu kevalaroṇīśabdāt | tataśca rauma iti na sidhyet ityālocya vaśieṣaṇaṃ tadantasya tasya ca saṃjñā syāditi ākare vyākhyātam | tatra tasya cetyaṃśo'nupādeyaḥ | tadabhāve'pi vyapadeśivadbhāvena roṇīśabdasya roṇīśabdāntatvamādāyāṇsiddherityāśaṅkāṃ manasikṛtyāha--pūrvādinirityādinā | tathā ca prātipadikaviṣaye vyapadeśivadbhāvavirahāt roṇīśabdasya roṇīśabdāntatvābhāvāt tasya ca saṃjñā syādityavaśyaṃ vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ | vyapadeśivadbhāvo'prātipadikena ityasyāṃ paribhāṣāyāṃ jñapakaṃ tu pūrvādiniḥ iti yogavibhāgaḥ | "pūrvātsapūrvādiniḥ' ityekasūtraṃ kṛtvā kṛtapūrva ityādeḥ kṛtādiśabdapūrvakatvāt kevalapūrvaśabdasya vyapadeśivadbhāvena sapūrvakatvācca pūrvī kṛtapūrvī ityubhayatrāpi inipratyayasya sādhayituṃ śakyatvāt tathā akṛtvā pūrvādiniḥ sapūrvācceti yogavibhāgakaraṇamahimnā prātipadikaviṣaye vyapadeśivadbhāvo nāstīti jñāpyata iti bhāvaḥ | iti prathamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhatasyāditaḥ' (sū.1-2-32)|| svaritasyetyādi devabrahmaṇoranudāttavacanaṃ jñāpakam, idaṃ kāṇḍaḍḍatdh;m, idaṃ kāṇḍaḍḍatdh;m "udāttādanudāttasya svarita' (sū 8-4-66) ityataḥ paramiti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhatasyādita udāttamardhahrasvam' (sū.1-2-32) iti sūtre bhāṣye svaritasya ardhamātrātmakaḥ ādyavayavaḥ udāttasaṃjñaka iti sūtrārthaṃ pratipādya, "tasyādita udāttam' (sū.1-2-32) iti sūtramārabya "udāttasvaritaparasya sannataraḥ' (sū.1-2-40) iti sūtraparyantā navasūtrī "udāttādanudāttasya svaritaḥ' (sū.8-4-66) iti sūtrādūrdhvaṃ paṭhanīyā hatyuktvā prayojanamuktam "svaritāt siddhiryathā syāt "svaritātsaṃhitāyāmanudāttānām' (sū. 1-2-39) iti| tasyāyamarthaḥ- imaṃ meṃ gaṅge yamune sarasvati sutudri' ityatra svaritāt meśabdātpareṣāṃ gaṅgeprabhṛtīnāmāmantritatvādanudāttānāṃ "svaritātsaṃhitāyāmanudāttānām' iti sūtreṇa ekaśrutirbhavati | meśabdasya svaritastu "udāttādanudāttasya svaritaḥ' (sū.1-2-39) iti sūtreṇa ekaśrutau kartavyāyāṃ "udāttādanudāttasya svaritaḥ' (sū.8-4-66) ita sūtreṇa vihitaḥ meśabdasya svaritaḥ asiddha iti svaritātparatvaṃ gaṅgeprabhṛtīnāṃ nāstīti ekaśrutirna sidhyet yathāśrutāṣṭādhyāyīpāṭhe | pūrvoktanavasūñyāḥ "udāttādanudāttasya' (8-4-66) ityanantaraṃ pāṭha e tu ekaśrutividhāyakasya "svaritātsaṃhitāyām' (sū.1-2-39) iti sūtrasyāpi navasūñyantarbhūtasya anantaratvāt tataḥ pūrvabhasya
"udāttādanudāttasya' iti sūtrasya nāsiddhatvamiti meśabdasya svaritatvasiddhyā tataḥ pareṣāmanudāttānāṃ gaṅgeprabhṛtīnāmekaśrutirnirābādheti| na ca svaritādityāśritya anudāttānāmekaśrutividhānāt svaritasyāsiddhatve svaritādityāśrayāyogāt svaritasya siddhatvaṃ jñāyata iti vyartho'yaṃ pariśrama iti vācyam | yatra svaritasyāsiddhatvaprasaktirnāsti tatra svaritādityāśritya tataḥ pareṣāmanudāttānāmekaśrutividhāyakatayā sūtrasārthakyāt | yathā "kāryaṃ devadattayajñadattau' ityatra kāryaśabdaḥ ṇyadantaḥ "titsvaritam' (6-1-185) iti sūtreṇa svaritāntaḥ | tataḥ parayoranudāttayordevadattayajñadattayoḥ ekaśrutirbhavati | atra "titsvaritam' ityasya tripādīsthatvābhāvenāsiddhatvaprasaktireva nāstīti atra svaritamāśritya tataḥ parasyānudāttarayaikaśrutividhāyakatayā sūtrasārthakyam | tataśca imaṃ me gaṅge ityatra pūrvektānupapattiparihārarthaṃ pūrvoktanavasūñyāḥ "udāttādanudāttasya' (sū.8-4-66) ityasmādanantaraṃ pāṭhaḥ kartavya iti bhāṣyābhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ navasūñyāḥ aṣṭamādhyāye pāṭhamuktvā tatra jñāpakamapi tatraiva bhāṣye-"devabrahmaṇoranudāttavacanaṃ jñāpakaṃ parametatsūtrakāṇḍaḍḍatdh;m iti' iti granthena uktam | "devabrahmaṇoranudāttaḥ' (sū.1-2-38) iti sūtreṇa devabrahmaśabdayoḥ subrahmaṇyānigadāntargatayoḥ svaritasyānudātto vidhīyate | "devā brahmāṇa āgacchata' hatyudāharaṇam | devāḥ brahmāṇaḥ ityanayoḥ "āmantritasya ca' (sū.6-1-198) iti sūtrema ādirudātto bhavati | tataḥ śeṣanighāte tasya "udāttādanudāttasya svaritaḥ' (sū.8-2-38) iti sūtreṇānudātto vidhīyate | tatra "devabrahmaṇoḥ' (sū.1-2-38) ityanudātte kartavye "udāttādanudāttasya'(8-4-66) iti svaritasyāsiddhatvāt anudātta eva vartata iti tasya punaranudāttavidhānamanarthakaṃ sat navasūtrātmakaṃ kāṇḍaḍḍatdh;m "udāttādanudāttasya svaritaḥ' ityataḥ paramiti jñāpayati| tadrītyā ca navasūtrātmakakāṇḍaḍḍatdh;āntargatena "devabrahmaṇoḥ' iti sūtreṇa pareṇa pūrvasya "udāttādanudāttasya' (sū.8-4-66) iti svaritasyāsiddhatvaṃ na bhavatīti tasyānudāttavacanaṃ sārthakamiti jñāpakaparabhāṣyāśyaḥ | tadetatsarvaṃ manasikṛtyāha--svaritasyetyādīti | svaritasyetyanuvartamāne devabrahmaṇoḥ anudāttavidhāyakaṃ "devabrahmaṇoranudāttaḥ' iti sūtraṃ pūrvoktārthe jñāpakamityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa apṛkta ekāl (sū.1-2-41) || idamapyekagrahaṇaṃ varṇagrahaṇe jātigrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati | jhrvi.ṭa idamapīti | apinā "nipāta ekāc' (sū 1-1-14) iti sūtrasthamekagrahaṇaṃ samuccīyate | etacca pūrvameva vivṛtamiti neha vivriyate | jhrjñā.ṭa arthavadadhātuḥ (sū.1-2-45)|| apratyaya ityasya prasajyapratiṣedhatve "noṅdhātvoḥ' (sū.6-1-175) iti vibhaktisvarapratiṣedāt jñāpakāt ūṅantādeḥ prātipadikatvam | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaarthavadadāturapratyayaḥ prātipadikam' iti sūtre apratyaya ityasya pratyayo na ityevaṃ prasajyapratiṣedhaparatvam iti pakṣe pratyayāntaṃ pratyayaśca prātipadikasaṃjñake na bhavataḥ iti sūtrārthaḥ syāt | tataśca brahmabandhuśabdāt striyāṃ "ūṅutaḥ' (sū.4-1-66)iti sūtreṇa ūṅi brahmabandhu ū iti sthite "akaḥ savarṇe dīrvaḥ' (sū.6-1-101) iti savarṇadīrghe brahmabandhū iti sthite ekādeśasya ūkārasya paradivadbhāvena pratyayatvāt brahmabandhū ityasya pratyayāntatvāt prātipadikasaṃjñāniṣedhe tataḥ svādyutpattirna syādityāśaṅkya ūṅantasya prātipadikatvaṃ jñāpakena sādhayati--apratyaya ityasyetyādinā | ayamāśayaḥ--ūṅantasya paraṃ pratyayāntatve'pi prātipadikasaṃjñā bhavati | tatra jñāpakaṃ "noṅdātvoḥ' (sū.6-1-175) iti vibhaktisvarapratiṣedhaḥ | tena hi sūtreṇa ūṅaḥ
dhātośca ādeśabhūtādyaṇaḥ pare śasādaya udāttāḥ na syuriti pratipādyate | brahmabandhvā ityudāharaṇam | ūṅantasya prātipadikatvābhāve tataḥ pare śasādaya eva durlabhā iti śaśāderudāttatvaniṣedhaḥ ūṅantasya prātipadikatvajñāpanadvārā tataḥ vibhaktyutparattiṃ jñāpayatītyarthaḥ | bhāṣye tu "utpadyante ūṅantātsvādayaḥ' ityeva vacanaśarīraṃ dṛśyate | tasyāpyuktārtha eva tātparyam | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhana ṅisaṃbuddhyoḥ' (sū.8-2-8) iti pratiṣedhāt jñāpakāt pratyayalakṣaṇenāpratyaya iti niṣedho na | jhrvi.ṭa tatraiva sūtre prasajyapratiṣedhapakṣa eva rājan s iti sthite dīrghe halṅyādinā sulope ca rājān ityatra "nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya' (sū.8-2-7) iti nalope rājā iti rūpamiṣyate | tanna syāt | sulope satyapi tasya pratyayalakṣaṇena pratyayāntatvāt pratyayāntaṃ prātipadikaṃ neti prātipadikasaṃjñāniṣedhāt tatsaṃjñānimittakanalopāsaṃbhāvādityāśaṅkya, apratyaya iti niṣedhaviṣaye pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ na pravartate iti jñāpanānna doṣa iti samādhate-- na ṅisaṃbuddhyoriti pratiṣedhāt jñāpakāditi | "na ṅisaṃbuddhyoḥ' iti sūtreṇa hi ṅau saṃbuddhau ca nasya lopo na syādityucyate | parame vyomannati ṅau chandasyudāharaṇam | atra ṅerlopānantaraṃ pratyayalakṣaṇena pratyayāntatvamāśritya apratyaya iti niṣedhātprātipadikatvaṃ neti prātipadikanimittakanalopāpravṛttyā nalopaniṣedhakaṃ "na ṅisaṃbuddhyoḥ' iti sūtramanarthakam | kṛtaṃ ca tat - pratyayalakṣaṇamāśritya apratyaya iti niṣedho na pravartata iti jñāpayati | ato na doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhatiṣyapunarvasvoḥ' (sū.7-2-63) atra bahuvacanagrahaṇāt jñāpakāt sarvā dvandvo vibhāṣayaikavaditi jñāpyate | evaṃ ca tiṣyapunarvasviti tadvyārvatyam | athavā jātiraprāṇināmityekavattvam | nātrabhavantaḥ prāṇinaḥ prāṇā evātrabhavanta iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "tiṣyapunarvasvornakṣatradvandve bahuvacanasya dvivacanaṃ nityam' (sū.1-2-63) iti sūtre bahuvacanasyeti kimartham | na ca tadabhāve ekavacanasyāpi dvivacanaṃ syāt, tataśca tiṣyapunarvasu ityekavacanāntaṃ rūpaṃ na siddhyediti vācyam | tiṣyapunarvasvordvandve ekavacanasya durlabhatvāt tatsthāne dvivacanaprasaktyabhāvāt | na ca tiṣyaśca punarvasū ca eṣāṃ samāhāra iti samāhāradvandve ekavadbhāvāt ekavacanasaulabhyamiti vācyam | prāṇyaṅgadīnāmeva samāhāre dvandvavidhānāt | na ca "jātiraprāṇinām' iti sūtreṇātra samāhāradvandva ekavadbhāva iti vācyam | aprāṇināmiti pratiṣedhāt | tathā caikavacanāntasya tiṣyapunarvasuśabdasyābhāvāt ekavacanasya dvivacanaprasaktyabhāvāt "tiṣyapunarvasvoḥ' iti sūtre bahuvacanasyeti vyarthamityāśaṅkya, ata eva tat sarvo dvandvo vibhāṣā ekavaditi jñāpayati | tathā ca tiṣyaśca punarvasū cetītaretarayogadvandve tasya ekavadbhāvapakṣe tiṣyapunarvasu ityatra ekavacanasya dvivacanaṃ mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ bahuvacanasyetyuktam | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu bābhravāśca śālaṅkāyanāścetītaretarayogadvandve bābhravaśālaṅkāyanam iti ekavacanāntasya sādhutvam ityuktaṃ bhāṣye | tadetadāha--atra bahuvacanagrahaṇāt jñāpakāditi | tadvyārvatyamiti | bahuvacanasyetyasya vyavacchedyamityarthaḥ | vastutastu tiṣyapunarvasvoḥ loke prāṇitvena vyavahārābāvāt pratimanvantaraṃ nakṣatrabhedena tiṣyapunarvasvādhārā jātiḥ saṃbhavatīti ca hetoḥ aprāṇijātivācitvena jātiraprāṇināmityanena ekavadbhāve sati sasyaikavacanasya dvivacanaṃ mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ bahuvacanagrahaṇamityevocitamityāha--yathaveti | praṇā evātra bhavanta iti | tiṣyapunarvasvadhīnasthitikatvāt prāṇināṃ tiṣyapunarvasvoḥ kathaṃcitprāṇatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate na tu prāṇitvamiti bhāvaḥ | tataścāpraṇināmiti niṣedho na pravartata ityāśayaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa bhrātṛputrau svasṛ
(sū.1-2-68)|| "pumān striyā' (sū.1-2-67) ityeva siddhe idaṃ sūtraṃ "yatrordhvaṃ prakṛtestallakṣaṇo viśeṣastatra vṛddho yūnetyādyekaśeṣa ityarthasya jñāpakam | jhrvi.ṭa nanvekaśeṣaprakaraṇe bhrātuḥ svastrā saha, putrasya duhitrā saha coktau bhrātṛputrau śiṣyete ityarthakaṃ "bhrātṛputrau svasṛduhitṛbhyām' (sū.1-2-68) iti sūtram anarthakam | "pumān striyā' (sū.1-2-67) iti sutreṇaiva gatarthatvādityāśaṅkya jñāpanaparatayā sārthakyamāha - pumān striyetyeveti | yatra prakṛterūrdhvameva strīpuṃlakṣaṇaḥ viśeṣo'sti na tu prakṛtau tatraiva pumāṃstriyā iti sūtraṃ pravartate | yathā haṃsaśca haṃsī ca ityatra haṃsa iti prakṛtirekaivobhayatra | tataḥ parameva bhedo vartata iti tatra pumāstriyā ityekaśeṣe haṃsau iti bhavati | tena haṃsaśca varaṭā cetyatraikaśeṣo na, prakṛtāvapi bhedāt | bhrātā ca svasā cetyatrāpi prakṛtāveva bhedo'satīti "pumāṃstriyā' ityasyāpravṛttyā tatraikaśeṣavidhāyakatayā "bhrātṛputrau' iti sūtraṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | iti prathamādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa bhūvādayaḥ (sū.1-3-1)|| ādigrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati asti ca pāṭho bāhyaśca sūtrāditi | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "bhūvādayo dhātavaḥ' iti sūtre ādigrahaṇaṃ vyartham | tathā hi sūtrakāreṇa bhūprabhṛtīn dhātūn paṭhitvā te yadi bhūvādiśabdena parāmṛśyante tarhi "te dātavaḥ' ityeva sūtraṃ paṭhanīyam | yadi ca sūtrakārema būvādayaḥ apaṭhitiḥ tadāpi bhūvādayaḥ ityādigraheṇa te parāmarṣṭuṃ na śakyante ityāśaṅkyāha--ādigrahaṇamiti | sūtrādbahirbhūto dhātupāṭho vartate yatra sthitānāṃ dātūnāṃ ādiśabdena parāmarśaḥ sulabhaḥ iti jñāpanārtham ādipadaṃ prayuktavān sūtrakāra iti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca sūtreṣu ye paṭhyante mṛḍaḍḍatdh;mṛdādayaḥ tān samāpya "te dhātavaḥ' iti sutrakāraṇe tatrāpaṭhitānāṃ āṇavayati ityādīnāmapi dhātusaṃjñā syāt | ataḥ sūtravārtikabhāṣyagaṇapāṭheṣu dṛṣṭānāmeva dhātusaṃjñāsiddhyarthaṃ bhūvādaya ityuktamityavadheyam | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhana vibhaktau' (sū.1-3-4)|| ataddhita iti vaktavyamiti thamo masyetsaṃjñāparitrāṇārthanokāreṇānubandhena vibhaktau tadvite pratiṣedhābhāvajñāpanāt pratyākhyātam | dānīṃ tu yāntaḥ kariṣyate luptanirdiṣṭayakāreṇa | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "na vibhaktau tusmāḥ' iti sūtreṇa vibhaktisthānāṃ tavargasakāramakārāṇāmitsaṃjña niṣidhyate | tataśca kimśabdāt "kimo't' (sū.5-3-12) iti sūtreṇa at iti pratyate takārasya "halantyam' (sū.1-3-3) iti sūtreṇetsaṃjñāyāṃ lope ca "kvāti' (sū 72-105)iti sutreṇa kimaḥ kvādeśe ca "kka' iti rūpamiṣyate | tatra "at' pratyayasya taddhitasya "prāgdiśovibhaktiḥ' (sū. 5-3-1) iti sūtreṇa vibhaktisaṃjñakatvāt tatsthasya takārasya "na vibhaktau tusmāḥ' (sū.1-3-4) iti sūtreṇa itsaṃjñāniṣedhaḥ syādityāśaṅkya vārtikakṛtā"atadvita iti vaktavyam' ityuktam | taddhitabhinnavibhaktisthānāmeva tavargadīnāmitsaṃjñā niṣidhyata iti taddhitasthatakārasyetsaṃjñā bhavatyeveti tadāśayaḥ | idaṃ ca vārtikaṃ jñāpakena taddhitasthatavargādīnāṃ itsaṃjñāniṣedho na bhavatīti prasādhya pratyākhyātaṃ bhāṣyakṛtā | tadaha-ataddhita iti vaktavyamitīti | asya ca pratyākhyātamityanenānvayaḥ | "idamasthamuḥ' (sū.5-3-94) iti sūtreṇa "idam' śabdāt prakāravacane thamupratyayo vidhīyate | pratyate ca ukāra itsaṃjñakaḥ | atra ukārāsañcanaṃ māstu "tham' ityeva pratyayo'stu iti śaṅkāyāṃ, tathā sati makārasya "halantyam' iti sūtreṇetsaṃjñā syāditi tadvāraṇārthamukāraḥ saṃyojitaḥ | tataśca upadeśe makāro'ntyo na bhavatīti netsaṃjñeti samādhānaṃ vaktavyam | paraṃ tu tham iti mānte pratyaye upadiṣṭe'pi tasya "prāgdiśo vibhaktiḥ' (sū.5-3-2) ityanena vibhaktisaṃjñakatvāta "na vibhaktau tusmāḥ' iti sūtreṇaiva makārasyetsaṃjña na bhaviṣyatīti tasya itsaṃjñātaḥ paritrāṇārthaṃ
kriyamāṇamukāroccāraṇaṃ vyartham | kṛtaṃ ca tat taddhitavibhaktisthatusamānāmitsaṃjñāniṣedho neti jñāpayati | tataśca thamo makārasyetsaṃjñā syāditi tanniṣedhārthamukāroccāraṇaṃ sārthakam | tenaiva atprabhṛtitaddhite'pi itsaṃjñāpratiṣedho na prarvatsyatīti "ataddhita iti vaktavyam ' iti vārtikamanāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu taddhitavibhaktau tusmānāmitsaṃjñāniṣedho na pravartata iti jñāpane idānīmityatra "dānīm ca' (sū.5-3-18) iti sūtreṇa vihito yo dānīm pratyayastadantyamakārasyetsaṃjñā prāpnotītyāśaṅkyāha--dānīm tu yāntaḥ kariṣyata iti | dānīmy ita yakārānta eva pratyayaḥ | sa ca cakāraḥ sūtre cakārarūpe vali pare "lopo vyorvali' (sū.6-1-66) iti luptatvānna śrūyate | tathā copadeśe makārasyāntyatvaṃ nāstīti netsaṃjña | na ca pratyayasya yathārāntatve idānīmityādau yakāraśravaṇāpattiriti vācyam | "saṃyogāntasya lopaḥ' (sū.8-2-23) ita yakārasya lopādityāśayaḥ luptanirdiṣṭayakāreṇeti | lopo vyorvalītyaneneti śeṣaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa tasya lopaḥ (sū.1-3-9) "dadātidadhātyorvibhāṣā' (sū.3-1-139) iti jñāpakāt nānubandhakṛtamasārūpyam | "śitsarvasya' (sū.1-1-55) iti jñāpakāt nānubandhakṛtamanekāltvam | "udīcāṃ māṅaḥ' (sū.3-4-19) iti sānubandhakasyātvabhūtasya nirdeśāt nānubandhakṛtamanejantatvam | ekāntā anubandhā ityeva nyāyyam | sahetukatvāt | anudāttaṅitaḥ (sū.1-3-12) "anupasargādvā' (sū.1-3-43) iti vikalpavidhānaṃ jñāpakam vikaraṇebhyo niyamo balīyānityasya | vikaraṇavyavadhāne'pi niyamapravṛtterjñāpakamiti "śadeḥ śitaḥ' (sū.1-3-60) iti sūtre bhāṣye dhvanitam | iti prathamādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa vipratiṣedhe (sū.1-4-2) "asiddhaṃ bahiraṅgam' ityasyāṃ "vāha ūṭh' (sū.6-4-132) ityūṭhgrahaṇaṃ jñāpakam | indre dvāvacau | tatraiko yasyeti lopenāpaḍhiddhaṭhṭhaḍavayate | apara ekādeśena | tato'nacka indraḥ saṃpannaḥ | tatra vṛddheḥ prasaṅgābhāvānnaṣedho vyarthaḥ sanujñāpakaḥ pūrvottarapadayostāvatkāryaṃ bhavati naikādeśo'ntaraṅga iti | yasmātpratyayavidhiḥ (sū.1-3-13) "gatiranantaraḥ' (sū.6-2-49) ityanantaragrahaṇaṃ kṛdgrahaṇe gatikārakapūrvasyāpītyasyāḥ jñāpakam | suptiṅantam (sū.1-4-14) idamantagrahaṇaṃ saṃjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇe tadantagrahaṇaṃ na ityasyā jñāpakam | jhrvi.ṭa etāni sarvāṇyapi paribhāṣārūpāṇi vacanāni paribhāṣenduśekhare vivṛtānīti neha vivriyante | evaṃjātīyakānāṃ kāsāṃcitparibhāṣāṇāmapi jñāpakasaṃgrahe'smin samāveśaḥ kathamupajāta iti na jānīmaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa bahuṣu bahuvacanam (sū.1-4-21) bahuvacananirdeśo jñāpako nānādravyavācibahuśabdasya grahaṇaṃ na vaipulyavācina iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu bahuṣu bahuvacanamiti sūtreṇa bahutve'rthe bahuvacanaṃ vidhīyate | tatra ca bahutvaṃ vaipulyaṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣaśceti dvividham | tatra viśeṣakābhāvāt dvayorapyarthayorbahuvacanaṃ prāpnoti | tataśca bahurodana ityatra vaipulyasya prakṛtito vivakṣitatvāt bahuvacanaprasaṅgaityāśaṅkyāha--bahuvacananirdeśa iti | bahutvasyaikatvāt bahau bahuvacanamiti nirdaiṣṭavye sati yat bahuṣu iti bahuvacananirdeśanaṃ tajjñāpayati nānādravyavācī yo bahuśabdaḥ sa eva bahuṣu ityatropātto na vaipulyavācī bahuśabda iti | ayamāśayaḥ--sūtre bahutvasyaikatve'pi bahutvāśrayasya bahutvāt āśrayagataṃ bahutvaṃ bahutve āropya bahuṣu iti bahuvacananirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ | tena ca yasya bahutvasyāśrayo bahurbhavati tasmin bahutve bahuvacanaṃ bhavatīti sūtrārthaḥ saṃpadyate | vaipulyarūpasya bahutvasyāśraya eka iti ekāśrite vaipulyātmake bahutve bahuvacanaṃ na bhavati | saṃkhyārupasya bahutvasyāśrayo nāneti saṃkhyārupe bahutva eva bahuvacanamiti |
jhrjñā.ṭa tatraiteṣu prapatyayaniyame avyayānāṃ padatvāprāpterarthaniyamo vaktavyaḥ | prakṛtārthāpekṣo vā pratyayaniyamaḥ | jhrviṭa ṭhavdyekayordvivacanaikavacane' "bahuṣu bahuvacanam' (sū.1-4-21) ityādīnāṃ niyamārthatvamāśrīyate | niyamaśva dvividhaḥ pratyayaniyamaḥ arthaniyamaśveti | ekasminnevaikavacanam, dvayoreva dvivacanam, bahūṣveva bahuvacanamiti pratyayaniyamaḥ ekasminnekavacanameva, dvayordvivacanameva, bahuṣu bahuvacanameveti cārthaniyamaḥ | tatra pratyaniyamāśrayaṇe uccais nīcais ityādīnāmavyayānāṃ padasaṃjñā na prapnoti, asubantatvāt | pratyayā hi viśiṣṭāyāṃ saṃkhyāyāṃ niyatā iti niḥ saṃkhyebhyo'vyayebhyaḥ subanutpatteḥ subantatvaprayuktā padasaṃjñā na syāt |padatvābhāve ca rutvādyabhāvaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkya, ata evānupapatteḥ pratyayaniyamaṃ parityajya arthaniyama āśrayaṇīya ityāha--tatraiteṣu iti | eteṣu bahuvacanādiṣu | pratyayaniyamapakṣe'pi na doṣaḥ | prakṛtārthānapekṣya niyamaḥ kriyate | ekatvādayaśca prakṛtāḥ | tataśca ekasminnevaikavacanamiti pratyayaniyamena saṃkhyātvena ekatvasajātīyayordvitvabahutvayorekavacanavyāvṛttirlabhyate | tāvatā niḥ saṃkhyebhyo'vyayebhyaḥ prātipadikārtha ekavacanaṃ na vyārvatyate | tataścāvyayebhyo'pi svādyatpattisiddheḥ padatvamaviruddhamityāśayenāha--prakṛtārthāpekṣo veti | pūrvoktaniyamadvayapratipādakaṃ vārtikaṃ paṭhati--- jhrjñā.ṭa sūpāṃ karmādayo'pyarthāḥ saṃkhyā caiva tathā tiṅām | prasiddho niyamastatra niyamaḥ prakṛteṣu vā || jhrvi.ṭa niyamastatreti | arthaniyama ityarthaḥ | niyamaḥ prakṛteṣu veti | pratyayaniyama ityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa tatrārthe "avyayādāpsupaḥ' (sū.2-4-82) iti jñāpakādvā avyayebhyaḥ supaḥ | avyayebhya autsargikamekavacanamiti pakṣantaraṃ "tadvitaśca'(sū.1-1-31) iti sūtre bhāṣye | jhrvi.ṭa bhavatu sāmānyapekṣaḥ pratyaniyamaḥ ekatva evaikavacanaṃ nānyasmin kasmiṃścidaśyartha iti | tathāpi jñāpakādavyayebhyaḥ svādyutpattiḥ sidhyatītyāha--tatrārtha iti | ekatvādyartha evaikavacanādikamiti pakṣe'pītyarthaḥ | pratyayaniyamena niḥ saṃkhyebhyo'vyayebhyo yadisupo vyārvatyante tarhi avyayebhyaḥ supāmanutpattyā teṣām "avyādāpsupaḥ' (sū.2-4-82) iti lugvidhānamanarthakaṃ syāt | kṛtaṃ ca tat avyayebhyaḥ subutpattirastīti jñāpayati | ato na padatvānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | avyayebhyaḥ supa iti | supa iti prathamābahuvacanam | utpadyanta iti śeṣaḥ | vastutastu - avyayānna sarvavacanānyutpadyante, kiṃ tvekavacanamevetyāha-- avyayebhya iti | "taddhitaścāsarvavibhaktiḥ' iti sūtrabhāṣye "dhdyekayordvivacanaikavacane' "bahuṣu bahuvacanam' iti sutranyāsaṃ bhaṅūktvā "ekavacanam' "dvayordvivacanam' "bahuṣu bahuvacanam' iti sūtravinyāsaṃ kṛtvā ekavacanamutsargataḥ kariṣyate, dvibaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavorarthayostasya dvivacanabahuvacane bādhake ityuktam | tataśca ekavacanamiti sāmānyavidhinā dvitbabahutvābhāve ekavacanamiti labhyate | ato'saṃkhyānāmavyayānāṃ dvitvabahutvābhāvāt sāmānyasutroktamekavacanameva bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ sāmānyasūtreṇa prathamāyā evaikavacanaṃ bhavati, uta sarvavibhaktyekavacanamiti tvanyat | jhrjñā.ṭa adhiparī anarthakau (sū.1-4-93)|| etajjñāpayati anarthakānāmapi nipātānāmarthavat kṛtamiti | idaṃ hi gatyupasargasaṃjñābādhena karmapravacanīyasaṃjñārtham | yadvā arthāntarābhāvādanarthakau dhātunoktāṃ kriyāmāhatuḥ | uktārthānāmapi prayoga ityarthajñāpakam| jhrvi.ṭa nanu "adhiparī anarthakau' iti sutreṇānarthakayoḥ adhiparyoḥ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñālijhānamanakchatam | na ca gatyupasargasaṃjñābādhanārthaṃ tadvidhānam iti vācyam | kriyoyogo hi gatisaṃjñopasargasaṃjñā ca vidhīyate | anarthakayośca
kriyānvitārthakatvarūpakriyāyogābhāvāttayoḥsaṃjñayoraprasakteḥ ityāśaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakya jñāpanārthatayā sūtrasārthakyāmāha--etajjñāpayatīti | anarthakānāmapyādhiparyādīnāmarthavataḥ śabdasya yatkāryaṃ bhavati tadbhavatīti jñāpyate | arthavataḥ kriyoyoganimittake gatyupasargasaṃjñe bhavataḥ | te anarthakayoradhiparyorapi bhavetāmiti tadvāraṇārthaṃ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāvidhānam | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu "nipātasyānarthakasyāpi prātipadikasaṃjñā vaktavyā' iti arthavatsūtre proktaṃ vacanamanāvaśyakamiti | vastutastu adhiparī api nānarthakau | "sarve'pyupasargā dhātvarthadyotakāḥ' iti hi matam | tatra dhātvarthakriyāgataviśeṣadyotakatvamanyeṣāmupasargāṇām | adhiparī tu dhātuvācyāṃ kriyāṃ dyotayataḥ na tu tadgataṃ viśeṣam | etadabhiprāyeṇauva "anarthakā' viti sūtre uktam |tathā ca kriyādyotakatvameva kriyāyogaḥ | tasya cādhiparyorapi sadbhāvāt prāptāṃ gatisaṃjñāmupasargasaṃjñāṃ ca bādhitumanena karmapravacaniyasaṃjñāvidānamiti na sūtravairyathyam | ata eva ca noktavacanajñāpakatvamapītyāśayenāha --yadvā arthāntarābhāvāditi | dhātuvācyakriyāvyatiriktasya tadgataviśeṣarūpasya dyotyārthasyābhāvādityarthaḥ | āhaturiti | dyotayata ityarthaḥ | nanu dhātūktamevārthaṃ yadyadhiparī āhatustarhi tayoḥ prayogo vyarthaḥ, uktārthānāmaprayoga iti nyāyādityata āha--uktārthānāmapīti | ata evoktārthānāmapi prayogo'stīti jñāpyata iti bhāvaḥ | yathā brāhmaṇau dvāvānayetyatra dvivacanenaiva dvitve'vagate sphuṭatarapratipattyarthaṃ punaḥ prayogastadvat punaḥ prayogo na virudhyata iti "adhiparī anarthakau' iti sūtraṃ jñāpayatīti yāvat | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhalaḥ parasmaipadam' (sū.1-4-99)|| sici vṛddhau parasmaipadagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ puruṣasaṃjñā parasmaipadasaṃjñāṃ na bādhata iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "laḥ parasmaipadam' ityatra parasmaipadagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam | kiṃ tu "lasya' (sū.3-4-77) ityatraiva prakaraṇe parasmaipadagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | iha prathamādhyāye parasmaipadasaṃjñāvacane ekasaṃjñādhikārāt tiptasjhiprabhṛtīnāṃ puruṣasaṃjñāyā api vidhānāt parabhūtā sā parasmaipadasaṃjñāṃ bādheta | parasmaipadasaṃjñāvidhānaṃ tu śatṛkvasvoḥ sāvakāśamityāśaṅkyāha--sici vṛddhāviti | yadi hi puruṣasaṃjñayā parasmaipadasaṃjñāyastibādiṣu bādhaḥ syāttadā "sici vṛddhiḥ parasmaipadeṣu' (sū.7-2-1) iti sūtre parasmaipadaśabdena tibādīnāṃ nirdeśo'saṃgataḥ syāt| ataḥ puruṣasaṃjñayā parasmaipadasaṃjñā na bādhyata iti jñāpyata iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa vibhaktiśca (sū.1-4-104)|| trīṇi trīṇītyanuvṛttau "aṣṭanaā' (sū.7-2-84) ityādivacanād bhavati | tannivṛttau "tasmācchaso naḥ puṃsi' (sū.5-2-203) iti natvavidhānāt, aujasoriti vaktavye prathamayorityuktyā ca pūrvasavarṇadīrghavidhau trikagrahaṇamiti jñāpyate | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "vibhaktiśca' (sū.1-4-104) iti sūtre "tiṅastrīṇitrīṇi' (sū.1-4-100) iti sūtrāt trīṇi trīṇi ityanuvartate na vā ? yadyanuvartate tadā trikasyaiva vibhaktisaṃjñā bhavati naikasyeti labhyate | tathā sati aṣṭanśabdasya jasādau ekaikasmin parataḥ ātvaṃ na syāt | "aṣṭana ā vibhaktau, (sū.7-2-84) iti sūtreṇa hi vibhaktau parata ātvaṃ vidhīyate | na hyaṣṭan śabdātparaṃ yugapattrikamastīti doṣaḥ | yadi trīṇi trīṇīti nānuvartate tadā svādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ vibhaktisaṃjñakatvaṃ labhyata ityekaikasmin parato'pyātvamaṣṭanaḥ sidhyati | tathāpi "prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' (sū.6-1-102) ityatra prathamayorvibhaktyorityanena samudāyasyāgrahaṇāt vibhaktisaṃjñakayoḥ prathamayoḥ su au ityanayoreva acītyanuvṛttyā aujasoreva vā parataḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrgho bhavet jasādau parato na bhavet iti pakṣadvaye'pi doṣamāśaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakya samāghate-- trīṇa trīṇītyanuvṛttāviti | anuvṛttipakṣe
trikātmakasamudāyasyaiva vibhaktisaṃjñakatve'pi trikasya yugapatprayogābāvāt ātvavacanasārmathyāt vibhaktiśabdo vibhaktyavayave varti iti vibhattayavayave parata ātvavidhānāt ekaikasmin parato'pyātvaṃ sidhyatīti na doṣaḥ | bhavatīti | aṣṭana ātvamiti śeṣaḥ | trīṇi trīṇīti nānuvartata iti pakṣe pratyekaṃ vibhaktisaṃjñāyāmapi "prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' iti sūtre prathamayostrikayoreva grahaṇaṃ na pratyayayoriti pratipādayati--tannivṛttāviti | trīṇi trīṇītyasyānanuvṛttāvityarthaḥ | "tasmācchaso naḥ puṃsi' iti sūtraṃ hi nacchabdena pūrvasavarṇadīrghaṃ parāmṛśya tataḥ parasya śasavayavasya sakārasya natvaṃ vidhatte | tatra tasmādityasyānupādāne etān gāḥ pasyetyatra go a śas iti sthite "auto'mśasoḥ ' (sū.6-1-93) iti sūtreṇa okāra - akārayoḥ ākāre ekādeśe gās ityavasthāyāṃ sakārasyāpi natvaṃsyāditi tadvāraṇārthaṃ tasmādityucyate | gās ityatratya ākāraḥ na pūrvasavarṇadīrgha iti pūrvasavarṇadīrghātparatvaṃ sakārasya nāstīti na natvam | yadi ca "prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' ityanena prathamayoḥ pratyayayoḥ aujasoreva pūrvasavarṇadīrghātparo na bhaviṣyatīti kiṃ tasmādityanena | tatkriyamāṇaṃ prathamayorityanena trikadvayameva gṛhyata iti jñāpayati | ataḥ amśasorapi pūrvasavarṇadīrghasiddhyā na ko'pi doṣaḥ | athavā aujasoreva pūrvasavarṇadīrghasyeṣṭatve "aujasoḥ pūrvasavarṇa' ityeva sūtraṃ kuryāt | tathā akāraṇācca prathamayostrikayoreva grahaṇamiti jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | ityuktyā cetyatra cakārema natvavidhānaṃ samuccīyate || iti prathamo'dhyāyaḥ |
<2>
|| atha dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
jhrjñā.ṭa pāremadhye ṣaṣṭhyā vā (2-1-18)|| vetyetat jñāpayati samāsataddhitānāṃ vṛttirvibhāṣā (vṛddhi) jhrvṛttiṭa viṣaye nityo'pavāda iti | kṛte tvasminnekayā vṛttirvibhāṣā aparayā vṛttiviṣaye'nityo'pavāda iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "pāremadhye ṣaṣṭhyā vā' iti sūtre vāgrahaṇamanarthakam | na ca vikalpena samāsavidhānārthaṃ tat, tena samāsābhāvapakṣe pāraṃ gaṅgāyā iti vākyasyāpi sādhutvaṃ labhyata iti vācyam | mahāvibhāṣādhikāradeva vikalpasiddheḥ | na ca mahāvibhāṣayā etatsamāsābhāvapakṣe vākyāsya sādhutvalobhe'pi "ṣaṣṭhī' iti sūtraviṅitaṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣasya sādhutvaṃ na labhyata iti gaṅgapāramiti samāsasādhutā na syāditi ṣaṣṭhīsamāsasādhutvabodhanārtha vāgrahaṇamiti vācyam | ṣaṣṭhīsamāsasūtre "pāremadhye' iti sūtre cobhayatra mahāvibhāṣānuvṛttyā ṣaṣṭhīsamāsāpavādasyāpi "pāremadhye' ityavyayībhāvasamāsasya mahāvibhāṣādhikārāt vikalpena bādhakatvamiti etadabhāvapakṣe ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ, tasyāpi vaikalpikatvāt tadabhāvapakṣe vākyasyāpi sādhutvamitīṣṭasiddhaḥ | ato vāgrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkya vacanaviśeṣajñāpakatayā sārthakyamāha--vetyetaditi | pūrvoktarītyā vyarthaṃ sat vāgrahaṇaṃ evaṃ jñāpayati yat samāsavṛttirmahāvibhāṣayā vikalpena bhavati | tatra yadi kaścidapavādo bhavati sa utsargasamāsasya nityamevāpanādaḥ bhavatīti | tenāpavādasūtre mahāvibhāṣānuvṛttāvapi pakṣe vākyasya sādhutvamevānumanyate, na tu utsargasamāsasya | tathā ca prakṛte'pi vāgrahaṇābhāve mahāvibhāṣānuvṛtāvapi ṣaṣṭhī samāsāpavādabhūtasya etasyābhāvapakṣe vākyasādhutvameva labhyeta, na tu ṣaṣṭhīsamāsasya | tadarthaṃ vāgrahaṇam | sati ca tasmin mahāvibhāṣayā vākyāsādhutvaṃ, vāgrahaṇena utsargo'pi pakṣe'bhyanujñāyate iti vākyasya ṣaṣṭhīsamāsasya avyayībhāvasyeti trayāṇāmapi sādhutvaṃ sidhyati | itthaṃ ca mahāvibhāṣādhikāramadhyapatite'pavādasūtre vāgrahaṇaṃ nāsti cet tat utsargasya nityaṃ bādhakatvāt utsargaśāstraṃ nānumanyata iti niṃrṇayo labhyata ityavadheyam | atra ṣaṣṭhīsamāsāpavādabhūtaḥ vaikalpiko'pi pakṣevākyasādhutvamanumanyamānaḥ naṣaṣṭhīsamāsamanumanyata iti ca jñāpanaphalamavaseyam | kṛte tvasminniti | vāgrahaṇe kṛta ityarthaḥ | tathā ca vibhāṣā vā iti vikalpavācakapadadvayasadbhāvāt tatraikena samāsaḥ vikalpena vidhīyate | tena vākyamapi pakṣe sādhu iti labhyate | apareṇa ca apavādaḥ vikalpenocyate | tenotsargo'pi bhavatīti labhyata ityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa caturthā tadarthārtha (sū.2-1-36)| "caturthī tadarthe' (sū.6-2-43) iti pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāva eva, "arthe'(sū.6-2-44) "kte ca' (sū.6-2-45) ityarthagrahaṇācca jñāpakāt | tathā hitasukhagrahaṇāt tādārthyacaturthyā eva tadartheti samāsa iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "taturthī tadartha' (sū.2-1-36) iti sūtreṇa tārdathye vikṛtivācakacaturthyantasya prakṛtivācakena subantena samāso vidhīyata iti vaktavyam | anyathā randhanāya sthālī ityādāvapi tārdathyasamāsāpatteḥ | evaṃ ca aśvaghāsādayaḥ na sidhyanti aśvaghāsayoḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvābhāvāt | na cāśvasya ghāsaḥ aśvaghāsa ityevaṃ tārdathyātmakasaṃbandhavāciṣaṣṭhyantaghaṭitasamāsarūpatā aśvaghāsādīnāṃ bhaviṣyatīti tatra caturthīsamāsābhāve'pi na kṣatiriti vācyam | caturthīsamāsapakṣe "caturthī tadarthe' (sū.6-2-43) iti sutreṇa pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ, ṣaṣṭhīsamāsapakṣe "samāsasya' (sū.6-1-223) iti sūtreṇa samāsāntodāttasvara iti svare bhedāt caturthīsamāsakāryaṃ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsena sidhyati iti vaktuṃ
aśakyatvāt ityāśaṅkya, pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaro'pi prakṛtivikṛtibhāvasthala eva pravarttate | tathā cāśvaya ghāsa iti samāsapakṣe'pi samāsāntodātta eva bhavatīti svare bhedābhāvāt aśvaghāsādīnāṃ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsatvenaiva sādhutvamiti samādhānaṃ manasikṛtya "caturthī tadarthe' (sū.6-2-43) iti sutreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaro'pi prakṛtivikṛtibhāvasthala evetyatra jñāpakamāha--"caturthī tadarthe' ityādinā | ayamāśayaḥ- yadi "caturthī tadarthe' iti sutreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ purvapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvavirahasthale'pi syāt tadā "arthe' (sū.6-2-44) "kte ca' (sū.6-2-45) iti sūtradvayaṃ vyartham | devatārtham ityādau caturthyantasya devakatādipadasya arthaśabde pare pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ "arthe' ita sutreṇa vidhīyate | gohitam ityādau ktānte pare pūrvapadasya caturthyantasya prakṛtisvaraḥ "kte ca' iti sūtreṇa vidhīyate | tatrobhayatrāpi "caturthī tadarthe' iti sūtreṇaiva pūrvapadaprakṛtisvare siddhe punarvidhānaṃ na kartavyam | kṛtaṃ ca tat "caturthī tadarthe' iti sūtravihitaḥ svaraḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāve eveti jñāpayati | tataśca devatārtham gohitam kityādau prakṛtivikārabhāvābhāvāt svarāprāptau tatprāptaye "arthe' "kte ca' iti sūtradvayam | tathā ca aśvaghāsādīnāṃ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsatvenāpi svarādikaṃ sidhyatīti praghaṭṭakārthaḥ | "arthena nityasamāse vaktavya' iti vārtikapratyākhyānārthaṃ mahābhāṣye yogavibhāgaḥ kṛtaḥ | "caturthī' ityekaṃ sūtram | "tadarthārtha' ityaparaṃ sūtram | tatra "caturthī' iti sūtrasya caturthyantaṃ subantena saha samasyata ityarthaḥ | "tadarthārtha' ityasya ca tadarthasyottarapadasya "artha' śabda ādeśo bhavati ityarthaḥ | tataśca brāhmaṇāya payaḥ iti samāse kṛte paya iti punaḥ payaḥ śabdaprayogastu arthaśabdaḥ payaḥ śabdasyādeśa iti sphoraṇārtham iti phalati | yadyovaṃ yogavibhāgaḥ kriyate tarhi chātrāya rucitamityatrāpi rucitaśabdasya arthaśabdādeśaḥ syāt ityāśaṅkāyāṃ bhāṣyakorairuktam-- "ācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati--"tārdathye yā caturthī sā samasyate, na caturthīmātram' iti | yadayaṃ hitasukhagrahaṇaṃ karoti' iti | yogavibhāgapakṣe'pi "caturthī' iti sūtreṇa tārdathye yā caturthī tasyā eva samāsaḥ | na caturthīsāmānyasya | anyathā gobhyo hitaṃ gohitamityatrāpi caturthītyanenaiva samāse siddhe tatra samāsasiddhiphalakasya hitasukhagrahaṇasya vairyathyāpatteḥ | tathā ca chātrāya rucitamityatra yā caturthī na sā tārdathye | atastatra samāsasyaivābhāvāt "tadarthārtha' ityanena samāsāvayavottarapadasya vidhīyamānaḥ artha ityādeśo'pi na bhavatīti bhāṣyabhiprāyaḥ | tamimaṃ saṃdarbhaṃ manasikṛtyāha---tathā hiteti | jhrjñā.ṭa tadvitārtho (sū.2-1-51)|| samāsenokte'pi tadvitārthe tadvitobhavati | "dvigorluganapatye' (sū.4-1-88) iti dvigoruttarataddhitasya lugvidhānanat jñāpakāt | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "taddhitārthottarapadasamāhāre ca' iti sutreṇa taddhitārthe diksaṃkhyayoḥ subantena saha samāsaḥ ucyate | tasya cāyamarthaḥ paryavasyati-- taddhitasya yo'rthastasmit prakṛtyarthavyatiriktapratyayārthābhāvāt pāñcanāpitirityādau taddhito na prāpnotītyāśaṅkyāha--samāsenokte'pīti | "dvigorluganapatye' iti sūtreṇa dvigusamāsāduttarasya anapatyārthakasya taddhitasya lugvidhīyate | yathā pañcasu kapāleṣu saṃskṛtaḥ puroḍaḍḍatdh;āśa ityatra "saṃskṛtaḥ puroḍaḍḍatdh;āśaḥ' ityarthe "saṃskṛtaṃ bhakṣāḥ' (sū.4-2-16) iti sūtreṇa aṇo vidhānāt aṇaḥ arthe pañcan sukapāla su anayoḥ samāse "taddhitārtha' iti sutreṇa kṛte tataḥ aṇi tasya "dvigorluganapatye' (sū.4-1-88) ityanena luki pañcakapālaḥ iti bhavati | atra taddhitārthe
dvigusamāsavidhānāt samāsenokte'rthe taddhitasyāṇaḥ anutpattyā lugvidhānamanarthakam | aṇaḥ utpattau hi tasya lugvidhānam arthavat | tataśca lugvidhānaṃ jñāpayati samāsenokte'pi tadditārthe taddhito bhavati iti | tataśca jātasya taddhitasya lugvidhāyakatayā sutraṃ sārthakam | tena pāñcanāpitirityādau samāsenokte'pyapatyārthe taddhitasiddhirniṣpratyūhetyāśayaḥ | vastutastaddhitārthe vācye ityasya sthāne taddhitārthe viṣaye iti vyākhyāne nāyaṃ doṣaḥ prāpnoti | tathāpi lugvidhānameva taddhitārthe viṣaye iti vivakṣitamiti jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | taduktamudyote--- "evaṃ taddhitārthe viṣayabhūta ityarthena ca sidhyati | vastuto jñāpakenāyamevārtho bodhyata iti bodhyam ||' iti (udyotaḥ ; 2-1-51) jhrjñā.ṭa upamitaṃ vyāghrā (sū.2-1-46)|| sāmānyaprayoge sāpekṣatvādasārmathyena vṛtteraprāpteḥ sa niṣedho jñāpayatibhavati pradhānasya sāpekṣasyāpi vṛttiriti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "upamitaṃ vyāghrādibhiḥ sāmānyāprayoge' (sū.2-1-56) iti sūtre sāmānyāprayoge iti vyartham | na ca puruṣo vyāghra iva śūra ityatra sāmānyadharmavāciśūrādiśabdaprayoge samāsavāraṇārthaṃ taditi vācyam | samasyamānapuruṣaśabdasya samāsāghaṭakaśūrapadasāpekṣatvena samāsāghaṭakapadanirapekṣatvarūpasya sārmathyasyābhāvādevātra samāsasya vārayituṃ śakyatvādityāśaṅkya jñāpakatayā tatsārthakyamāha--sāmānyaprayoga ityādinā | samasyamānayoḥ padayormadhye viśeṣyavācipadasya samāsāghaṭakapadasāpekṣatve'pi samāso bhavatīti jñāpyate | tathā ca puruṣo vyāghra iva śūra ityatra viśeṣyasamarpakasya puruṣapadasya śūrapadasāpekṣatve'pi samāsaprāptau tannivṛttyarthaṃ sāmānyāprayoga iti vacanam | sāmānyavāciśūrapadaprayogānna tatra samāsaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ jñāpanasya prayojanaṃ tu rājapuruṣo'bhirūpa ityādau viśeṣyavācipuruṣapadasya samāsāghaṭakābhirūpādipadasāpekṣatve'pi samāsasiddhiriti | vṛttiriti | samāsa ityarthaḥ | bhāṣye-- "bhavati vai pradhānasya sāpekṣasyāpi samāsaḥ' ityeva vacanaśarīrasyāpi darśanāt | jhrjñā.ṭa yuvā khalati (sū.2-1-67)|| idaṃ jñāpayati prātipadikagrahaṇe liṅgaviśiṣṭasyāpi grahaṇamiti | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyaṃ vyākhyātā paribhāṣenduśekhara ityudāsmahe | jhrjñā.ṭa dvitīyatṛtīya (sū.2-2-3)|| mahāvibhāṣāyāṃ prakṛtāyāmanyatarasyāṃgrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ yatrotsargāpavādaṃ mahāvibhāṣayā vikalpyate tatrāpavādena mukta utsargo na bhavatīti | jhrvi.ṭa vacanametat pūrvaṃ "pāremadhye ṣaṣṭhyā vā' iti sūtrasthavāgrahaṇena jñāpitatayopāttaṃ vyākhyātaprāyam | prayojanaṃ tu pakṣe ṣaṣṭhīsamāso'pīti bhikṣādvitīyamityādeḥ sādhutvasiddhiriti | jhrjñā.ṭa upapadam (sū.2-2-19)|| atiṅiti pratiṣedho jñāpayati anayoryogayornivṛttaṃ "supsupā' (sū.2-1-4) iti | tena gatikārakopapadānāmiti siddham | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyamiti na vyākhyāyate | jhrjñā.ṭa anekamanyapadārthe (sū.2-2-24)|| arthagrahaṇasārmathyāt padārthaḥ kṛtsnaḥ sadravyaḥ saliṅgaḥ sasaṃkhyaśca gṛhyate | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhaanekamanyapadārthe' (sū.2-2-24) iti sūtre bahuvrīhiṇā yadyanyapadārtho'bhidhīyate tadā citragurdevadatta iti bahuvrīhyanantaraṃ devadattasyānuprayogo nopapadyate, bahuvrīhiṇaiva anyapadārthasya devadattasyāpyabhihitatvāt ityāśarūpeṇānabhidhānāt devadattaśabdānuprayoga upapadyata iti samādhānamuktavā, athavā vibhaktyartho'bhidhīyate iti pakṣāntaramuktaṃ bhāṣye | padena prakṛtyarthaviśeṣaṇakaḥ pratyayārtho'bhidhīyata iti vibhaktayarthasyaiva prādhānyāt anyapadārthagrahaṇena grahaṇam | tathā ca bahuvrīhisamāsena vibhaktyarthaḥ saṃbandhādirabhidhīyate
