|| atha tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ ||
jhrjñā.ṭa ādyudāttaśca (sū.3-1-3)|| "yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣvapicca liṅ' iti nyāsena siddhe "yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣūdātto ṅicca' (3-4-103) iti āgamā anudāttā iti jñāpayati | itameva āgamā avidyamānavadityarthasyāpi jñāpakamityuktam | idaṃ dvayamapyekadeśyuktiḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣūdātto ṅicca' iti sūtre udāttagrahaṇaṃ kimartham? pratyayasvareṇaiva pratyayabhaktasya yāsuṭo'pyudāttatvasiddheḥ | na ca liṅādeśāānāṃ pitāṃ tipsipmipāṃ "anudāttau suppitau'(sū.3-1-4) iti sūtreṇānudāttatvāt tadbhaktasya yāsūṭo'pi anudāttatve prāpte tadbādhanārthamudāttavacanamiti vācyam | tathā sati "yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣvapicca liṅ' ityeva sūtrayet tatra apicca liṅ ityanena liṅādeśānāṃ sarveṣāmapittvātideśena pittvaprayuktānudāttatvāsiddheḥ pratyayasvareṇodāttatvasyaiva prāpteḥ udāttagrahaṇavairyathyāt ityāśaṅkya, "āgamā anudāttāḥ' iti vacanajñāpakatayā sārthakyamāha--yāsuḍiḍḍatdh;ti | tathā cāgamānāmanudāttatvāt yāsuṭo'pyanudāttatvaṃ mā prasāṅkṣīditi udāttavacanaṃ kṛtamiti bhāvaḥ | idamevetyādi | jñāpakamuktamiti | "ādyudāttaśca' (sū.3-2-3) iti sūtre iti śeṣaḥ | yāsuḍḍḍatdh;viśiṣṭasyaiva pratyayatvena pratyayādyudāttasvareṇaiva yāsuṭa udāttatve siddhe yāsuṭa udāttavacanaṃ āgamā avidyamānavadityarthajñāpakam | sati cāvidyamānatve tadrahitapratyayasyaivādyudāttatve śeṣanighātenāgamānāmanudāttatvaṃ siddhamiti tatra pratipāditam | tathā ca śeṣanighātaprāptānudāttatvavyāvṛttaye udāttagrahaṇaṃ caritārthamiti tadāśayaḥ | nanu yāsuṭaḥ parasmaipadanimittakatvāt lādeśottaraṃ pravṛttyā pūrvamevāntaraṅgatvātparatvādvā pratyayādyudāttatve tataḥ yāsuḍaḍḍatdh;svarako vā syāt śeṣanighātenānudātto veti udāttasiddhyarthaṃ kriyamāṇamudāttavacanaṃ caritārthaṃ kathaṃ jñāpakamiti āśaṅkya ata eveyamekadeśyuktirityāha--idaṃ dvayamapīti | jhrjñā.ṭa supa ātmanaḥ kyac (sū.3-1-8)|| "aśvāghasyāt' (sū.7-4-37) iti kyaci ītvabādhanārthākāravidhānāt jñāpakāt aghaśabdācchandasi parecchāyāṃ kyajiti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "supa ātmanaḥ kyat' iti sutre ātmanaḥ ityetat supa ityatrānveti | tataśca ātmasaṃbandhyarthabodhakaṃ yat subantaṃ tataḥ icchāyāmarthe kyacpratyayo bhavatīti sutrārtho labhyate | evaṃ sati chandasi "mā tvā vṛkā agāyavaḥ' ityatra aghamicchanta ityarthe aghaśabdātkyac na syāt vṛkā hi na ātmasaṃbandhi aghamicchanti kintu parasyāgham | tathā ca aghaśabdasya eṣitṛvṛkasaṃbandhyaghaboghakatvābhāvāt kathaṃ tataḥ kyajityāśaṅkyāha--aśvāghasyāditīti | chandasi paraṃ parasaṃbandhiviṣayakecchāyāmapi kyac bhavati | tatra jñāpakaṃ tu "aśvāghasyāt ' iti sutram | tasya ca sūtrasya aśva agha ityetayoḥ kyaci parataḥ ākāradeśe bhavatītyarthaḥ | tatrāghaśabdāt parecchāyāmeva kyac saṃbhavati | yate loke na ko'pyātmanaḥ aghamicchet | kiṃ tu parasyaivāghamicchati | evaṃ ca kyaci parataḥ aghaśabdasya ākāraṃ vidadhat pāṇiniḥ aghaśabdāt kyacamanumanyate | ato jñāpakāt aghaśabdāt chandasi parasyāghamicchatītyarthe'pi kyac bhavatīti sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhanamovarivaḥ' (sū.3-1-19)|| kyajādyantāḥ kriyāvacanāḥ | "sanādyantāḥ' (sū.3-1-32) iti dhātusaṃjñāvidhānāt jñāpakāt | tatprayojanaṃ ca tatkāryatavyadādayaḥ | sādhane ca tavyadādayaḥ | kriyāyā eva ca sādhanam | bhāvo'pi kriyaiveti | parasādhane tu naiṣāmutpattiḥ |sādhana iti sasaṃbandhikatvāt | yasya dhātoryat sādhanaṃ tatra taddhātoreva pratyayotpatteḥ | jhrvi.ṭaṭhanamovarivaśvitraṅaḥ kyac' iti sutre "kyajādiṣu pratyayārthanirdeśa'
iti vārtikakāreṇoktam | tasya cāyamarthaḥ--namasśabdāt pūjāyāmarthe kyac, varivasśabdāt saparyāyāmarthe kyac, citraṅaḥ āścarye'rthe kyac bhavatītyevaṃ kyacpratyayasyārthe'pi nirdeṣṭavyaḥ | anyathā kyajantasya kriyāvācakatvaṃ na bhavet iti | idaṃ ca vārtikaṃ pratyāciśyāsunā bhāṣyakṛtā jñāpakena kyajādyantasya kriyāvācakatvaṃ samarthitam | tadāha--kyajādyantāḥ iti | ādiśabdena ṇiṅparigrahaḥ | "sanādyantā dhātavaḥ' (sū.3-1-32) iti sūtreṇa kyajādyantasya dhātusaṃjñā vidhīyate | tatprayojanaṃ ca dhātorityaghikṛtya vihitānāṃ tavyadādīnāṃ siddhiḥ | karmādirūpe sādhane ca tavyadādayo vidhīyante | sādhanaṃ ca kriyāyāḥ | tadyadi kyajādyantasya kriyāvācakatvaṃ na syāt tadvācyakriyāyā abhāvāt kriyāsādhane vidhīyamānāḥ tavyadādayaḥ kyajādyantebhyo na bhaveyuḥ | tataśca tavyadādiphalakadhātusaṃjñāvidhānamanarthakaṃ syāt | yato dhātusaṃjñā uktā ataḥ kriyāvācakatvaṃ kyajādyantasya jñāyata iti bhāvaḥ | nanu sādhane tavyadādaya iti katham | "tayoreva kṛtyaktakhalarthāḥ' (sū.3-4-70) iti sutreṇa bhāve'pi tavyadādīnāṃ vidhānāt ityāśaṅkya, astvevam | tathāpi bhāvaśabdasya yaduttaraṃ tavyādayo bhavanti tadvācyakriyāparatvāt kyajādyantasya kriyāvācakatvābhāve taduttaraṃ bhāve'pi tavyadādirdurlabha iti bhāve tavyadādividhānaṃ tatphalakadhātusaṃjñāvidhānaṃ ca gamayatyeva kyajādyantasya kriyāvācakatāmiti samādhānamāha--bhāvo'pi kriyaiveti | nanu kyajādyantānāṃ kriyāvācakatvābhāve'pi svārthikatvena dravyavācitve'pi ca śabdāntaravācyakriyākārake tavyadādīnām utpattiḥ dhātusaṃjñāyāḥ phalam, yathā dravyavācivṛkṣaśabdāt dṛśikriyākarmatvavivakṣayā vṛkṣaṃ paśyetyādau dvitīyā ityāśaṅkyāha--parasādhane tviti | sādhanaśabdasya sasaṃbandhikatvāt kasya sādhane tavyadādaya ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ yasya dhātorarthasya yat sādhanaṃ tasmāddhātoḥ pratyaya ityeva labhyate | yathā mātari vartitavyamityukte kasya mātari iti śaṅkāyāṃ svasyāṃ mātari iti labhyate tadvat | tathā ca dvitīyādīnāṃ prātipadikādvidhānāt pratipadikasya ca dravyavācakatvāt vacanaprāmāṇyāt parasādhane utpattirbhavati | tavyadādayastu kriyāvāciṣu dhātvantareṣu svasādhane sāvakāśāḥ kyajādyantebhyaḥ parasādhane notpadyante | evaṃ ca svasādhane tavyadādiphalakaṃ dhātusaṃjñāvidhānamevaiṣāṃ kyajādyantānāṃ kriyāvācakatve mānamiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa muṇḍaḍḍatdh;miśra (sū.3-2-21)|| halikalyoratvavidhānāt jñāpakāt vṛddho kṛtāyāṃ lopaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "muṇḍaḍḍatdh;miśraślakṣṇalavaṇavratavastrahalakalakṛtatūstebhyo ṇic' (sū.3-2-21) iti sūtreṇa muṇḍaḍḍatdh;ādibhyaḥ karotītyarthe ṇicpratyayo vidhīyate | tatra ca hala, kala iti dvau śabdau paṭhitau | hali, kali anayorikārāntayoḥ ṇici parataḥ atvaṃ nipātyata iti tena pratīyate | haliṃ gṛḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāti halayati kaliṃ gṛḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāti kalayati iti codāhṛtam | atra hali kali iti ikārāntābhyāmeva ṇic ityevāstu | kimarthaṃ kṛtātvābhyāṃ halikalibhyāṃ ṇic iti vyākhyāyate | na ca sanvadbhāvaniṣedārthaṃ tathā vyākhyāyate, tathā hi--hali kali ābhyāṃ ṇici luṅi caṅi acakalat ajahalat iti rūpe iṣyete | te ca nipātitātvāt ṇici satyeva ghaṭete | ahala i at ityatra "ṇāviṣṭhavat' ityakāralope "caṅi' (6-1-11) iti dvitve ṇilope ca ajahalat iti sthite aṅgasyāglopitvena "anaglope' (8-4-93) iti sanvadbhāvapratiṣedhāt "sanyataḥ' (sū.7-4-89) iti abyāsasya itvaṃ na bhavati | yadi ca hali itīkārāntāt ṇic syāt tadā "ṇāviṣṭhavat' iti iṣṭhavadbhāvātideśāt "ṭeḥ' (sū.6-4-155) iti ikārasya lope "aco ñṇiti'
(sū.7-2-115) iti vṛddhau ca prāptayoḥ paratvāt vṛddhau aikāre tasya "ṭeḥ' iti lope aikārasyāktvābāvāt tallopaḥ aglopo na bhavatīti "anaglope' iti pratiṣedhāpravṛtteḥ "sanvalladhuni' (sū.7-4-93) iti sanvadbhāve "sanyataḥ' ityabyāsasya itve ajīhat acīkalat iti bhavet | ataḥ kṛtātvābhyāṃ halikalibhyāṃ ṇijiti "vyākhyāyata iti vācyam | "nāglopiśāsvṛditām'(sū.7-4-2) iti sūtrebhāṣye aggrahaṇādvṛddherlopo balīyān iti jñāpitatvena a hali i at atyatrāpi vṛddhiṃ bādhitvā hali itīkārasya ṭeḥ lope aglopitvaṃ aṅgasyāstīti "anaglope' iti niṣedhāt sanvadbhāvāprāpteḥ | tathā cādantatvanipātanaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkyāha--halikalyoriti | pūrvektarītyā vyarthaṃ sadadantatvanipātanaṃ halikaliśabdaviṣaye vṛddhauṃ kṛtāyāṃ lopa tii jñāpayati | tathā ca ikārāntāṇṇici ādau vṛddhau tato lope aglopitvābhāvena sanvadbhāve "sanyataḥ' iti itvaṃ bhavediti kṛtātvābhyāṃ ṇic uktaḥ | itthaṃ ca hala kala ityadantābhyāṃ ṇici vṛddhaukṛtāyāmākāre tasya lope'pi aglopo bhavatīti "anaglope' iti niṣedhānna sanvadbhāva iti nābhyāsasyāta itvamiti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣye tu--"halikalyoratvanipātanaṃ sanvadbhāvapratiṣedhārtham' iti dṛśyate | tasyāpi kathaṃciduktārthe tātparyamavaseyam | jhrjñā.ṭa cleḥ sic (sū.3-1-44)|| sicaḥ kitkaraṇāt jñāpakāt sijantasya nalopo neti | evaṃ ca sica idittvaṃ pratyākhyeyam | sthānivadbhāvenedittvasiddheśca | jhrvi.ṭa nanu cleḥ sthāne yaḥ sijādeśaḥ tasminnādeśe cakāraḥ ikāraścobhāvitsaṃjñakāviṣyete | tatrekārākhyānubandhasaṃyojanam vyartham | na ca sijantasya idittvaprayuktakāryārthaṃ tatsaṃyojanam | tathā hi aman s ta ityatra sicaḥ idittvābhāve sijantasyāṅgasyānidittvena tadupadhāyā nakārasya "aniditām' (sū.6-4-24) iti lopaḥ syāt iti vācyam | "hanaḥ sic,(sū.1-2-14) iti sūtreṇa hanteḥ parasya sicaḥ kidvidhānāt jñāpakāt sijantasya nalopo neti jñāpanāt amaṃstetyatra nalopābhāvasiddheḥ | tathā hi--āṅpūrvakahanadhātorluṅi cliḥ cleḥ sic ta, "ā han s ta' iti sthite "hanaḥ sic' ityanena sicaḥ kittvāt | tasmin parataḥ "anudāttopadeśavanatitanotyādīnāmanunāsikalopo jhali kṅiti' (sū.6-4-37) iti sūtreṇa nakārasyānunāsikasya lope "ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvādaṅgāt' (sū.8-2-27) iti sūtreṇa sakārasya lope āhata iti rūpam | atra sicaḥ kittvāvidhānaṃ vyarthaṃ yadi sac iti anidittvaṃ tasya syāt | tadā hi āhan s iti sajantāṅgasyānidittvena "aniditāṃ hala upadhāyāḥ kṅiti' (sū.6-4-24) iti sūtreṇa nalope iṣṭasiddheḥ kiṃ sicaḥ kittvāvidhānena | kṛtaṃ ca tat sijantasya upadhāyā nakārasya "aniditāṃ hala upadhāyāḥ' iti lopo na bhavatīti jñāpayati | tathā ca hanternakārasya lopasiddhaye sicaḥ kittvavidhānaṃ sārthakam | amaṃstetyatra ca sijantasya nalopo na bhavatītyuktyā nalopābhāvaḥ sidhyatīti tadarthaṃ sica idittvaṃ nāvaśyakam | tathā ca sica idittvaṃ māstu | sac ityevāstu tii nirṇayaḥ | tadetadāha--sicaḥ kitkaraṇāt ityādinā | cli ityasyedittvāt tatsthāne jātasya sicaḥ sthānivadbhāvenaivedittvaṃ labhyate | tathā ca sarvathā sica idittvaṃ vyarthamevetyāha--sthānivadbhāveneti | vastutastu idittvaṃ vināpi sakaleṣṭasiddheḥ pradarśitatvāt sthānivadbhāvenedittvāsādhanaṃ mudhā | ata eva "ididvāsthānivattvāt' iti vārtikaṃ ekadeśyuktiḥ' iti bhāṣyapradīpodyote granthakāreṇaivoktamiti avadheyam | jhrjñā.ṭa vāsarūpo'striyām (sū.3-1-94)|| "dadātidadātyorvibhāṣā' (sū.3-1-139) iti jñāpakāt nānubandhakṛtamasārūpyam | "haśaśvatorlaṅ
ca' (sū.3-2-116) iti jñāpakāt lodeśeṣu vāsarūpavidhirnāsti iti | jhrvi.ṭa anayorvacanayoḥ paribhāṣārūpatvādiha na vyākhyāyete | iti tṛtīyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhacareṣṭaḥ' (sū.3-2-16)|| "bhikṣāsenādāyeṣu ca' (sū.3-2-17) ityatra bhikṣāgrahaṇāt jñāpakāt adhikaraṇa eva na karmaṇi | jhrvi.ṭa nanu subante upapade caradhātoḥ "careṣṭaḥ' (sū.3-2-16) iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ ṭapratyayaḥ kurūṃścarati ityatra kuto na bhavati | na ca "careṣṭaḥ' ityatra "adhikaraṇe śeteḥ'(3-2-15) ityataḥ adhikāraṇe ityasyānuvṛtteḥ karmaṇyupapade na bhavatīti vācyam | karmaṇītyasyāpyanuvṛtteḥ | anyathā uttarasūtrairvidhīyamānānāṃ pratyayānāṃ karmaṇyupapade prāptyasaṃbhavāt ityāśṅkya "careṣṭaḥ' iti sūtraṃ param adhikaraṇe upapada eva bhavati na karmaṇi iti jñāpakāt sādhayati--bhikṣāsenādāyeṣu cetyatreti | yadi "careṣṭaḥ' iti sūtraṃ karmaṇyupapade'pi pravartteta tarhi bhikṣāṃ caratītyatra anenaiva ṭapratyayena bhikṣācara iti rūpasiddheḥ "bhikṣāsenādāyeṣu ca' (sū.3-2-16) iti sūtre bhikṣāgrahaṇaṃ vyartham | kṛtaṃ ca tat "careṣṭaḥ' iti sūtram adhikāraṇe upapada eva bhavatīti jñāpayati | tathā ca karmaṇyupapade ṭapratyayārthaṃ bhikṣāgrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | prayojanaṃ tu kurūṃścarati ityādau karmaṇyupapade careṣṭo na bhavatīti bhābaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa satsūdviṣa (sū.3-2-61)|| atropasarge'pīti jñāpayati anyatra sūbgrahaṇe upasargagrahaṇaṃ neti | tena vadaḥ supyanupasargagrahaṇaṃ coditam | tanna vaktavyaṃ bhavati | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhasatsūdviṣadruhaduhayujavidabhidacchidajinīrājāmupasarge'pi kkip' (sū.3-2-61) iti sūtre vartikakāreṇa "sadādiṣu subgrahaṇam' iti proktam | subante upapade sadādibhyaḥ kkipū bhavatīti tadarthaḥ | vedyāṃ sīdatītyatra sūbante vediśabde upapade sadadhātoḥ kkipi vediṣat iti udāharaṇam | bhāṣyakārastāvat yadi subante upapade kkibityucyate tarhi upasargasyāpi subantatvāt supītyanenaiva tadupasaṃgrahāt sūtre upasarge'pīti na vaktavyamityāśaṅkya jñāpakārtha tadvaktavyamityuktavān | supītyanenaiva upasargasyāpi grahaṇe siddhe upasargagrahaṇaṃ kriyamāṇamevaṃ jñāpayati yat yatra supi upapade pratyayaścodyate tatra upasarge upapade na bhavatīti tadāśayaḥ | tathā ca upasarge upapade'pi kkippratyayārthaṃ "satsūdviṣa' iti sūtre upasargagrahaṇaṃ kṛtamiti cāritārthyat | prayojanaṃ tu--"vadaḥ supi kyap ca' (sū.3-2-106) iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānaḥ kyap pratyayaḥ upasarge upapade na bhavatīti | evaṃ ca tatsūtre "anupasargagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam' iti vārtikaṃ na kartavya bhavatīti siddham | ata eva pravādyamityādau upasarge upapade vadeḥ kyap na bhavati | sarvamidamabhipretyāha--atropasarge'pīti | jhrjñā.ṭa ātmamāne (sū.3-2-83)|| tāseḥ parasya lasārvadhātukasyānudāttatvavidhānaṃ jñāpayati sati śiṣṭo'pi vikaraṇasvaraḥ sārvadhātukasvaraṃ na bādhata iti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaātamamāne khaś ca' iti sūtre "karmakartari ca' iti vārtikena ātmakarmakamanane vartamānāt manyateḥ supyupapade karmakartaryarthe khaś bhavatīti kathitam | evaṃ vaktuścāyamabiprāyaḥ yat asmin khaśi pare "karmavat karmaṇā tulyakriyaḥ' (sū.3-1-87) iti sūtreṇa karmavaddhāvāt "sārvadhātuke yak' (sū.3-2-67) iti yak bhavati | tathā ca darśanīyaṃmanya ityatra yakāraśravaṇaṃ phalamiti | tadupari evamāśaṅkā bhavati---atra karmakartṛgrahaṇaṃ māstu | kartaryevāyaṃ khaś vidhīyatām | divāditvāt asya manadhātoḥ kartrarthake sārvadhātuke khaśi pare śyani darśanīyaṃmanya iti yakāraghaṭitaṃ rūpaṃ sidhyatyeva | na ca rūpaṃ yadyapi sidhyati, tathāpi svare bhedo bhavati yaki sati antodāttasvaraḥ pratyayasvareṇa, śyani sati "ñnityādirnityam' (sū.6-1-197) ityādyudāttasvaraḥ iti
vācyam | khaśsvareṇa śyansvarasya bādhāt antodāttatvasiddheḥ | evamapi ādau khaś tasya svaraśca, tadanantaraṃ śyan tatprayukta svaraśceti śyansvarasya khaśsvare sati vihitatvāt balīyastvamityādyudāttatvameva bhavediti | asyāḥ śaṅkāyāḥ jñāpakasiddhaṃ vacanamālambya samādhānaṃ brūte---tāseḥ parasyeti | atrāyamāśayaḥ--"tāsyanudāttenṅidadupadeśāllasārvadhātukamanudāttam' (sū.6-1-186) iti sūtreṇa tās ityataḥ parasya lasārvadhātukasyānudāttatvaṃ vidhīyate | yathā kartā ityādau "ā' ityasya tāsaḥ parasya luḍaḍḍatdh;ādeśasya anudāttatvaṃ bhavati | pratyayasvarasyāpavādo'yam | yadi sati śiṣṭaḥ svaraḥ balīyān syāt tarhi pratyayasvare sati śiṣṭaḥ tāssvaraḥ pratyayasvaraṃ bādhata iti tenaiva pratyayasyānudāttatve siddhe tāseḥ parasya lasārvadhātukasya anudāttavidhānaṃ nāvaśyakam | yato'nudāttatvaṃ vihitaṃ tenaivaṃ jñāpyate yat sati śiṣṭo'pi vikaraṇasvaraḥ sārvadhātukasvaraṃ na bādhate iti | tathā ca kartā ityādau pratyayasvaro bhavediti tadbādhanārthaṃ tāseḥ parasyānudāttavidhānam | evaṃ ca darśanīyaṃmanya ityādau yadi śyan tadā sa khakhsvaraṃ na bādhata itiantodāttataiva, yakyapi antodāttataiveti svare'pi bhedābhāvāt śyanaiveṣṭasiddheḥ kartaryevāyaṃ khaś ucita iti karmakartṛgrahaṇaṃ na kartavyamiti śthitam | jhrjñā.ṭa laṭ sme (sū.3-2-118)|| idaṃ jñāpayati--smalakṣaṇaḥ purālakṣaṇaścadyatane na bhavata iti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhalaṭ sme' iti sūtrasya smābde upapade bhūtānadyatanaparokṣe laṭpratyayo bhavatītyarthaḥ | liṭo'yamapavādaḥ | nalena sma purādhīyata ityādikamudāharaṇam | tatra ca sūtre vārtikadvayam--"sma purā bhūtamātre, na sma purādyatane' iti | tasya cāyamarthaḥ--prathame vārtike smapurāśabdābhyāmādyantābhyāṃ "laṭ sme' "aparokṣeca' "nanau pṛṣṭaprativacane' "nanvorvibhāṣā' "puri luṅ cāsme' (sū.3-2-118-3-2-122) iti pañcasūtrī lakṣyate | tatra bhūtamātre pratyayo bhavati | smapurālakṣaṇastu pratyayaḥ adyatane na bhavati | arthāt "laṭ sme' "aparokṣe ca' "puri luṅcāsme' iti sūtratrayeṇa anadyatane bhūtaviśeṣe pratyayo vidhīyate | "nanau pṛṣṭaprativacane' "nanvorvibhāṣā' ityetābhyāṃ tu bhūtamātre pratyayo vidhīyata iti vyavasthā āśrayaṇīyā iti | iyaṃ ca vyavasthā sūtrato na labhyata iti vartikakārasyāśayaḥ | vastutastu sūtrādeveyaṃ vyavasthā labhyata ityāha--idaṃ jñāpayatīti | "aparekṣe ca' (sū.3-2-119) iti uttarasūtrārambhāt "laṭ sme' (sū.3-2-118) iti sūtre parokṣagrahaṇamanuvartate ikati jñāyate | tataśca parokṣagrahaṇasaṃbaddhamanadyatanagrahaṇamapi tatra anuvartata iti "laṭ sme' iti sutraṃparokṣānadyatanaviṣayamiti sidhyati | "aparokṣe ca' (sū.3-2-119) ityayamaparokṣānadyatanaviṣayaḥ | "puri luṅ cāsme' (sū.3-2-122) ityatra tu luṅgrahaṇamahimnā anadyatanaviṣayakatvaṃ labhyate | yadi hyayaṃ bhūtamātre vidhiḥ syāt tadā "nanvorvibhāṣā' (sū.3-2-121) ityato vibhāṣāgrahaṇānuvṛttyā vikalpena laṭo vidhānāt tadabhācavapakṣe bhūtasāmānye vihito luṅ bhaviṣyatīti luṅgrahaṇamanarthakam | kṛtaṃ ca tat "puri' iti sūtrasyānadyatane luṅaḥ avidhānāt iti pakṣe luṅlābhārthaṃ luṅgrahaṇam | itthaṃ ceyaṃ trisūtrī anadyatanabhūtaviṣayiṇīti siddham | madhyamayoḥ "nanau pṛṣṭaprativacane' (sū.3-2-120) "nanvorvibhāṣā' (3-2-121) iti sūtrayostu parokṣagrahaṇasyāparokṣagrahaṇasya ca nivṛttyā tatsahacaritānadyatanagrahaṇasyāpi nivṛtterbhūtamātraviṣayakatvamiti viṣayavibhāgasya sūtreṇaiva lābhādvārtikamanavakaramiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa nindahiṃsa (sū.3-2-146)|| nindādibhyo vuñvacanāt jñāpakāt tācchīlikeṣu sarvala eva tṛjādayo vāsarūpeṇa na bhavanti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu
"nindahiṃsa'(sū.3-2-146) ityādisūtreṇa nindādibhyo vuñvidhānamanarthakam | "ṇvultṛcau' (sū.3-2-133) iti sūtra vihitaṇvulaivaiṣṭasiddheḥ | rūpasvarayorviśeṣābhāvāt | nindaka ityatra ṇvulpakṣe "liti' (sū.6-2-193) iti sūtreṇa pratyayātpūrvamudāttaṃ bhavatīti dhātorikāraḥ udāttaḥ | vuñpakṣe'pi "ñnityādirnityam'(sū.6-1-197) iti sūtreṇa samudāyādiḥ sa evodātta iti svare'pi bhedābhāvāt | na ca ekācdhātuviṣaye svare bhedo yadyapi nāsiti, tathāpyanekācdhātuviṣaye svarabhedo bhavatyeva | atra sūtre asūyadhāturapa nirdiṣṭaḥ | tato vuñi asūyakaśabde samudāyādirudāttaḥ | ṇvuli tu pratyayātpūrvaḥ sakārottara ūkāra udātta iti svare bhedo'stīti tadarthaṃ sūtramiti vācyam | tarhi "asūyo vuñ' ityeva sūtramastu nindādigrahaṇasya vyarthatvādityā śaṅkya vacanaviśeṣajñāpakatayā sārthakyamāha--nindādibhya iti | ayamarthaḥ--nindādighahaṇābhāve nindaka ityādirūpaṃ na sidhyati | na ca vāsarūpanyoyena tṛnaḥ vikalpena ṇvulbādhakatvāt pakṣe ṇvul sidhyatīti vācyam | tarhi tata eveṣṭasiddheḥ nindādigrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ sat tacchīlādiṣvartheṣu vāsarūpanyāyo na pravartata iti jñāpayatīti tṛnā nityaṃ ṇvulo bādhā syāt | tathā ca nindaka ityasya rūpasya siddhyarthaṃ vuñasūtre nindādigrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃ tācchīlikaprakāraṇavihitapratyayaviṣaye sāmānyasūtraṃ na pravartata iti | vāsarūpanyāyena prāptāstṛjādayaḥ tācchīlikeṣu na bhavantītyanvayaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa jucaṅkramya (sū.3-2-250)|| atra padagrahaṇāt jñāpakāt tācchīlikāstācchīlikeṣu vāsarupeṇa na bhavanti | "sūdadīpe' tyatra dīpagrahaṇāt jñāpakāt bhavati yuco reṇa samāveśaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "jucaṅkramyadandramyasṛgṛdhijvalaśucalaṣapatapadaḥ' iti sūtreṇa juprabhṛtibhyo dhātubhyaḥ tacchīlādiṣu kartṛṣu "yuc' pratyayo vidhīyate | tatra padadhātorgrahaṇamanarthakam | tasya anudāttettvena halāditvena ca "anudāttetaśca halodeḥ'(sū.3-2-149) iti sāmānyasūtreṇaiva yucpratyayasiddheḥ | na ca "laṣapatapadasthābhūvṛṣahanakamagamaśṝbhya ukañ'(sū.3-2-154) iti viśeṣavihitena ukañā sāmānyavihitasya yuco bādhe prāpte ukañā saha samāveśasiddhyarthaṃ padeḥ atra yucsūtre grahaṇamiti vācyam | vāsarūpanyayona asarūpasya ukañaḥ vikalpena yucbādhakatvātpakṣe yuco'pi siddheḥ ityāśaṅkya jñāpanārtha padagrahaṇamityāha--atra padagrahaṇāditi | pūrvoktarītyā vyarthaṃ sat padagrahaṇamevaṃ jñāpayati yat tācchīlikeṣvapavādavidhiṣu tācchīlikā utsargavidhayaḥ vāsarūpanyāyena prasaktāḥ pratiṣiddhā bhavantīti | itthaṃ ca ukañā yucaḥ bādhāt yuco'pi siddhyarthaṃ padagrahaṇaṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadyevaṃ jñāpyate tarhi "sūdadīpadīkṣaśva'(sū.3-2-153) iti sūtre dīpigrahaṇaṃ vyartham | tena hi sūtreṇa dīperthuc niṣidhyate | yucca "anudāttetaśca halādeḥ' (sū 3-2-149) iti sūtreṇa prāptaḥ | tasya "namikampiramyajasakamahiṃsadīpo raḥ '(3-2-167) iti sūtravihito rapratyayaḥ apavādaḥ | ubhayorapi tācchīlikatvāt tācchīlikāpavādarapratyayaviṣaye tācchīlikasyotsargasya yucaḥ uktajñāpakamahimnā aprāpteḥ tanniṣedhasiddhyarthaṃ "sūdadīpadīkṣaśca' iti sūtre tīpighahaṇamanarthakamityāśaṅkya, ata eva "yuco reṇa samāveśaḥ' iti vacanajñāpanadvārā tat sārthakam ityāha--sūdadīpetyatreti | rapratyayena yucpratyayasamāveśāt kadācit dīpeḥ yujapi syāditi tanniṣedhalābhārthaṃ dīpigrahaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ jñāpanasya prayojanaṃ tu kamrā kanyā ityādau pakṣe yujapi bhavati tena kamanā kanyā ityādisiddhiriti | || iti tṛtīyādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ || jhrjñā.ṭa tumunṇvulau (sū.3-3-10)| purnaṇvalvidhānaṃ jñāpayati kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyāmupapade
vāsarūpeṇa tṛjādayo neti | evaṃ ca bādhaka (laḍiḍḍatdh;vaṣaye)jhrlṛḍiḍḍatdh;vaṣayeṭa ṇvularthaṃ tat | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhananu tumunṇvulau kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyām' iti sūtreṇa ṇvulvidhānamanarthakam | "ṇvultṛcau' iti sāmānyasūtreṇaiva kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyāmupapade bhaviṣyati kāle ṇvulsiddhyā bhoktuṃ vrajatītyarthe bhojako vrajatītyādisiddheḥ | na ca "lṛṭ śeṣe ca' (sū.3-3-23) iti sūtreṇa kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyāmupapade bhaviṣyati lṛṭpratyayavidhānāt viśeṣavihitena tena ṇvulo bādhā mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ ṇvulgrahaṇamiti vācyam | vāsarūpapanyāyena vikalpena lṛṭaḥ ṇvulbādhakatvāt pakṣe ṇvulo'pi siddheḥ ityāśaṅkyāha--purnaṇvulvidhānamiti | tathā ca kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyāmupapade vāsarūpanyāyo na pravartata iti jñāpanāt lṛṭā ṇvulo bādho mā bhūdityetadartha purnaṇvulvidhānam | tataśca vādhakalṛḍiḍḍatdh;vaṣaye'pi ṇvul siddhyati | tena ca ṇvulbhinnāḥ tṛjādayaḥ pratyayāḥ lṛṭā nityaṃ bādhyante | ataḥ kariṣyāmīti vracatītyarthe kārako vrajatītivat kartā vrajatītyādayo na prayogāḥ iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa grahavṛdṛniści (sū.3-3-58)|| steyasya (sujvidhau) jhrghañvidhauṭa pratiṣedhāt asteye cāniṣṭatvāt | evaṃ ca niścigrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati--"thāthaghañktāvitrakāṇām'(sū.6-2-144) ityantodāttatvaṃ niṣpūrvāccinoterneti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu abvidhāyake "grahavṛdṛniścigamaśca' iti sutre niṣpūrvasya cidhātorgrahaṇamanarthakam | "erac' (sū.3-3-56) ityacpratyayenaiva niścaya iti rūpasiddheḥ | acpakṣe cittvāt antodāttatā, appakṣe'pi "thāthadhañktābitrakāṇām' ityanena antodāttateti svare'pi viśeṣābhāvāt | na ca "hastādāne cerasteye'(sū.3-3-40) iti sūtreṇāsteye ghañprāptau tadbādhanārthaṃmabvidhānamiti vācyam | "asteye' iti pratiṣedhena steye ghaño'prāpteḥ | asteye ca hastādānaviṣaye niścāya ityevaṃ ghañeveṣyate nāp | nathā ca niṣpūrvāccinoterabvidhānamanarthakamityāśaṅkya, ata eva "thātha' ityādisūtreṇa vihitaḥ antodāttasvaraḥ niṣpūrvāccinoterneti jñāpayati | tathā ca appratyayapakṣe thāthādisvarāpravṛttyā dhātusvareṇa cayaśabde cakārottarākāraḥ udāttaḥ | sa eva niścayaśabde'pi "gatikārakopapadāt kṛt'(sū.6-2-139) iti sūtreṇa bhavatīti madhyodatto niścayaśabdaḥ | acpakṣe cāntodātta iti svarabhedāt hastādānaviṣaye steye acā svaraparyanteṣṭasiddherasaṃbhavāt niṣpūrvāccerappratyayavidhānamiti samādhānamāha---steyasyetyādinā | ghañvidhau iti | "hastādāne cerasteye' (sū.3-3-40) iti vidhāvityarthaḥ | aniṣṭatvāditi | appratyayasyeti śeṣaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa praiṣātisarga (sū.3-3-163)|| idaṃ kṛtyavacanaṃ loṭā bādhābhāvārthaṃ sat strayadhikārādūrdhvaṃ vāsarūpavidhirneti jñāpayati | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "praiṣātisargaprāptakāleṣu kṛtyāśca' iti sūtre kṛtyagrahaṇaṃ māstu | sāmānyena bhāvakarmaṇorvihitāḥ kṛtyāḥ praiṣādiṣvanyatra ca bhaviṣyanti | na ca praiṣādiṣu viśeṣavihitenānena loṭā kṛtyānāṃ bādhā mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ kṛtyagrahaṇamiti vācyam | vāsarūpanyāyena loṭo vikalpena bādhakatvāt pakṣe kṛtyānāmapi siddheḥ ityāśaṅkya, evaṃ tarhi stryadhikārādūrdhvaṃ vāsarūpavidhirneti jñāpanārthaṃ tat | tathā ca asya "praiṣātisarga' iti sūtrasya "striyāṃ ktit' (sū.3-3-94) ityadhikārātparaṃ vidyamānatvena atra vāsarūpanyāyānavatārāt loṭā kṛtyānāṃ nityaṃ bādhe prāpte samāveśasiddhyarthaṃ kṛtyagrahaṇamityāha--idaṃ kṛtyavacanamiti | na ca "vāsarūpo'striyām' ityatra astriyāmityanenaivāsyārthasya siddheḥ jñāpanapanavakaramiti vācyam | tena hi striyāmityadhikāre vāsarūpo neti jñāyate |striyāmityadhikārādūrdhvaṃ neti na labh.yate ityāśayāt | striyāṃ
ityadhikāraśca "napuṃsake bhāve ktaḥ'(sū.3-3-114) ityatraiva vicchinna iti dhyeyam | iti tṛtīyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ jhrjñā.ṭa udīcāṃ māṅo (sū.3-4-19)|| atra sānubandhakasyātvabhūtasya grahaṇaṃ jñāpayati nānubandhakṛtamanejantatvamiti | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyam | ato na vyākhyāyate | jhrjñā.ṭa lasya (sū.3-4-77)|| ṇalo litkaraṇāt lakārāṇāṃ lasya nettvam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu pacatītyādau laṭaḥ sthāne tibādeśaḥ | lakāraśca "laśakkataddhite' (sū.1-3-8) iti sūtreṇetsaṃjñakaḥ | tataśca laṭaḥ littvāt tadādeśasya tibāderapi sthānivadbhāvena littvāt pacatītyādau litsvaraḥ prāpnotītyāśaṅkyāha--ṇalo litkaraṇāditi | liṭaḥ sthāne tiptasya sthāne ṇal tatra sthānivadbhāvenaiva ṇalo littve sidve ṇalo litkaraṇaṃ laḍaḍḍatdh;ādilakārāṇāṃ lasyetsaṃjñā neti jñāpayati | tathā ca sthānina eva littvābhāvāt ādeśe taddurlabhamiti ṇali litkaraṇaṃ sārthakam | evaṃ ca pacatītyādāvapi tibāderlittvaṃ neti na litsvaraprasaṅga ita bhāvaḥ | bhāṣye tu-- "na lādeśeṣu litkṛtaṃ bhavati' iti vacanaśarīraṃ dṛśyate | ubhayatra phalamekamevetyavadheyam | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭita ātmanepadānāṃ ṭere (sū.3-4-79)|| ṭita ātmanepadānāmiti sūtre ānasya pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ | uktaṃ vā | jñāpakaṃ vā sānubandhakasyādeśavacanamitkāryābhāvasyeti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "ṭita ātmanepadānāṃ ṭere' iti sūtreṇa ṭito lakārasya sthāne yānyātmanepadāni teṣāṃ ṭeḥ etvaṃ vidhīyamānaṃ, pacamāna ityādau ānasya ṭerapi prāpnoti | ānasyāpi ṭitsthānikātmanepadatvādityāśaṅkaya,"ṭita etve ātmanepadeṣvānapratiṣedhaḥ' iti vārtikāt ānasya na etvamityāha--iti sūtra ityādinā| vastuto "gāṅ liṭi' (sū.2-4-49) iti sūtre iṅaḥ dhātoḥ gāṅ ādeśo vidhīyate | tatra dhātoḥ ṅittvāt ādeśasya gā ityasyāpi sthānivadbhāvena ṅittvaṃ bhaviṣyatīti ādeśe ṅakārānubandho vyarthaḥ san yatra sānubandhakasya ādeśaḥ ucyate tatra ādeśe itsaṃjñāprayuktāni kāryāṇi na pravartanta iti jñāpayatītyuktam | tathā ca "laṭaḥ śatṛśānacau' iti sānubandhakasya sthāne śānacaḥ vidhānāt śānaci ṭittvaprayuktamettvaṃ na pravartiṣyate | pacate ityādau "lasya' iti sūtreṇa anubandharahitasya sthāne vidhānāt tādīnāṃ etvaṃ prarvatsyati, ato na doṣaityāśayavānāha--aktaṃ veti | "gāṅ liṭi' iti sūtre vārtike iti śeṣaḥ | atra ca-- "sānubandhādyatra ṣaṣṭhī ucyāryate tatrākṛtāyāmevetsaṃjñāyāmādeśaḥ pravartate| kṛtāyāṃ vā itsaṃjñāyāṃ tannibandhanāni kāryāṇi na pravarttante' iti "gāṅ liṭi' iti sūtrasthaḥ pradīpagrantho'nusandheyaḥ | iti tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<4>
|| atha caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||
jhrjñā.ṭa ajādya (sū.4-1-4)|| "śūdrā cāmahatpūrvā' (vā) iti jñāpayati iha tadantavidhiriti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaajādyataṣṭāp' iti sūtre "sūdrā cāmahatpūrvā jātiḥ' iti vārtikam | tasya ca śūdrajātiḥ vācyā cet amahatpūrvaḥ śūdraśabdaḥ striyāṃ ṭāpaṃ labhate ityarthaḥ | ajādigaṇe paṭhitasya śūdraśabdasya ṭāp sāmānyataḥ prāptaḥ | tatra jātivācakatvaṃ mahacchabdapūrvakatvābhāvaśceti viśeṣaṇadvalayaṃ yadyasti śūdraśabdasya tadaiva ṭāp iti vyavakthā vārtikakṛtā kṛtā bhavati | tatra amahatpūrvā iti viśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham | mahāśūdraśabdasya ajādigaṇe pāṭhābhāvādeva ṭāpo'prasakteḥ | na ca śūdraśabdo'jādiṣu paṭhyate | tena ca tadantavidhinā mahāsūdraśabdo'pi gṛhyeteti vācyam | grahaṇavatā prātipadikena tadantavidhaḥ mahāśūdraśabdo'pi gṛhyeteti vācyam | grahaṇavatā prātipadikena tadantavidheḥ pratiṣedhāt ityāśaṅkya strīpratyayeṣu tadantavidhirastīti jñāpanārthaṃ tadityāha--śūdrā ceti | tathā ca tadantavidhinā śūdraśabdenamahāśūdrabdo'pi gṛhyeteti tadhdyāvṛttyarthamamahatpūrvagrahaṇam | jñāpanaprayojanaṃ tu "bhavatī' ityatra ugidantāt bhavacchabdāt yathā "ugitaśca' (sū.4-1-6) iti ṅīp tathā tadantavidhinā ugidantāt atibhavacchabdādapi ṅīp bhavatīti atibhavatīti rūpasiddhiḥ iti bhāvaḥ | iheti | strīpratyayaprakaraṇa ityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭiḍḍḍatdh;ḍhāṇañ (sū.4-1-15) vāmadevāḍḍḍatdh;ḍaḍḍatdh;yaḍḍḍatdh;ḍaḍḍatdh;yau (sū.4-2-9) iti ḍiḍḍatdh;ttvam "ananubandhakagrahaṇe na sānubandhakagrahaṇam' "tadanubandhakagrahaṇe atadanubandhakagrahaṇaṃ na' iti paribhāṣayoḥ jñāpakam | jhrjñā.ṭa prāgdīvyato'ṇ (sū.4-1-83)| ayameva vikṛtanirdeśo jñāpayati--"ekadeśavikṛtamananyava" diti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhapīlāyā vā' (sū.4-1-118) iti vāgrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ "nāpavādaviṣye'ṇ bhavati' iti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaprāgdīvyato'ṇa' (sū.4-1-83) iti sūtre kiṃ prāgdīvyato yāḥ prakṛtayaḥ tābhyaḥ aṇ iti vivakṣitam, āhosvit prāgdīvyato ye'rthāsteṣvaṇ iti vivakṣitam | ādyapakṣe apavādabhūta - iñādiviṣayepyaṇprasaṅgaḥ | dvayorekaviṣayatvena vikalpa eva syānnabādhyabādhakabhāva iti | dvitīyapakṣe artheṣvidamaṇvidhānamiti prakṛtyantare sāvakāśo'ṇpratyayaḥ ekasminnarthe prakṛtiviśeṣādutpadyamāneneñādinā bādhyeteti na doṣo bhavati | tatra prathamapakṣe'pi doṣo nāstīti sādhayitumāha--pīlāyā veti vāgrahaṇamiti | yadyapavādaviṣaye'pyaṇ bhavet, tarhi "pīlayā vā' iti sūtre vāgrahaṇamanarthakam | pīlāśabdāt apatyārthe "dvyacaḥ' (sū.4-1-121) iti aṇapavāde ḍhaki prāpte tadapavādatayā punaraṇ vikalpena vidhīyate "pīlāyā vā' iti sūtreṇa | yadyapavādaviṣayeṣvapyaṇ syāt vāgrahaṇaṃ athavā etatsūtramapyantareṇa aṇḍhakoḥ vikalpena samāveśasiddhyā sūtraṃ vyarthaṃ bhavediti apavādaviṣaye aṇ na bhavatīti jñāpyate | ataḥ iñādiviṣaye aṇo na prasaktiriti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa prāk ṭhakaḥ iti vaktavye "prāgdīvyataḥ' (sū.4-1-83) ityuktirjñāpayati artheṣvayamiti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "prāgdīvyataḥ' ityasya sthāne "prākṭhakaḥ' ityeva lāghavāt kuto noktam | ṭhakśabdena ca "prāgvahateṣṭhak' (sū.4-4-1) iti sūtraṃ parāmṛśyate ityāśaṅkya--prāgdīvyato ye'rthāḥ teṣvaṇ bhavatīti jñāpanārthaṃ prāgdīvyata ityuktam | tena "prāgdīvyataḥ' (sū.4-4-2) iti sūtrapūrvabhāviṭhagarthe'pyaṇ bhavatīti labhyate | tatra ca prāk ṭhaka ityukte ṭhagviṣaye aṇ na prāpnuyāditi prāgdīvyata ityuktiścaritārthā | evaṃ jñāpanasya prayojanaṃ tu samāne'rthe ekasyāḥ prakṛterdvayoḥ aṇañpratyayoḥ prasaktau prakṛtyantare sāvakāśasyāṇaḥ iñ bādhaka iti
bādhyabādhakabhāvo nirṇīto bhavatīti ityāśayavānāha--prāk ṭhaka iti vaktavya iti | ayamiti | aṇityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa yūni luk (sū.4-1-90)|| "gotrokṣoṣṭra' (sū.4-2-39) iti vuñūvidhau rājanyamanuṣyagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ laukikaṃ paraṃ gotramiti | yuvā ca loke gotramityupacaryate | kiṃ gotro'si māṇavaka ! gārgyāyaṇaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhayūni luk' iti sūtrasya prāgdīvyatīye'jādau pratyaye vivakṣite yuvapratyayasya lugbhavati, tasminnivṛtte sati yo yataḥ prāpnoti sa tato bhavatītyarthaḥ | tasya prayojanaparigaṇanavelāyāṃ "iñṇyau sarvatra' iti vārtiṃkaṃ pravṛttam | iñaḥ udāharaṇam aupagavīyāḥ ita | atra upagorapatyamityaṇ aupagavaḥ, tataḥ yuvāpatye iñi aupagaviḥ, tataḥ chapratyaye vivakṣite ādau iñaḥ yuvapratyayasya luk, tasmin lupte chapratyayaḥ aupagavīyāḥ iti sidhyati | atra lukaḥ kiṃ prayojanam ? iñyalupte'pi vṛddhalakṣaṇe che aupagavīyā iti rūpasiddherityāśaṅkāyāṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa--"vṛddhavadatideśe sati iño gotra ityaṇ prāpnoti' iti proktam | tasyāyamarthaḥ---"prāgdīvyato'dhikāre yūno vṛddhavadatideśaḥ' iti vārtikena yuvapratyayasya gotravatkāryātideśāt iño lugabhāve "iño gotre'(sū.4-2-112) ityaṇ prapnuyāt | asmābhistu chapratyaya iṣyate | ataḥ iño lugvaktavyaḥ iti | tadupari "naiṣa doṣaḥ, pratyākhyāyate'sāvatideśaḥ ' iti bhāṣyam | tathā ca yūno gotravadatideśābāvāt iñaḥ lugabhāve'pi "iño gotre' ityaṇ na bhaviṣyati, api tu vṛddhalakṣaṇaścha eva bhaviṣyatītīṣṭasiddheḥ "yūni luk' iti lukaḥ aupagavīyā ityetadudāharaṇaṃ na bhavitumarhatīti bhāṣyāśayaḥ | tataḥ yadi yūno vṛddhavadatideśaḥ (gotravadatideśaḥ) pratyākhyāyate tadā gotravadatideśasya yāni prayojanānyuktāni teṣāṃ kaṃtha siddhiḥ iti śaṅkāyāṃ--"tāni jñāpakena siddhāni' iti bhāṣyakṛtā abhihitam | ayamatrāśayaḥ--gārgyāṇānāṃ samūhaḥ gārgyāyaṇakam, gārgyāyaṇakānāṃ kiṃcidgārgyāyaṇakam, gārgyāyaṇo bhaktirasya gārgyāyaṇakaḥ, gotraśrayo vuñ yathā syāditi yūno vṛddhavadatideśasya prayojanānyuktāni | tatra prathame udāharaṇe gargasyāpatyaṃ gotraṃ gārgyaḥ gargādibhyo yañ | gārgyāsyāpatyaṃ yuvā gārgyāyaṇaḥ "yañiñośca' (sū.4-1-101)iti phak | tataḥ samūhārthe "gotrokṣoṣṭrorabhra' (sū.4-1-39) iti vuñ, gārgyāṇakam iti rūpaṃ bhavati | atra phakaḥ yuvapratyayasya gotravadbhāve satyeva tataḥ "gotrokṣoṣṭrorabhra' iti sūtreṇa samūhorthe vṛñ bhavet iti | etacca atideśasya prayojanaṃ jñāpakena sādhayitvā atideśaḥ pratyākhyātaḥ | tathā hi--"gotrokṣoṣṭrorabhrarājarājanyarājaputravatsamanuṣyājādvuñ' (4-2-39) iti sūtre rājanyamanuṣyaśabdau śāstrīyagotrābhidhāyinau | "rājaśvaśurādyat' (sū.4-1-161) iti sūtreṇa yatpratyaye manuṣyaśabdaḥ sidhyati | tatra "gotrokṣoṣṭro' iti sūtre gotragrahaṇenaiva rājanyamanuṣyābhyāṃ vuñaḥ siddheḥ kiṃ pṛthak rājanyamanuṣyagrahaṇena | gṛhītau tu jñāpayataḥ apatyadhikārāduttaraṃ gotragrahaṇaṃ laukikagotraparamiti | laukikaṃ ca gotramapatyamātram | kiṃgotro'si māṇavaka! gārgyāyaṇa ityādau yuvāpatye'pi gotraśabdasya loke prayogāt | rājanyamanuṣyaśabdau ca laukike prayoge jātiviśeṣavācinau laukikagotrābhidhāyinau na bhavata iti gotragrahaṇena grahītumaśakyatvāt tayoḥ pṛthaggrahaṇam | itthaṃ ca gārgyāyaṇaśabdasya yuvāpatyārthakaphakpratyayāntasyāpi laukikagotrārthakatvāt gotravadbhāvātideśaṃvināpi gotragrahaṇena grahaṇāt tataḥ samūhārthe "gotrokṣo' iti sūtreṇa vuñaḥ siddhatvāt atideśaḥ pratyākhyāyata iti | tadetat sarvaṃ manasikṛtyāha--iti vuñvidhāvityādinā | paramiti | apatyādhikārāduttaramityarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa anṛṣyānantarye (sū.4-1-104)|| ṛṣāvanantare
netyarthaḥ | gotra ityadhikārāt siddhe yadimaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ śāsti tajjñāpayati bidādiṣu ye'nṛṣayaḥ (kathyante)jhrpaṭhyanteṭa teṣāmanantare'pi vṛttirbhavatīti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "anṛṣyānantarye bidādibhyo'ñ' (sū.4-1-104) iti yathāśrutasūtrāt bidādibhyo'patyārthe'ñpratyayo bhavati | ṛṣīṇāmānantarya avyavadhāne tu na bhavatīti arthaḥ pratīyate | tathā sati kuśikasya gotrāpatyamityartha kuśikaśabdādañi kauśika iti rūpamiṣyate, tannasidhyati | kuśiko'pi ṛṣiḥ tasya putraḥ gādhirapi ṛṣiḥ tasya putraḥ viśvāmitroṃ'pi ṛṣiḥ iti ṛṣīṇāmavyavadhānasya sadbhāvena anṛṣyānantartha iti niṣedhādityāśaṅkyāha--ṛṣāvanantare neti | anantaramevānantaryam | ṛṣāvityavadheradhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī | tathā ca ṛṣibhyo'nantarāpatye añpratyayo na bhavatīti anṛṣyānantarye ityasyārthaḥ | kuśikaśabdādgotrāpatye año na ko'pyuparodha iti bhāvaḥ | nanu "gotre kuñjādibhyaḥ ścaphañ' ( sū.4-1-98) iti sūtrāt gotre ityasyāsmin sūtre'nuvṛttyā gotrāpatya evedaṃ sūtraṃ pravartate | tatrānantarāpatye prasaktyabhāvāt anṛṣyānantarya iti vacanamanarthakamityāśaṅkyāha-- gotra ityadhikārāditi | bidādigaṇe ṛṣayaḥ anṛṣayaśca paṭhyante | tatrānṛṣibhyaḥ anantarāpatye'pyañ bhavatīti jñāpanārthamanṛṣyānantarya ityuktam | tena nanānduranantarāpatyamityarthe nānāndra iti añ sidhyati | evaṃ pautraḥ dauhatra ityapi sidhyati | bidādigaṇapaṭhitebhyaḥ ṛṣivācakaśabdebhyastu gotrāpatya evāñ ita viṣayavyavasthā labhyata iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa āragudīcām (sū.4-1-230)| rakā siddhe āragvacanam anyato'pyayamiti jñāpayati | jhrvi.ṭa nanu godhāśabdāt "godhāyā ḍhrak' (sū.4-1-129) iti sūtreṇa apatyarthe ḍhrakaṃ vidhāya "āragudīcām' (sū.4-1-130) iti sūtreṇa udīcāṃ mate āragvihitaḥ | gaudhāra iti rūpam | atra godhāśabdasyākārāntatvāt tataḥ rakpratyayenāpi gaudhāra iti rūpasiddhaḥ ārakpratyayavidhānamanarthakamityāśaṅkyāha--rakā siddha iti | anākārāntebhyo'pi śabdebhyaḥ āragiṣyate yathā mauṇḍaḍḍatdh;āraḥ jāṇḍaḍḍatdh;āra iti | rakpratyayāntatayā tu te na sidhyantītyāragvacanamiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjña.ṭa ataśca (sū.4-1-177)|| ata iti tadantāgrahaṇam, avantyādibhyo lugvacanāt | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "ataśca' iti sūtre ityasya tadrājaviśeṣaṇatvāttadantavidhau satyāṃ akārāntasya tadrājasaṃjñakapratyayasya striyāṃ lugbhavatītyartho labhyate | tataśca āmbaṣṭhasyāpatyaṃ sauvīrasyāpatyamityarthe janapadakṣatriyobhayavācinaḥ āmbaṣṭhaśabdāt sauvīraśabdācca "vṛddhetkosalājādāññyaṅ' (sū.4-1-169) iti sūtreṇa ñyaṅi akārāntatvena āmbaṣṭhyā sauvīryā ityubhayatrāpi "ataśca' (sū.4-1-177) iti luk prāpnotītyāśaṅkyāha--ata iti tadantāgrahaṇamiti | tathā ca "ataśca' iti sutre tadantavidhirna bhavati | akārarūpasya tadrājasya striyāṃ luk ityeva sūtrārthaḥ | śūrasenī ityādikamudāharaṇam | śūrasenaśabdādañi tasya lugbhavati | ñyaṅstu akārantatve'pi akārarūpatvābhāvānna lugiti bhāvaḥ | tadantagrahaṇaṃ na bhavatītyatra jñāpakamāha--avantyādibhyo lugvacanāditi | "striyāmavantikuntikurubhyaśca' (sū.4-1-174) iti sūtreṇa avantyādibhyaḥ parasya tadrājasya ñyaṅaḥ lugvidhīyate | yadi "ataśca' iti sūtre tadantavidhinā akārāntasya tadrājasyāpi lugvidhīyeta tadā avantyādibhyaḥ parasya ñyaṅo'pi "ataśca' ityenenaiva luki siddhe "striyāmavanti' iti sūtraṃ kimartham | kṛtaṃ ca tatsūtraṃ "ataśca' iti sūtre tadantavidhirnāstīti jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhana prācyabhargādiyaudheyādibhyaḥ'
(sū.4-1-178) iti pratiṣedho jñāpako bhavati parśvādibhyo lugiti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaataśca' (sū.4-1-177) iti sūtre "parśvādibhyo lugvaktavyaḥ'| "parśūḥ' iti bhāṣyam | tasyāyamarthaḥ parśorapatyaṃ strītyarthe parśuśabdāt "dhdyañmagadha' (sū.4-1-170) iti sūtreṇāṇ | tasya "ataśca' (sū.4-1-177) iti luk | tataḥ "parśvādiyaudheyādibhyo'ṇañau' (sū.5-3-117) iti svārthe'ṇ | tasya ca bhinnaprakaraṇasthatvāt aprāpto luganena bhāṣyeṇa vidhīyate | tataḥ "ūṅutaḥ' ityūṅ parśūriti rūpasiddhiriti | idaṃ vacanaṃ jñāpakenaiva luksiddheḥ anāvaśyakamityāśayenha--na prācyetyādinā | yaudheyādigaṇapaṭhitebhyaḥ śabdebhyaḥ parasya akārasya tadrājasya luk pratiṣidhyate | "na prācyabhargādiyaudheyādibhyaḥ' (4-1-178) iti sūtreṇa | yaudheyītyudāharaṇam | yudhāyā apatyaṃ strītyarthe "dvyacaḥ' (sū.4-1-121) iti ḍhak | tataḥ svārthe'ñ (5-3-117) tasya luki pratiṣiddhe añantalakṣaṇe ṅīni "śārṅgaravādyaño ṅīn ' (sū.4-1-73) ityanena kṛte yaudheyīti bhavati | yadi pāñcamikasyāpi tadrājasya "ataśca' iti luṅ na syāt tadā prasaktyabhāvāt yaudheyādibhyo vihitasyāño lukpratiṣeghavidhānamanarthakaṃ syāditi sa pratiṣedhaḥ parśvādibhyaḥ parasya akārasya lugbhavatīti jñāpayatītyarthaḥ | nanu yaudheyaśabdāt ḍhakpratyayāntāt añaṃ vināpi "ṭiḍḍḍatdh;ḍhāṇañ' (sū.4-1-15) iti ṅīpi yaudheyīti rūpasiddheḥ atra añaḥ kā āvaśyakatā iti cenna | ṅīpi sati udāttanivṛttisvareṇāntodāttaṃ padam, ṅīni tvādyudāttamiti svare bhedasadbhāvādityāśayāt | || iti caturthādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ || jhrjñā.ṭa tadasminnastīti deśe (sū.4-2-67)|| "tadasyāstyasmin' (sū.5-2-94) iti dvistadgrahaṇākāraṇāt kkacidekayogo'pyanuvṛttirbhavatīti jñāpyate | "tadadhīte tadveda' (sū.4-2-59) iti tu pramādakṛtamācāryasya śakyamakartum | jhrvi.ṭa nanu cāturarthikaprakaraṇasyādau "tadasminnastīti deśe tannāmni' (sū.4-2-67) "tena nirvṛttam' (sū.4-2-68) "tasya nivāsaḥ' (sū.4-2-69) "adūrabhavaśca' (sū 4-2-70) iti catvāri sūtrāṇi arthacatuṣṭayapratipādakāni paṭhyante | tatra kimimāni catvārthapi militvāekaṃ sūtram uta pṛthak pṛthak imāni catvāri sūtrāṇi iti kaṃcidvicāraṃ prastutavān bhāṣyakāraḥ | "deśe tannāmnī' tyasya "tadasminnastīti' "tena niṃrvṛttaṃ' "tasya nivāsaḥ' "adūrabhavaśca' iti caturṣvāpi saṃbandho bhavati | tadasminnastītyarthe aṇādayo bhavanti pratyayāntena prakṛtināmake deśe gamye, tena nirvṛttamityarthe tṛtīyāntādaṇādayo bhavanti tannāmni deśe gamye, tasya nivāsa ityartha ṣaṣṭhayantādaṇādayo bhavanti tannāmni deśe gamye, tasya adūrabhava ityarthe ṣaṣṭhyantādaṇādayo bhavanti tannāmni deśe gamye iti krameṇa caturṇāmarthaḥ | udumbarāḥ santyasmin deśe audumbaraḥ, kuśāmbena nirvṛttā kauśāmbī nagarī, śibīnāṃ nivāso deśaḥ śaibaḥ, vidiśāyāḥ adūrabhavaṃ nagaraṃ vaidiśam ityudāharaṇāni | tatra caturṇāṃ milātvā ekasūtratvamiti pakṣe "deśe tannāmni' ityasya caturṣvavayaveṣu saṃbandho na syāt | na hyekayogaghaṭakasyāvayavasya taddhaṭakāvayavāntare'nuvṛttiḥ saṃbhavati | tathā sati "tadadhīte tadveda' (sū.4-2-59) iti sutre dvitīyatacchabdasya vyarthatvāt | prathamatatpadasyaiva veda ityatrāpyanuvṛttyā iṣṭalābhāt ityāśaṅkyāha--tadasyāstyasminnitīti | ayamāśayaḥ | "tadasyāstya sminnati matup' (5-2-94) iti sūtre "tat' ityasya asminnityatrāpyanuvṛttiṃ svīkṛtya tadasminnityarthe matup iti vyākhyānamiṣyate | atra ekasminneva sūtre taddhaṭakabhāgāntare asminnityatra tad ityasyānuvṛttiriṣyate | ekayoge'nuvṛttyabhāve hi tadasyāsti tadasminnimi matup iti dvistadgrahaṇaṃ kuryāt | yatasyathā
na karotiṃ ato jñāpayati ekasūtre'pyastyanuvṛttiriti | nanvevaṃ sati "tadadhīte tadveda' (4-2-59) iti sūtre dvitīyatatpadaṃ vyarthamityāśakyeṣṭāttirityāha--tadadhīte iti | pramādakṛtamiti | śiṣyāṇāṃ sukhāvaboghāya lāghavaṃ pratyanavadhānalakṣaṇena pramādena kṛtamityarthaḥ iti kaiyaṭaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa (kartrādibhyo) jhrkattryādibhyoṭa (sū.4-1-95)|| atra grāmādityanurvacya grāmācceti vaktavyamiti pratyākhyātam | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhakatryādibhyo ḍhakañ' iti sūtre "grāmācceti vaktavyam | grāmeyaka' iti vārtikam | etatpratyākhyātaṃ bhāṣye--"tattarhi vaktavyam | na vaktavyam | kattryādibhyo ḍhakañityatra grāmādityetadanuvarttiṣyate' iti | tadāha--atra grāmādityanurvatyeti| "grāmādyakhañau' (sū.4-2-94) iti sūtrāditi śeṣaḥ | || iti caturthādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ || jhrjñā.ṭa vibhāṣā pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇa (sū.4-3-24)|| "ghakālataneṣu' (sū.6-3-17) ityalugvacanaṃ jñāpayati subantāttaddhitotpattiḥ iti | yadyapi sārmathyaṃ subantenaiva tathāpi prātipadikādhikārāt tata eva saṃbhāvyetetyato jñāpakasādhyametat | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhavibhāṣā pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāparaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇābhyām' iti sūtreṇa pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāparāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇaśabdābhyāṃ vibhāṣā ṭyuṭyulau pratyayau tayostuḍaḍḍatdh;āgamaśca vidhīyante | pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇetanam aparāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇetanam ityudāharaṇam | atra pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāparāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇābhyāṃ saptamīśravaṇārthaṃ tayoḥ sūbantatvaṃ vaktavyamiti vārtikakāraḥ akathayat | tasya ca"ṅyāpprātipadikāt' (sū.4-1-1) ityadhikārāt prātipādikādeva taddhita ityāśayaḥ | bhāṣyakārastu subantāt taddhitotpattiṃ jñāpakena prasādhya vārtikaṃ pratyācakhyau | tadāha--ghakālataneṣviti | "dhakālataneṣu kālanāmnaḥ' (sū.6-3-17) iti sūtreṣakālavācakaśabdāt parasyāḥ saptamyāḥ ghakālaneṣu parataḥ alugvacanāt sūbantāt taddhitotpattiriti jñāpyate | kathamanyathā saptamyā evābhāvāt lukaḥ aluko vā prasaktiḥ iti bhāvaḥ | nanu na jñāpakasādhyametat | "samarthānāṃ prathamā' dityadhikāre taddhitavidhānāt "kiṃ punaḥ samartham arthābhidhāne yacchaktaṃ kṛtavarṇānupūrvīkaṃ padam ' iti samarthasūtre bhāṣyoktyā sūbantapadādeva taddhitotpattilābhādityatrāha--yadyapīti | tathāpītyādi | ṅyāpprātipadikādityapyanuvartate taddhitaprakaraṇe | atastadanuguṇatayā samarthaśabdārtho vaktavyaḥ | sa ca yatad supi sati arthabodhe śaktaṃ tatsamarthamiti | tathā ca supi sati arkhabodhe śaktāt prathamāt prātipadikāt taddhita ityarthaḥ | tataśca prātipadikābhyāṃ pūrvāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇāparāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍaṇābhyāṃ ṭyuṭyuloḥ satoḥ saptamīśravaṇaṃ na syāditi pūrvoktajñāpakamahimnā subantāt taddhitotpattiṃ prasādhya "ghakālataneṣu' ityanena alukā saptamīśravaṇaṃ samarthanīyamiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa tasya vyākhyāna (sū.4-3-66)|| vyākhyānaṃ śabdagranthaviṣaya eva prasiddhamiti bhāṣye | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "tasya vyākhyāna iti ca vyākhyātavyanāmnaḥ' iti sūtreṇa vyākhyātavyavācinaḥ ṣaṣṭhayantāt vyākhyāne'bhidheye yathāprāptaṃ taddhito bhavatīti kathanāt supāṃ vyākhyānaḥ saupo grantha ityādāviva pāṭaliputrasya vyākhyānī sukosalā ityādāvapi taddhitaḥ syāt |yādṛśaḥ sukośalāyāṃ prākāradisanniveśaḥ tādṛśaḥ pāṭaliputre ityevaṃ pāṭaliputraṃ sukosalayā vyākhyāyata iti sūtrapravṛttiyogyatāyāḥ saddhāvādityāśaṅkya, vyākhyātavyanāmna ityatra nāmagrahaṇāt vyākhyātavyatvena prasiddhasya śabdātmakasya granthasya yannāma tata eva taddhatiḥ ato noktadoṣa ityāśayavānāha--vyākhyānaṃ śabdagranthaviṣaya eveti | śabdātmako yo granthaḥ tadviṣaya evetyarthaḥ | evakāreṇa aśabdātmakapāṭaliputrādeḥ vyavacchedaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa
vāsudevārjunā (sū.4-3-98)|| ayameva nirdeśo jñāpayati abhyarhitaṃ pūrvamiti | vāsudeveti ca tatra bhagavataḥ saṃjñā | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "vāsudevārjunābhyāṃ vun' iti sūtre arjunavāsudevābhyāmiti nirdeṣṭavyam | arjunaśabdasya vāsudevaśabdāpekṣayā alpāctaratvena "alpāctaram' (sū.2-2-34) iti sūtreṇa pūrvanipātaprāpterityāśaṅkyāha--ayameveti | na kevalam alpāctaraṃ pūrvaṃ prayujyate kiṃtu baḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavaco'pi abhyarhitasya pūrvaṃ prayogo bhavatīti jñāpanārthamevaṃ nirdeśaḥ | arjunavāsudevayormadhye vāsudevo'bhyarhitaḥ, tasya paramātmatvāt | ataḥ bhagavadrūpaśreṣṭhavācino vāsudevaśabdasya pūrvanipāto yukta iti bhāvaḥ tena tāpasagirī ityādi sidhyati | jhrjñā.ṭa anudāttādeśca (sū.4-3-140)|| bhikṣādiṣu garbhiṇīśabdapāṭho jñāpayati yāvatyeva dvitīyasvarasya prādurbhāvastāvatyeva pūrvanidhātaḥ (pratīkṣeta) jhrpravartetaṭa tarhi garbhaśabdādādyudāttādini tato ṅīpi tadantādaṇeva prāpnoti nānudāttalakṣaṇo'ñiti taddhaiyarthyaṃ spaṣṭameva | padagrahaṇaṃ parimāṇārtham vākyasya mā bhūditi | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaanudāttādeśca' iti sutrasya anudāttādeḥ prātipadikāt vikārāvayavayorarthayorañpratyayo bhavatītyarthaḥ | dādhitthaṃ kāpitthamityādikamudāharaṇam | tatra ca sūtre "anudāttāderaño vidhāne ādyudāttānṅīṣa upasaṃkhyānam ' iti vārtikam | tasya cāyamarthaḥ--ādyudāttaprakṛtikaṅīṣantādapyañ bhavati vikārāvayavayorarthayoriti vaktavyamiti | kuvalyā vikāraḥ kauvalamityudāharaṇam, kuvalaśabdo "grāmādīnāṃ ca' (phiṭsūtra) ityādyudāttaḥ tataḥ gaurāditvānṅīṣ | tataḥ vikāre añ | nanu ādyudāttātkuvalaśabdānṅīṣi ṅīṣaḥ pratyayasvarema udāttatbe śeṣanighātena kuvalīśabdasyānudāttatvaṃ vartata iti sūtreṇaivātra añaḥ siddheḥ vārtikasya kā āvasyakateti cenna | ṅīṣaḥ udāttatve'pi śeṣanidhātasyātrāpravṛtteḥ | "anudāttaṃ padamekavarjam' (sū.3-1-158) iti hi, atra śeṣasyānudātto vaktavyaḥ | tacca sūtraṃ padaviṣayam | kuvalīśabdaśca na padam, sūbantatvābhāvāt ityāśayāt | tadupari "na vā samarthasyānudāttāditvāt ' iti vārtikam | tasyacāyamarthaḥ--"anudāttādeḥ' ityetat na ṅyāpprātipadikāt ityasya viśeṣaṇam | yena kuvalīśabdasya ṅyantasya pūrvektarītyā anudāttāditvaṃ nāstīti kathaṃ tataḥ añ syāditi śaṅkāyā avasaro bhavet | apa tu samarthasya viśeṣaṇam | samartha ca subantameva | tataśca anudāttādeḥ subantāt añ iti sūtrārthaḥ | ataḥ kuvalīśabdāt subutpattyanantaraṃ subantādañ | subantasya ca padatvāt "anudāttaṃ padamekavarjam' iti sūtrapravṛttyā īkāra ekaḥ udāttaḥ anyatsarvamanudāttamiti kuvalī ṅas ityasya anudāttāditvāsadbhāvāt tataḥ añi kauvala iti rūpaṃ sidhyati sūtrarītyaiveti | bhāṣyakārastu anudāttāderityetat ṅyāpprātipadikādityasyaiva viśeṣaṇam, na subantādityasya | tathā sati vāco vikāraḥ ityatrāpi "sāvekācaḥ' (sū.6-1-186) iti vibhakterudāttatayā sūbantasyānudāttaditvādañ prāpnuyāt | tarhi kuvalī iti ṅyantasya padatvābhāvenaśepanidhātāprāptyā anudāttāditavā bhāvāt kathaṃ tataḥ añ sidhyediti śaṅkāyāṃ evaṃ samādhīyate | kasmiṃścit śabde yāvatyeva dvitīyasya svarasya prādurbhāvaḥ tāvatyeva pūrvapravṛttasya svarasya sthāne anudātto bhavatītyāśrīyate | kuvalaśabdaḥ "grāmādīnāṃ ca' iti ādyudāttaḥ | tataḥ ṅīṣi īkārasya udāttaḥ | asmin dvitīṃye svare prādurbhūte pūrvasyādyudāttasya nivṛttiḥ anudāttaśca bhavati | tathā ca kuvalīśabdasyānudāttāditvāt sūtreṇaiva añ sidhyati | yāvatyeva dvitīyasya svarasyetyādyāśrayaṇe kiṃ pramāṇamiti ceta-- bhikṣādaṣiu gārbhiṇīśabdapāṭha iti gṛhāṇa | garbhiṇīnāṃ samūha ityarthe
"anudāttāderañ' (sū.4-2-44) ityañi prāpte tadapavādatayā aṇsiddhyarthaṃ bhikṣādigaṇe (sū.4-2-38) garbhiṇīśabdaḥ paṭhyate | yadi padasya nighātaḥ tarhi tatpāṭho vyarthaḥ | tathā hi harbhaśabdaḥ "artigṝbhyāṃ man' (u.sū. ) ityuṇādisūtreṇa mannantatvāt ādyudāttaḥ | tataḥ in | tataḥ nāntalakṣaṇaḥ ṅīp | garbhiṇī iti ṅyantasya sūbantatvābhāvena padatvabhāvāt "anudāttaṃ padam ' iti sūtrapravṛttau ādyudāttatvaṃ garbhiṇībhabdasya nāpaitīti "anudāttāderañ' iti sūtrāpravṛttyā kiṃbhikṣādiṣu garbhiṇīśabdasya añbādhanārthapāṭhena | kṛtaśca pāṭhaḥ jñāpayati yāvatyeva dvitīyasya svarasaya prādurbhāvaḥ tadaiva pūrvapravṛttasvaraḥ nighāto bhavatīti | tathā ca insvarakāle pūrvapravṛttaḥ ādyudāttaḥ nighāto bhavatīti anudāttāditvalakṣaṇāñprāptau tadbādhanāya garbhiṇīśabdasya bhikṣādigaṇe pāṭhaḥ caritārthaḥ | prayojanaṃ tu kauvalaityādāvañsiddharata pratyapādayat | sarvametanmanasikṛtyāha--bhikṣādiṣviti | yāvatyeveti yadaivetyarthaḥ | tāvatyeveti | tadaivetyarthaḥ | tarhīti | anyathetyarthaḥ | anudāttalakṣaṇo'ñiti | "anudāttāderañ' (sū.4-2-44) iti sūtreṇeti śeṣaḥ | tarhi "anudāttaṃ padamekavarjam' (sū.6-1-158) iti sūtre padagrahaṇamanarthakam | subanatpatteḥ pūrvamapi dvitīyasvaraprāptikāle śeṣanidhātasyopapāditatvādityata āha--padagrahaṇamiti | parimāṇārthamiti | yasya padatvaṃ bhaviṣyati tasyaiva ekavarjamanudāttaṃ yathā syāt padasamūhasya mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ padagrahaṇamiti bhāvaḥ || iti caturtho'dhyāyaḥ |

|| atha pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
jhrjñā.ṭa prākkrītācchaḥ (sū.5-1-1)|| "asamāse niṣkādibhyaḥ' (sū.5-1-20) | niṣkādiṣvasamāsagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ pūrvatra tadantavidheḥ pratiṣedho neti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "asamāse niṣkādibhyaḥ' iti sūtre asamāsagrahaṇaṃ vyartham | na ca tadabhāve paramaniṣkaśabdātsamāsādapyanena ṭhak syāt , ṭhañeveṣyate | tathā ca paramaniṣkaśabdavyāvartanārthaṃ asamāsagrahaṇamiti vācyam | grahaṇavatā pratipadikena tadantavidhaḥ pratiṣedhāt niṣkādibhyo vidhīyamānaṣṭhak niṣkādyantānna bhaviṣya tīti asamāsagrahaṇamantareṇāpi paramaniṣkadivyāvṛttisiddherityāśaṅkya, etatsūtrātprāktaneṣu sūtreṣu prātipadikagrahaṇena tadantadasyāpi grahaṇamiti jñāpanārtham asamāsagrahaṇam | tathā ca tannyāyenātra sūtre'pi tadantavidhiprāptau paramaniṣkādibhya'pi ṭhak syāditi tadvāraṇārthamasamāsagrahaṇaṃ sārthakam | jñāpanaprayojanam tu "khalayavamāṣatilavṛṣabrahmaṇaśca' (sū.5-1-7) iti sutreṇa khalādibhyo vidhīyamānaḥ hitārthe yatpratyayaḥ khalādyantādapi bhavati | tena kṛṣṇatilebhyo hitaḥ kṛṣṇatilyaḥ rājamāṣebhyo hitaṃ rājamāṣyamityādikaṃ sidhyati | sarvamidamabhipretyāha--niṣkādiṣviti | jhrjñā.ṭa viṃśatitriṃśadbhyām (sū.5-1-24)|| viṃśatikātkhaḥ (sū.5-1-32) iti kapratyayāntoccāraṇaṃ jñāpayati bhavatyetatsūtropāttābhyāṃ kanniti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "viṃśatitriṃśadbhyāṃ ḍḍḍatdh;vunnasaṃjñāyām' itā sūtreṇa ārhīyeṣvartheṣu viṃśatitriṃśadbhyāṃ ḍḍḍatdh;vunpratyayo vidhīyate | viṃśakaḥ triṃśakaḥ ityudāharaṇam | tatrāsaṃjñāyāmityanuktau saṃjñāyāṃ viṣaye'pi ḍḍḍatdh;vun bhavet, kaneva tu tatra viṃśatikaḥ triṃśatkaḥityevamiṣyate | ataḥ asaṃjñāyāmityuktamiti vaktavyam | paraṃ tu pratyudāharaṇe viṃśatikaḥ triṃśatkaḥ ityubhayatrāpi kan durlabhaḥ | "saṃkhyāyā atiśadantāyāḥkan' (sū.5-1-22) iti sūtre tyantāyāḥ śadantāyāśca kanpratiṣedhādityāśaṅkya,"viṃśatikātkhaḥ' iti sūtre kanpratyayāntatayā uccāraṇena viṃśatitriṃśadbhyāṃ kan bhavatiti jñāpanānna doṣa ityāha--kapratyayāntoccāraṇamiti | na ca viṃśatikaśabda eva sūtrakāreṇoccāritaḥ | tena ca viṃśatikaśabdasya sādhutvāvagame'pi triṃśatkaśabdasya kathaṃ sādhutvamavagamyeta iti vācyam | viṃśatikaśabdena viṃśatiśabdaghaṭitasūtropalakṣaṇena tatsūtropāttābhyāṃ viṃśatitriṃśadbhyāmubābhyāmapi kanpratyayasya jñāpanādityāśayaḥ | yogāpekṣatvāt jñāpakatvasyeti yāvat | jhrjñā.ṭa tatra tasyeva (sū.5-2-116)|| asmādeva jñāpakādivaśabdena saptamī bhavati | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "tatra tasyeva' iti sūtreṇa saptamyantāt ṣaṣṭyantācca ivārthe vatipratyayo vidhīyate | mathurāyāmiva mathurāvat strughre prākāraḥ, devadattasyeva devadattavat yajñadattasya gāvaḥ ityādikamudāharaṇam | tatrevaśabaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍadayoge sambandhasāmānye ṣaṣṭhyāḥ sbhave'pi ivayoge kenāpi sūtreṇa saptamyāḥ avidhānena saptamyantādivārthe vatividhānaṃ kathaṃ saṅgacchata ityāśaṅkya, asmādeva ivārthe saptamyantādvatividhānāt jñāpakāt ivayoge saptamīvibhaktirbhavatīti jñāpanāt na ko'pi doṣa iti samādhatte--asmādeveti | ivaśabdeneti | yoga iti śeṣaḥ| jñāpanasya phalaṃ tu deveṣviva nāma brāhmaṇeṣviva nāma ityādiprayoga upapanno bhavatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa kimidaṃbhyām (sū.5-2-40)|| etadeva jñāpayati kimidaṃbyāṃ vatubiti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "kimidaṃbhyāṃ ve ghaḥ' iti sūtraṃ kimidaṃbhyāṃ parasya vatupo vakārasya ghatvaṃ vidhatte | kiyān iyān ityudāharaṇam | tatra kimidaṃbyāṃ vatupo durlabhatvāt dhatvavidhānamasaṃgatam | "yattadetebyaḥ parimāṇe vatup ' (sū.5-2-39) iti sūtre yattadetacchabdebya eva
vatupo vidhānādityā śaṅkyāha--etadeveti | kimidaṃbhyāṃ vatupo'bhāve tadavayavavakārasya ghatvavidhānamanupapannamiti ādeśavidhānānyathānupapacyā vatuppratyayaḥ kimidaṃbyāṃ bhavatītyanumīyata iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa (ṣaṣṭhāde) jhrṣaṣṭhyādeṭa ścāsaṃ (sū.5-2-58)|| asaṃkhyāderityetat jñāpayati (bhavati) hi jhrbhavatīhaṭa tadantavidhiriti | tenaikaviṃśatitamaḥ siddhaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu ṣaṣṭhyādeścāsaṃkhyādeḥ' iti sūtreṇāsaṃkhyādeḥ ṣaṣṭiprabhṛteḥ saṃkhyāvācakaśabdāt parasya ḍaḍḍatdh;ṭastamaḍaḍḍatdh;āgamo nityaṃ vidhīyate | ṣaṣṭitama ityādikamudāharaṇam | tatrāsaṃkhyāderityanuktau ekaṣaṣṭitamaḥ ityatrāpi nityaṃ tamaḍaḍḍatdh;āgamaḥ syāt, "viṃśatyādibhyastamaḍaḍḍatdh;nyatarasyāṃ' (sū.5-2-56) ityanena vikalpena tu tamaḍiḍḍatdh;ṣyate iti asaṃkhyāderityasya prayojanaṃ vaktavyam | tanna yuktam | grahaṇavatā prātipadikena tadantavidhipratiṣedhāt ṣaṣṭiśabdena ṣaṣṭiśabdāntasya ekaṣaṣṭiśabdasya grahaṇāsaṃbhavādeva tataḥ etatsūtrāpravṛtteḥ ityāśaṅkyāha-asaṃkhyāderatyietaditi | tathā ca pūrvoktarītyā vyarthaṃ sat asaṃkhyāderityetat iha tamaṭprakaraṇe tadantavidhirastīti jñāpayati | tataśca ekaṣaṣṭiśabdādapi tadantavidhinā anena sūtreṇa nityaṃ ḍaḍḍatdh;ṭastamaḍaḍḍatdh;āgamaprasaktau tadvāraṇārthaṃ asaṃkhyāderiti | ekaṣaṣṭiśabdasya saṃkhyāvācakaikaśabdādyavayavakatvenāsaṃkhyāditvaṃ nāstīti na doṣaḥ | jñāpanaphalavamāha-tenaikaviṃśatitamaḥ siddha iti | "viṃśatyādiṃbhyastamaḍaḍḍatdh;nyatarasyāṃ' (sū.5-2-56) iti sūtre tadantavidhinā viṃśatyādyantasyāpi grahaṇāt ekaṣaṣṭiśabdādapi tena ḍaḍḍatdh;ṭastamaṭi ekaṣaṣṭitama iti sidhyati | ḍaḍḍatdh;ṭastamaṭprakaraṇe tadantavidhyabhāve tu ṣaṣṭiśabdāntasya ekaṣaṣṭiśabdasya "viṃśatyādibhyaḥ' iti sūtre grahaṇābhāvāt ḍaḍḍatdh;ṭastamaṭ na syāt ekaṣaṣṭha ityeva rūpaṃ bhavediti bhāvaḥ | "sidhmādibhya' iti sutre "prāṇisthādāto lajanyatarasyāṃ' (sū.5-2-96) iti sūtrādanyatarasyāmityanuvartate | tathā ca sidhmādibyaḥ matvarthe'nyatarasyāṃ lac bhavatīti sūtrārtho labhyate | evaṃ sati bavataḥ iti sūtrārthaḥ | ataḥ iniṭhanoḥ na prasaktiḥ | samuccayārtho'yamanyatarasyāṃśabda ityatra kiṃ jñāpakamiti cet picchādīnāṃ tundādīnāṃ ca pṛthak sūtre grahaṇamiti grahāṇa| tathā hi-"tundādibhya ilacca' (5-2-117) ityekaṃ sūtram | tatra ca catvāra- pratyayāḥ iṣyante ilac iniṭhanau matup ceti | yadyanyatarasyāgrahaṇānuvṛttyā pratyayacatuṣṭayaṃ sidhmādibyo'pi bhavet tarhi asyaivānyatarasyāṃśabdasya "lomādipāmādipicchādibyaḥ śanelacaḥ' (sū.5-2-200) iti sūtre'pyanuvṛttyā piccādabhyio'pi catvāraḥ pratyayā bhaveyuḥ | tathā sati tundādīnāṃ picchādigaṇe pāṭhenaiva "tundādibya ilacca' iti pṛthaksūtramanarthakam | yadvā tundādigaṇe picchādīnāṃ pāṭhenaiva picchādibyaḥ "tundādibhyaḥ ilacca' iti sūtreṇa pratyayacatuṣṭayasiddau "lomādipāmādi' sūtre picchādigrahaṇamanarthakam | evaṃ cānayoḥ picchāditundādyoḥ pṛthakpāṭhaḥ pūthagyogakaraṇaṃ cāyukta miti "sidhmādibhyaśca' iti sūtre anyatarasyāṃśabdaḥ samuccayārtho na vikalpārtha iti jñāpayati | tathā ca prakṛtasya matupaḥ samuccayena pratyayadvayasiddhāvapi pratyayacatuṣṭayaṃ tundādibhyo na sidhyediti picchādibhyaḥ pṛthakkṛtya "tundādibya ilacca' iti sūtraṃ praṇītam | tasya ca iniṭhanbyāmanantaraṃ pravṛttatvāt cakāreṇa matup iniṭhanau ca samuccīyete iti pratyayacatuṣṭayasiddhiḥ | sidhmādipicchādisūtrayostu iniṭhanoḥ pūrvaṃ pāṭhāt samuccayārthakenānyatarasyāṃgrahaṇena pūrvakṛto matubeva samuccīyata iti tebhyaḥ pratyayadvayameveti vyavasthā sidhyati | vikalpārthakatvāpakṣe tu abhāvapakṣe pūrveṇa pareṇa vā vihitāḥ sarve'pi pratyāyā yathāprāptaṃ bhaveyuriti bhāvaḥ |"sidhmādibhyasca' (5-1-97) iti sūtre'nuvṛttaṃ anyatarasyāṃgrahaṇaṃ samuccayārthakamityatra jñāpakāntaramupanyasyati "keśādvaḥ' ityādinā | "kesādvo'nyatarasyām' (sū.5-1-109) iti || atha ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||
jhrjñā.ṭa dāśvān sāḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍavān (sū.6-1-12)-"abyāsasyāsavarṇe' (sū.6-4-78) ityasavarṇagrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati vārṇādāṅgaṃ balīya iti | na hyantareṇa guṇavṛddhī asavarṇaparo'bhyāso bhavati | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyamiti na vyākhyāyate | jhr jñā.ṭa na samprasāraṇe (sū.6-1-37)|| ayameva niṣedho jñāpayati (pūrvasya) jhrparasyaṭa na (parasyeti) jhrpūrvasyetiṭa | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "na samprasāraṇe samprasāraṇaṃ' iti sūtreṇa samprasāraṇe parataḥ pūrvasya yaṇaḥ samprasāraṇaṃ na bhavatītyucyate | yūna ityādikamudāharaṇam | yuvan śabdāt śasi yuvan as iti sthite vakārasya "śvayuvamaghonāmataddhite' (sū.6-4-133) iti sūtrema samprasāraṇe ukāre yuu an iti sthite "samprasāraṇācca' (sū.6-1-108) iti pūrvarūpe yu un iti sthite savarṇadīrghe yūnaḥ iti rūpaṃ sidhyati | tatra yakārasyāpi samprasāraṇe prāpte "na samprasāraṇe' iti sūtreṇa vakārasthānike ukārarūpe samprasāraṇe pare yakārasya saṃprasāraṇaṃ na bhavatīti niṣidhyate | tadetat tadā ghaṭate yadi yuvanśabde vakārasya pūrvaṃ samprasāraṇaṃ syāt | yadā tu ādau yakārasya samprasāraṇaṃ kriyate tadā samprasāraṇa parakatvābhāvānniṣedho na pravarteta | tathā ca ādau yakārasya samprasāraṇaṃ paścadvakārasyetyevamubhayoḥ samprasāraṇaṃ prasajyata ityāśaṅkyāha--ayameva niṣedho jñāpayatīti | yadi hi dvayoḥ samprasāraṇaṃ syāt niṣedhārambho vyartho bhavet | niṣedhamahimnā ca parasya yaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ samprasāraṇaṃ na pūrvasyeti jñāpayati | tataśca parasya samprasāraṇe kṛte pūrvasyāpi prāptau satyāṃ niṣedha ārabhyate iti niṣedhasya caritārthateti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa prathamayoḥ pūrva (sū.6-1-102)|| atra vibhaktyorgrahaṇe "tasmācchaso naḥ'(6-1-103) iti pūrvalasavarṇadīrghaparāmarśakaṃ tasmāditi jñāpakam | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaprathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' iti sūtre, prathamaśabdaḥ kkacit sanniveśe krameṇa sanniviṣṭānāmādibhūtaṃ bodhayati | "dīrghājjasi ca' (sū.6-1-105) iti liṅgāt sabātmakasanniveśaghaṭakānāmādibhūtayorityarthaḥ paryavasyati | tatrādibhūtayoḥ kayoḥ ? kiṃ vibhaktyoḥ ? uta pratyayayoḥ iti sandehaḥ | nanu "iko yaṇaci' (sū.6-1-77) ityata acītyanuvartate | tacca prathamayorityasya viśeṣaṇam | tena ajādyoḥ prathamayorityarthaḥ sampannaḥ | tatra prathamannike su ityasyājāditvāsambhacave'pi avayavāntaragatamajāditvamādāya prathamayordvayorvibhaktyorajāditvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | pratyayayostu grahaṇe suśabdasyājāditvāsambhavāt aujasoścāprathamatvāt prathamayorajādyoḥ pratyayayoriti vyākhyānaṃ na sambhavati . tathā ca prathamayoḥ vibhaktyoreva grahaṇamiti kva sandehāvakāśa iti cenna | ajādī ityetadeva prathamāvityasya viśeṣaṇam | tathā ca ajāditvena siddhau yau prathamau itarājādipratyayāpekṣayeti yāvat tayoḥ parataḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrgha ityapi vyākhyānasambhavena aujasoḥ pratyayayoreva grahaṇamityapi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | tataśca prathamayorvibhaktyorgrahaṇaṃ uta prathamayoḥ pratyayayoriti sandeharatadavastha ityata āha--atreti | "tasmācchaso naḥ puṃsi' (sū.6-1-103) iti sūtre prathamayorajādyoḥ pratyayayorgrahaṇe aujasoreva pūrvasavarṇadīrghaḥ tena sūtreṇa labhyeta | śasi tu pūrvasavarṇadīrghāprāptyā kṛtapūrvasavarṇadīrghātparatvaṃ śasavayavasakārasya kathaṃ bhavitumarhati | atastasmādityanena śasyapi pūrvasavarṇadīrghamanumanyamānaḥ sūtrakāraḥ "prathamayo' riti sūtre prathamayorvibhaktyoreva grahaṇamabhipretīti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa samprasāraṇācca (sū.6-1-108)|| āṅi pararupaṃ jñāpakamantaraṅgabalīyastvasya| adya ā ūḍhā adyoḍhā dhātūpasarga(yogaḥ) jhryoḥṭa kāryaṃ
guṇo'ntaraṅgaḥ | pūrvamupasargasya dātunā yogaḥ nādyaśabdena | adyaśabdasya tu samudāyena yogāditi | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhasamprasāraṇācca' iti sūtre "ādguṇātsavarṇadīrghatvamāṅabhyāsayoḥ' iti vārtikam | āṅi abhyāse ca pare ādguṇasavarṇadīrghayoḥ prāptau savarṇadīrgha eva bhavati na ādguṇa iti tadarthaḥ | āṅi adyoḍhā ityudāharaṇam | adya ā ūḍhā iti sthite adya ā anayoḥ savarṇadīrghe adyā uḍhā iti sthite ādguṇe adyoḍhā iti sidhyati | ādau a ūḍhā anayorguṇe tu adya oḍhā iti sthite vṛddhau adyauḍhā iti bhaveta iti vārtikāśayaḥ | nanvetadvārtikamayuktam | ādau savarṇadīrghe sati "omāṅośca' (sū.6-1-95) iti sūtreṇa āṅi pararūpavidhānasya vairyathyāpatteḥ | tathā hi śiva ā ihītyatra ā ihītyanayorādguṇe śiva ehi iti śthite "vṛddhireci' (sū.6-1-88) ityanena vṛddhau prāptāyāṃ tadapavādatvena pararūpaṃ vidhīyate | yadi ca ādguṇātsavarṇadīrghaḥ ādau bhavet tadā śiva ā anayoḥ savarṇadīrghe paścādguṇo śivehi itīṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ siddhameveti kimāṅi pararūpavidhānena ityāśṅkyāha--āṅi pararūpamiti | tathā ca "antaraṅga balīyaḥ' iti vacanajñāpanārtha āṅi parūpavidhānam | dhātūpasargayorthatkārthaṃtadantaraṅgam | ādguṇasya ca dhātūpasargakāryatvenāntaraṅgatvāt ā ihi ityanayorguṇe paścāt vṛddhau satyāṃ śivaihi ityaniṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ prasajyeteti vṛddibādhanārthamāṅi pararūpavidhānaṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | upasargasya pūrvaṃ dhātunaiva yogaḥ na adyaśabdenetyatra niyāmakamāha adyaśabdasya tu iti | adya ityasyādhārārthasya āṅā sambandho nāsti, kriyāsāpekṣatvādādhārasya āṅaśca kriyāvācitvābhāvāt | kriyā ca dhātūpasargasamudāyenocyate | ataḥ dhātūpasargasamudāyenaiva adyaśabdasya sambandhaḥ, na tu kriyāyā avācakena kevalena āṅā iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa anudāttasya ca yatro (sū.6-1-161)|| ayacaścitkaraṇādatra anta ityasyāsambandho jñāpyate | evaṃ cārthādādirudāttaḥ | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaanudāttasya ca yatrodāttalopaḥ' iti sūtrasya yasminnanudātte pare udātto lupyate tasyānudāttasya udātto bhavatītyarthaḥ | atra avidyamānodāttakaṃ yaṃ nimittīkṛtya udāttalopaḥ tasyodātta iti vā,nimittatvānādareṇa avidyamānodotte yasmin varṇe parata udāttalopastasya varṇasyodātta iti vā vyākhyānaṃ bhavitumarhati | tatra prathamapakṣe "karṣātvato ghaño'nta udāttaḥ' (sū.6-1-159) iti sūtrāt anta ityanuvartaya avidyamānodāttakasyānta udātta iti sūtrarthaḥ sampadyate | dvitīyapakṣe tu anudāttasya varṇasyaivodāttavidhānāt anta iti nānuvartate phalābhāvāt | tathā ca dvitīyapakṣe anudāttasyāderudātta iti phalitam | prathamapakṣe anudāttasyānta udātta iti phalitam | tatra kataraḥ pakṣaḥ śreyān iti śaṅkāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ pakṣa eva śreyānityāha--ayacaścitkaraṇāditi | dvāvavayavāvasya trayo'vayavā asyeti cārthe "saṃkhyāyā avayave tayap' (sū.5-2-42) iti sūtrṇa dvitribhyāṃ tayap | taṣapaśca "dvitribyāṃ tayasyāyajvā' (sū.5-2-43) iti sūtreṇāyajādeśaḥ | tayap pittvādanudāttaḥ | tatsthāniko'yacū api sthānivadbāvenānudāttaḥ tasmin pare ca "yasyeti ca' (sū.6-4-148) itīkāralope dvayaṃ trayamityatra "anudāttasya ca yatrodāttalopaḥ' iti prakṛtasūtreṇa aya ityasyāderakārasyodāttatve prāpte antodāttatvāya ayacaścitkaraṇam | yadi ca "anudāttasya ca' iti sūtre anta ityanuvarteta tadā anudāttasya aya ityatyānta evodātto bhaviṣyatīti iṣṭasiddheḥ kimayacaścitkaraṇena | ataścitkaraṇamayaco jñāpayati yadatra sūtra'nta iti nānuvartate iti | yadi nānuvartate tadā anudāttasya aya ityasya udātta ityukte prathamātikrame kāraṇābhāvādarthādādereva
bhavediti antasyodāttatvārthamayacaścitkaraṇaṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu vindāte ityatra ākārasyodāttatvasiddhiḥ | anyathā antaḥ ekāra udāttaḥ syāt | "vida vicāraṇe' iti dhātoḥ parasya āte ityasya "tāsyanudāttenṅidadupadesāllasārvadhātukamanudāttaṃ' (sū.6-1-186) iti sūtreṇa anudāttatvam | śnamaḥ pratyayatvādudāttatvam | tataḥ śnasorallope ākārasyoktasūtreṇa udāttatvamiṣyate | antagrahaṇānuvṛttau tu ekāra evodātto bhavediti | jhrjñā.ṭa aṣṭano dīrghāt (sū.6-1-182)|| aṣṭano dīrghagrahaṇaṃ ṣaṭsaṃjñāyaṃ jñāpakaṃ ākārāntasya nuḍaḍḍatdh;rtham | ātvavikalpasya ca jñāpakametata | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "aṣṭano dīrghāt ' iti sūtrasya dīrghādaṣṭanśabdātparā śasādirvibhaktirudāttetyarthaḥ | aṣṭāsu ityudāharaṇam | atra dīrghagrahaṇaṃ māstu | na ca tadabhāve "aṣṭasu' ityatra hasvādaṣṭanśabdātparasyāḥ su iti vibhakterudāttatvaṃ stāt, tadvāraṇārthaṃdīrghagrahaṇamiti vācyam | "aṣṭasu' ityatra "jhalyupottamama' (sū.6-1-180) iti sūtreṇa pareṇa ṣaṭtricaturbhyau yā jhalādirvibhaktistadante pade upottamamudāttaṃ syādityarthakena upottamasya ṭakārottarākārasyaivodāttatayā "su' ityasya pūrveṇa "aṣṭanaḥ' ( sū6-1-172) ityanenodāttatvāprāpteḥ | na ca "aṣṭāsu' ityatrāpi upottamasvaraḥ paratvātsayāditi praśne yena nāprāptinyāyena "aṣṭanaḥ' iti svarasyopottamasvarabādhakatvamiti samādhātavyam | tathā ca tulyayuktyā "aṣṭasu' ityatrāpi paraṃ upottamasvaraṃ bādhitvā apavādatvāt "aṣṭanaḥ' ita vibhaktisvaraḥ syāditi tadvārakatayā dīrdhagrahaṇaṃ sārthakamiti vācyam | aṣṭāsu ityatra hi nakārasyātve kṛtenakārāntatvābāvena ṣaṭsaṃjñāvirahāt ṣaṭsaṃjñāmāśritya vidhīyamānaḥ jhalyupottamam iti svaro naiva bhaviṣyatīti tasminnavaśyaṃ prāpte sati aṣṭanaḥ iti sūtramārabyata iti vaktumaśakyatayā apavādatvābhāvāt | tathā ca aṣṭasu ityatra paratvāt upottamasvara eva bhaviṣyati | nalopekṛte'pi tasyāsiddhatvāt nakārāntatvena ṣaṭsaṃjñakatvādupottamasvarapravṛtteḥ | tathā ca dīrghagrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityata āha--aṣṭano dīrghagrahaṇamiti | tathā ca dīrghagrahaṇaṃ ātve kṛte'pi ṣaṭsaṃjñā bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca aṣṭāsu ityatrāpi ṣaṭsaṃjñakatvāt upottamasvare prāpta eva "aṣṭanaḥ' iti sūtrārambhāt yena nāprāptinyāyenāpavādatvaṃ "aṣṭana' ityasya sūtrasyeti aṣṭāsu ityatra upottamasvaro na pravartate | tulyanyāyena aṣṭasu ityatrāpyapavādatvāt vibhaktisvare prāpte tadvārakatayā tīrghagrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | anyatra jñāpanasya phalamāha--ākārāntasya nuḍaḍḍatdh;rthamiti | aṣṭan ām ityatra "aṣṭana ā vibaktau'(sū.7-1-84) iti nakārasya ātve kṛte ākārāntasyāpyuktajñāpakena ṣaṭsaṃjñakatvāt "ṣaṭcaturbhyaśca' (sū.7-1-55) iti āmaḥ nuṭi aṣṭānāmiti rūpaṃ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | yadvā "aṣṭana ā vibhaktau' iti sūtravihitasya ātvasya nityatvāt saptamībahuvacane "aṣṭāsu' iti dīrghaghaṭitameva rūpaṃ na ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvaghaṭitaṃ "aṣṭasu' iti rūpam | tathā ca tadhdyāvartakaṃ dīrghagrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkya, aṣṭanaḥ vidhīyamānamātvaṃ vaikalpikamiti jñāpanārthaṃ dīrghagrahaṇam | tathā ca ātvābhāvapakṣe aṣṭasu iti rūpasyāpi saddhāvāt tatra "aṣṭanaḥ' iti sūtravihitasvarāpravṛtyarthaṃ tatsūtre dīrghagrahaṇaṃ sārthakamityāha--ātvavikalpasya ca iti | tathā ca ekameva dīrghagrahaṇaṃ vacanadvayasya jñāpakam | anyathānupapattyā anekasyāpyarthasya jñāpanasambhavādityāśyaḥ | tadetatsūcanāya cetyuktam | vastutastu aṣṭan ām ityatra ātvāpekṣayā antaraṅgatvāt ṣaṭsaṃjñāyāḥ ṣaṭsaṃjñāyāṃ kartavyāyāṃ ātvasya bahiraṅgatayā asiddhatvāt
nāntatvādeva ṣaṭsaṃjñāsiddheḥ tannimittakanuṭi "nopadhāyāḥ' (sū.6-4-7) iti dīrghe nalope ca aṣṭānāmitīṣṭarūpasya siddheḥ jñāpanaphalābhāvāt "bhavatyātve kṛte ṣaṭsaṃjñā' iti na jñāpanīyam | vibāṣā ātvamiti vacanajñāpakatayaiva dīrghagrahaṇam | prayojanaṃ ca aṣṭabhiḥ aṣṭābirityādirūpadvayasiddhiḥ | tatā ca ātvavikalpasya cetyatra cakāro vākārārthe iti pratibhāti | jhrjñā.ṭa titsavaritam (sū.6-1-185)|| "diva (auta) jhrutṭa (sū.6-1-131) iti taparatvaṃ jñāpayati (ūkāreṇa) jhrukāreṇaṭa bāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇamiti | evaṃ ca ūṭhi dīrghavāraṇāya taparatvam | titi pratyayagrahaṇaṃ kartavyamityetadādeśeṣu dakāramāśritya pratyākhyātam | taparasūtre dakāro'pi rcatvabhūtaḥ praśliṣyate || jhrvi.ṭa ṭhatitsvaritaṃ' iti sūtre "titi pratyayagrahaṇaṃ' iti vārtikam | tasya cāyamarthaḥ tit pratyayaḥ eva svarito bhavati iti vaktavyam | anyathā "kirati' ityatra "ṛta iddhātoḥ' (sū.7-1-100) iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānasya ikārasyāpi tittvena svaritatvāpatteriti | etaddhārtikaṃ pratyācikhyāsuḥ bhaṣyakāraḥ ṝtaḥ id iti dit ikāraḥ ādeśaḥ sa ca na tit iti svarito na pravartiṣyate iti avadat| tadupari ṝtaḥ id iti yadi chedaḥ kriyate tadā sthāninaḥ dīrghasya antaratamaḥ dīrgha eva ikāraḥ syāt | taparakaraṇe tu tatkālo ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasva eva bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya, bhāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ neti paribhabhāṣayā ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasva eva ikāro bhavati ataḥ id iti dakārānubandhavānevādeśa iti samādhāya, yadi bhāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ netyucyate tadā amūbhyāmityatra adā bhyāmityavasthāyāṃ dakārāt parasya ākārasya "adaso'serdādu do maḥ' (sū.8-2-80)iti sūtravihitaḥ ukāraḥ vidhīyamānatvāt savarṇasya ūkārasya grāhako na bhavet | tataśca amubhyāmiti ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasva eva ukāraḥ syāditi punaḥ ākṣipya bhāṣye uktam--- "evaṃ tarhyācāryapravṛttirjñāpayati bhavatyukāreṇa bhāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇamiti | yadayaṃ "diva ut' (sū.6-1-131) iti ukāraṃ taparaṃ karoti' iti | tadetanmanasikṛtyāha--iti taparatvaṃ jñāpayatītyādinā | svāṃśecāritārthyamāha--evaṃ ca ūṭhīti | dyubhyāmityatra div bhyāmiti sthite jhalparakatvāt vakārasya "cachvoḥ śuḍaḍḍatdh;nunāsike ca" (sū.3-4-19) iti sūtreṇa ūṭhi diūbhyāmiti sthite ūkārasya"diva ut' (sū 6-1-131) iti ukāre yaṇi ca dyubyāmiti sidhyati | atra bāvyamānasya ukārasya savarṇagrāhakatvena sthānyāntarthāt ukāre prāpte tannivartakatayā ut iti taparakaraṇaṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu amūbhyāmiti | nanu "diva ut' iti sūtravihitasya ukārasya tittvena "titsvaritaṃ' iti svaritavāraṇāya titi pratyayaghahaṇaṃ kartavyamenetyata āha--titi pratyayagrahaṇamiti| tathā ca "diva ud' iti sūtre'pi ud ityeva ādeśaḥ | tathā ca tittvābāvenaiva svaritasvaro na prāpsyatīti pratyayaghahaṇamanāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadi ud ityādeśastadā "taparastatkālasya' (sū 1-1-70) iti sūtrāpravṛttyā ūkāro'pyādeśo bhavedityāśaṅkyāha--taparasūtra iti | tathā ca dtaparaḥ ityatra dakārasya rcatvena ttapara iti sūtram | daparaḥ taparaśca, tatkālasya saṃjñeti sūtrārthaḥ | tataśca ud ityasyāpa tatkālasyaiva pravṛtetaḥ na dīrghasyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ |jhrjñā.ṭa bhīhrībhṛ (sū.6-1-192)|| atratyapratyayagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ svaravidhau saṅghātaḥ kāryīti | pūrvagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ svaravidhau saptamyastadantasaptamya iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "bhīhrībhṛhumadajanadhanadaridrājāgarāṃ pratyayātpūrvaṃ piti' (sū.6-1-192) iti sūtreṇa bhīprabhṛtīnāmabhyastānāṃ piti lasārvadhātuke pare pratyayātpūrvamudāttaṃ
syāditi vidhīyate | juhoti ityudāharaṇam | atra pratyayātpūrvasya okārasya udāttatvam | etatsūtrābhāve "anudātte ca' (sū.6-1-190) ityādyudāttasvaraḥ syāt | tatra pratyayagrahaṇaṃ kimartham | na ca tadabhāve "bibhayāni' ityatra "ni' iti piti pare pūrvasya āṭaḥ udāttatvaṃ bhavet | āṭaḥ pūrvasyaiva ceṣyate | tadarthaṃ pratyayagrahaṇam | sati ca tasmin yadāgamanyāyena āṭsahitasya pratyayatvāt pratyātpūrvasya udāttatvamityukte "āni' ityataḥ pūrvasya bhakārottarākārasyaivodāttatvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyetadarthaṃ pratyayagrahaṇamiti vācyam | pratyayagrahaṇābhāve'pi piti pūrvamudāttamityukte pitaḥ nerbhaktaḥ āḍaḍḍatdh;pi pidgrahaṇena grahīṣyata itiāni ityasmin pare tataḥ pūrvasyaivodāttatvamiti āṭaḥ udāttatvāpraptyā pratyayagrahaṇavairyathyādityāśaṅkya jñāpakatayā tatsārthakyamāha--atratyapratyayagrahaṇaṃ iti | tathā ca pūrvektarītyā vyarthaṃ sat pratyayagrahaṇaṃ svaravidhau saṃghātaḥ sāryīti jñāpayati | tataśca āni ityataḥ pūrvasya saṃghātasya udāttatvaṃ mā bhūt api tu pratyayātpūrvasyācaḥ udāttatvaṃ yathā syādityevamarthaṃ pratyayagrahaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu bhāṣye evamuktam "citsavarāttāsyādibhyo'nudāttatvaṃ vipratiṣedheneti yaduktaṃ tadupapantaṃ bhavati' iti | ayamasyāśayaḥ--āsīnaḥ śayāna ityatra śānayaḥ cittvāt cito'nta udāttaḥ prāptaḥ | "tāsyanudāttenṅidadupadeśāllasārvadhātukamanudāttaṃ ' (sū.6-1-186) iti śānacaḥ anudātto'pi prāptaḥ | ubhayoḥ prāptau "vipratiṣedhe paraṃ kāryaṃ' (sū.1-4-2) iti paratvāt anudātta eva bhavatīti vārtikakāreṇoktaṃ "tāsyanudāttet' iti sūtre | tacca svaravidhau yadi saṃghātasya kāryitvaṃ syāt tadaivopapadyate | anyathā "ādeḥ parasya' (sū.1-1-54) iti paribhāṣayā śānacaḥ ādeḥ ākārasyānudāttatvaṃ, antasya ca "citaḥ' ityantodāttatvamiti virodhābhāvāt viprataṣiedhasūtrasañcāro nopapadyeta | saṃghātasya svarakārthitve tu āna iti saṃghātasyānudāttatvaṃ tadantasyodāttatvena virudhyata ityevaṃ vipratiṣedhāt vārtikarītyā paratvādanudāttatvoktiḥ sādhu saṅgacchata iti | "bhīhrī' (sū.6-1-192) iti sūtra eva "piti' iti saptamīśravaṇāt "tasminniti nirdiṣṭe pūrvasya' (sū.1-1-66) iti paribhāṣayā pūrvasyeti lapsyata iti "pratyayātpūrvaṃ piti' (sū.6-1-192) iti pūrvagrahaṇaṃ kimarthamityāśaṅkayā'ha--pūrvagrahaṇamiti | tathā ca svaravidhau saptamyastadantasaptamyo bhavantīti jñāpanārthaṃ pūrvagrahaṇam | tadantapakṣe ca bhīprabhṛtibhyo vihitaḥ yaḥ pit tadantamudāttaṃ bhavatīti sūtrārthaḥ syāt | evaṃ ca sarveṣāmacāṃ paryāyeṇodāttatvaṃ syāt | ekasya pratyayātpūrvasyaiveṣyata iti tadarthaṃ pūrvagrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | jñāpanaprayojanaṃ tu "upottamaṃ riti' (sū.6-1-216) ityādau | tatra hi riti pratyaye parataḥ upottamasyodāttatvaṃ neṣyate, api tu ritpratyayāntasya yadupottamaṃ tasyodāttatvamiṣyata iti | jhrjñā.ṭa saṃjñāyāmupamānam (sū.6-1-204)|| upamānasyādyudāttavacanaṃ jñāpakaṃ anubandhalakṣaṇe svare pratyayalakṣaṇapratiṣedhasya | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "saṃjñāyāmupamāna' miti saṃjñāyāmupamānasyādyudāttatvavidhāyakaṃ sūtraṃ vyartham | na ca tadabhāve cañceva cañcā ityādau "saṃjñāyām' (sū.5-3-96) iti vihitasya kanaḥ "lummanuṣye' (sū.5-3-98) iti lupi cañcā iti śabde ādyudāttaḥ kathaṃ sidhyediti vācyam | luptasya kanaḥ pratyalakṣaṇamāśritya "ñnityādirnityam' (sū.6-1-198) iti sūtreṇaiva ādyudāttatvasiddheḥ | ādyudāttatvasya aṅgādhikārīyatvābhāvena "na lumatāṅgasya' (sū.1-1-63) iti pratyayalakṣaṇaniṣevasyāpravṛtterityāśaṅkyāha--upamānasya iti | tathā ca cañcetyādau pratyayalakṣaṇenaiva
siddhe svare "saṃjñāyāmupamānam' iti sūtraṃ vyarthaṃ sat anubandhanimittake svare pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ na bhavatīti jñāpayati | evaṃ ca "ñnityādirnityaṃ' iti svarasya itsaṃjñānimittakatvāt tasmin kartavye kanlupaḥ pratyayalakṣaṇābhāvāt cañcā ityasya ñnidantatvaṃ nāstīti ādyudāttasvarāprāptau tatprāpakaṃ "saṃjñāyāmupamānaṃ' iti sūtraṃ sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | jñāpanasya phalaṃ tu gargā ityādau gargasya gautrāpatyānītyarthe "gargādibhyoyañ' (sū.4-1-105) iti sūtravihitasya yañaḥ "yañañośca' (sū.2-4-64) iti luki tasya pratyayalakṣaṇamāśritya "ñnityādirnityaṃ' ityādyudāttaḥ prāptaḥ | sa ca anubandhalakṣaṇe svare pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ neti jñāpanānna bhavatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhanottarapade'nudāttādāvapṛthivī' (sū.6-2-142) ityādāvapṛthivītyādiparyudāso jñāpakaḥ "svaravidhau vyañjanamavidyamānavat' iti | "yato'nāvaḥ' (sū.5-1-213) ityatrānāva iti pratiṣedho jñāpakaḥ "halsvaraprāptau vyañjanamavidyamānava' diti | "gatiranantaraḥ' (sū.6-2-59) anantaragrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati kṛdgrahaṇe gatikārakapūrvasyāpīti | jhrvi.ṭa etāsāṃ tisṛṇāmapi paribhāṣāṇāṃ paribhāṣenduśekhare paṭhitatvādiha na vivriyante | jhrjñā.ṭa dvitribhyāṃ pāt (sū.6-2-197)|| mūrdhasviti nirdeśo jñāpayati vibhāṣā samāsānta iti | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhadvitribhyāṃ pāddanmūrdhasu bahuvrīhau (sū.6-2-197) iti sūtreṇa dvitribhyāṃ pareṣu pād dan mūrdhan ityeteṣu bahuvrīhisamāse antodāttasvaraḥ vidhīyate | tatra mūrvan śabdasyodāharaṇaṃ dvimūrdhaḥ trimūrdhaḥ iti | "dvitribhyāṃ ṣa mūrdhna' (sū.5-4-115) iti sūtreṇātra samāsāntaḥ ṣapratyayaḥ | tasya ca samāsāntapratyayasya nityatvāt "pāddanmūrdheṣu' (sū.6-2-197) iti kṛtasamāsāntatayā nirdeśa eva ucitaḥ | pād iti kṛtasamāsāntenopakramāt mūrdhaśabdasyāpi tathaiva nirdeṣṭumucitatvādityāśaṅkya, yataḥ "pāddanmūrghasu' iti akṛtasamāsāntatayā sūtre mūrdhaśabdo nirdiṣṭaḥ ata eva samāsāntapratyayo vaikalpika iti jñāpayati | tathā ca samāsāntābhāvapakṣamāśritya mūrdhasu iti nirdiṣṭaṃ sūtre | ataśca dvimūrdhā trimūrdhā ityakṛtasamāsānte'pi bahuvrīhāvanenāntodāttasvaraḥ sidhyatītyāśayavānāha--mūrdhasviti nirdeśa iti | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu supathī nagarī ityādau "inaḥ striyām' (sū.5-4-152) iti kap vaikalpikatvānna bhavatīti | "samāsāntavidhiranityaḥ' iti paribhāṣayāpi gatārthametadvacanam | "prateraṃśvādayastatpuruṣe (sū.6-2-193) ityantodāttatvāyāṃśvādiṣu rājanśabdapāṭho 'syā jñāpaka iti paribhāṣenduśekhare || jhrjñā.ṭa hṛdayasya hṛllekhayadaṇūlāseṣu (sū 6-3-50)|| lekhagrahaṇāduttarapadādhikāre pratyagrahaṇe rupagrahaṇaṃ jñāpyate | "khityanavyayasya' ityatrānavyayasyetyukteḥ khidantagrahaṇam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "hṛdayasya hṛllekha-' iti sūtre "yat' "aṇ' iti pratyayagrahaṇena "pratyayagrahaṇe yasmātsa vihitastadādestadantasya grahaṇa' miti paribhāṣayā yadantasya aṇantasya ca grahaṇāt yadante aṇante ca parata eva hṛdayasya hṛdādeśaḥ syānna tu kevale yati aṇi ca | tathā ca hṛdyaṃ hārdamiti na sidhyedityāśaṅkyāhalekhagrahaṇāditi | yadi aṇantasyātra grahaṇaṃ syāttadā lekhaśabdasya aṇantatvādeva tasmin pare hṛdayasya hṛdādeśe siddhe lekhagrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | yato lekhagrahaṇaṃ karoti tajjñāpayatyācāryaḥ aṇ ityanena svarūpameva gṛhyate na aṇantamiti |tathā ca aṇantasya lekhaśabdasya aṇgrahaṇena grahaṇāsambhavāt lekhagrahaṇaṃ kṛtamiti | yadvā jñāpakasya prakaraṇāpekṣatvāt uttarapadādhikāre pratyayagrahaṇe tadantagrahaṇaṃ nāstīti jñāpyate | tena kumārī gauritarā ityatra "gharūpakalpapū'-(sū.6-3-43) iti ghasaṃjñake pratyaye parato vidhīyamānaḥ ṅyo ḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍanasvaḥ ghānte na bhavati iti bhāvaḥ | yadyevaṃ jñāpyate tadā "khityanavyayasya'
(sū.6-3-66) iti vidhīyamānaḥ hrasvaḥ khiti parata eva bhavet na tu khidante | tathā ca śuniṃdhaya ityādau khidante dhayaśabde pare hrasvo na bhavet ityāśaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakyāha--khityanavyayasyetyatreti | yadi tadantagrahaṇamatrāpi na syāt tadā khitparakasyāvyayasya durlabhatvādeva avyayasya hrasvāprāpteḥ anavyayasyeti pratiṣedho'narthakaḥ syāt | kṛtaśca sa pratiṣedhaḥ atra sūtre tadantagrahaṇamastīti jñāpayati | tathā ca "doṣāmanyamahaḥ' ityādau hrasvavāraṇāya anavyayasyeti niṣedhaḥ sārthakaḥ | prayojanaṃ tu śuniṃdhaya ityādau khidante pare hrasvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || jhrjña.ṭa ṭhasamaḥ sami' (sū.6-3-93) "nahivṛti' (sū.6-3-116) añcatinahyādiṣu kvibgrahaṇāt dhātugrahaṇena tadādividhirbhavatīti | jhrjñā.ṭa nanu "samaḥ sami' iti sūtreṇa kvibante (apratyayānte) añcatau pare samaḥ samyādeśo vidhīyate | samyaṅ ityudāharaṇam | tathā "nahivṛtivṛṣivyadhirucisahitaniṣu kvau' (sū.6-3-116) iti sūtreṇa kvibanteṣu nahyādiṣu parataḥ pūrvapadasya dīrgha ucyate | upānat ityādikamudāharaṇam | tatra kvigrahaṇam māstu | na ca tadabhāve samañcanaṃ upanahanamityādāvapi sūtradvayaṃ pravarteteti vācyam | uttarapadādhikārīyatvāt uktasūtradvayasyāpi añcunahyādirūpe uttarapade pare sūtradvayaṃ pravartate | samañcanamityatra añcana ityasya uttarapadatve'pi añc ityasyottarapadatvābhāvānna doṣaḥ | kevalasya añc ityasya ca kvippratyaye satyeva uttarapadatvaṃ bhavati | anyathā dhātostasya padatvasyaiva durlabhatvāt | tathā ca uttarapade iti adhikāramahimnaiva kvibanta iti lābhasambhavāt kvigrahaṇamanarthakamityāśaṅkyāha-añcatinahyādiṣviti | tathā ca jñāpanārthaṃ kvigrahaṇaṃ kriyate | yatra saptamyantaṃ viśeṣaṇatayā dhātugrahaṇaṃ kriyate tatra algrahaṇatvābhāve'pi tadādividhirbhavatīti jñāpyate | itthaṃ ca añcatāvityasya nahivṛttītyādeśca uttarapade ityetadviśeṣaṇatvena tadādividhau sati añc ityādirdhāturādiryasyottarapadasya tasmin parataḥ samaḥ samyādeśaḥ pūrvapadasya dīrghaśca bhavataḥ iti sūtrārthaḥ syāt | tathā sati samañcanaṃ upanahanamityādāvapi bhavet añcana (sū.7-1-28)|| atra vibhaktyorgrahaṇe yuṣmadasmadoranādeśe "dvitīyāyāṃ' ca iti jñāpakam | (sū.7-2-86,87) jhrvi.ṭa nan "ṅeprathamayoram' iti sūtreṇa prathamayorityanena kiṃ prathamayorvibhaktyorgrahaṇaṃ uta prathamayoḥ pratyayoḥ iti saṃdeho jāyate | pratyayayorgrahaṇamiti pakṣe su au anayorevāmādeśaḥ syāt | sandehabījaṃ tu yadi prathamayoriti strīliṅganirdeśaḥ tadā vibhaktyorgrahaṇam | yadā tu
