"ṅeprathamayo' rityatra prathamayorvibhaktyoreva grahaṇaṃ na tu pratyayayoḥ| tatra jñāpakaṃ tu "dvitīyāyāṃ ca' iti sūtram | tasya ca sūtrasya dvitīyāvibhaktau parataḥ yuṣmadasmadorākāraḥ syādityarthaḥ | yadi ca "ṅeprathamayoram' iti sūtreṇa prathamayoḥ pratyayayoḥ su au ityanayoḥ amādeśo vidhīyeta tadā dvitīyāvibhakteramādeśābhāvāt yuṣmadasmadbhyāmanādeśarūpaiva dvitīyāvibhaktirbhaviṣyatīti tasyāṃ parataḥ "yuṣmadasmadoranādeśe' (sū.7-2-86) ityanenaiva ākārādeśe sidde "dvitīyāyāṃ ca' iti sūtraṃ vyarthaṃ bhavet | tasmāt "dvitīyāyāṃ ca' iti sūtraṃ "ṅeprathamayoḥ' iti sūtre prathamayorityanena prathamādvitīyāvibhaktyorgrahaṇamiti jñāpayati | tathā ca dvitīyāvibhakterapi tena amādeśavidhānāt yuṣmadasmadbhyāṃ parā am iti ādeśarūpaiva vibhaktirbhaviṣyati | tasyāṃ ca anādeśa iti pratiṣedhe prāpte ākāravivānārthaṃ "dvitīyāyāṃ ca' iti sūtramiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa neḍḍḍatdh;vaśi kṛti (sū.7-2-8)|| "kṛsṛbhṛ' iti sūtre vṛñvṛṅorgrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ pratyayāśrayo'pīṭpratiṣedho niyamyata iti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "neḍḍḍatdh;vaśi kṛti' iti sūtre kṛdgrahaṇaṃ māstu | na ca tadabhāve bibhidiva bibhidima ityatra vaśādestiṅo'pi iḍaḍḍatdh;āgamaniṣedhaḥ syāditi vācyam | "kṛsṛbhṛvṛstudrustuśruvo liṭi' (sū.7-2-23) iti sūtreṇa krādibhya eva parasyeṇniṣedha iti niyamāt bibhidiva
ityādāviṇniṣedhāpravṛtteḥ | na ca sa niyamaḥ prakṛtiviśeṣamāśritya pravṛtto ya iṇniṣedhastadviṣayakaḥ | "neḍḍḍatdh;vaśi' iti tu pratyayāśrayo niṣedhaḥ | etaṃ ca niṣedhaṃ krādiniyamo na spṛśati | tathā ca bibhidivetyatrāpi iṇniṣedhaḥ syāditi tadvāraṇāya kṛdgrahaṇamāvaśyakamiti vācyam | krādiniyamo na prakṛtilakṣaṇaniṣedhamātraviṣayaḥ, kiṃ tu prakṛtyāśrayaḥ pratyayāśrayo vā yāvāniṇniṣedhaḥ sa liṭi cet tarhi krādibhya eveti viyamāt bibhidiva ityatra iṇniṣedhāpravṛtteḥ kṛtītyasya vairyathyāt | prakṛtipratyayobhyalakṣaṇasyāpi iṇniṣedhasya krādiniyamaḥ ityatra jñāpakaṃ tu "kṛsṛbhṛvṛ' (sū.7-2-13) iti sūtre vṛ iti sāmānyena vṛṅvṛñorubhayorgrahaṇam | tayorgrahaṇasya prayojanaṃ hi vavṛmahe ityādāviṇmā bhūditi | yadi ca krādiniyamaḥ prakṛtyāśrayameva pratiṣedhaṃ bādheta na pratyayāśrayaṃ tadā "neḍḍḍatdh;vaśi kṛti' (sū.7-2-8) "rśyukaḥ kiti' (sū.7-2-11) iti sūtrābhyāmeva iṭpratiṣedhasya siddhatvāt nārtho vṛgrahaṇena | tatkriyamāṇaṃ pratyayāśrayasyāpi pratiṣedhasya nivṛttiṃ jñāpayati | tathā ca vṛgrahaṇābhāve krādibhinnatvena vṛṅvṛñoḥ, tataḥ pratyayāśrayaḥ "neṅvaśi' itīṇniṣedho'pi krādibhya eveṇniṣedha iti niyamena vyāvartitaḥ syāt | tataśca vavṛmahe ityādau iṇniṣedho na sidhyet | tatrāpīṇniṣedhasya siddhaye krādisūtre vṛgrahaṇamarthavadbhavati | tataśca krādiniyamādeva bibhidivetyādāviṇniṣedho na bhaviṣyatīti tatreṇniṣedhāpravṛttiphalakatvaṃ "neḍḍḍatdh;vaśi kṛti' iti sūtre kṛdgrahaṇasya na yujyata iti sthitam | sarvametanmanasi kurvannāha---kṛsṛbhṛ iti sūtra iti | jhrjñā.ṭa ghuṣiraviśabdane (sū.7-2-23)|| viśabdane curādiṇicā siddhe aviśabdana ityuktirghuṣervibhāṣā ṇico jñāpikā | tena jughuṣuḥ (puṣyamāṇā vā) jhrpuṣyamāṇavāḥṭa iti siddham | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "ghuṣiraviśabdane' iti sūtre aviśabdana iti māstu | ghuṣirhi aviśabdanārthakaḥ bvādau paṭhyate | curādau ca ghuṣirviśabdane iti paṭhyate | sa ca ṇijantaḥ | sūtre ca ghuṣiriti nirdeśāt bhauvādikasyāviśabdanārthakasyaiva grahaṇaṃ na curādeḥ | tadā hi "ghoṣiḥ' iti nirdeṣṭavyaṃ syāt | evaṃ ca ghuṣiriti nirdeśabalādeva viśabdanārthakacurādighuṣadhātuvyāvṛttipūrvakaṃ aviśabdanārthakasya bhauvādikaghuṣadhātoreva grahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīti aviśabdana iti vyartha ityāśaṅkyāha--viśabdana ityādinā | siddha iti | aviśabdane ityasminnaṃśe siddha ityarthaḥ |tathā ca aviśabdana ityetat vyarthaṃ sat ghuṣeścaurādikasya vikalpena ṇic bhavatīti jñāpayati | ṇijabhāvapakṣe ca ghuśiriti igantena viśabdanārthakasya caurādikasyāpi grahaṇaṃ prayojanaṃ tu viśabdanārthakacaurādikadhātoḥ ṇijrahitatayā "jughuṣuḥ' iti prāmāṇikaprayogaḥ upapanno bhavatīti | bhāṣye--- "mahīpālavacaḥ śrutvā jughuṣuḥ puṣyamāṇavāḥ' iti vākyaṃ pramāṇatayā uddhṛtam | svābhiprāyasya śabdena prakaṭīkaraṇaṃ viśabdanam | tathā ca aviśabdanārthe iṇniṣedhaḥ ghuṣṭā rajjuriti | viśabdena tu iṭ ghuṣitaṃ vākyamiti | jhrjñā.ṭa pratyayottara (sū.7-2-97)|| idameva jñāpayati atra maparyantānuvṛttirvā jñāpayati antaraṅgānapi vidhīn bahiraṅgo lugbādhata iti | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyam | jhrjñā.ṭa mṛjervṛddhiḥ (sū.7-2-114)|| bhrauṇahatye tatvanipātanaṃ dhātoḥ kāryamucyamānaṃ tatpratyaye bhavatītyasyārthasya jñāpakam | jhrvi.ṭa iyamapi paribhāṣā | jhrjñā.ṭa svāderiñi (sū.7-3-8)|| pratiṣedhe śvādigrahaṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ anyatra śvangrahaṇe tadādigrahaṇasya | śauvahānaṃ nāma nagaram, śauvādaṃṣṭro maṇirityartham | jhrvi.ṭa ṭhaśvāderiñi' iti sūtrasya yasya śvaśabdaḥ ādiḥ tasmāt iñi pare "dvārādīnāṃ ca' (sū.7-3-4) iti sūtravihitaḥ aica na bhavatītyarthaḥ | śvabhastrasyāpatyaṃ śvābastriḥ ityudāharaṇam |
nanu "dvārādīnāṃ ca' iti sūtreṇa dvārādigaṇapaṭhitānāṃ śabdānāmāderaco vṛddhirna bhavati, paraṃ tu yvābhyāṃ pūrvamaijāgamo bhavatīti vidhīyate | dvārādigaṇe ca ścanśabdaḥ paṭhyate | tathā ca ścanśabdasyāvayavāt vakārāt pūrvamaijāgamasya tena prāptāvapi śvabhastraśabdāvayavaviṣaye ejāgamo na tena prāpnoti tathā ca prāptyabhāvāt pratiṣedho'narthaka ityāśaṅkyāha--pratiṣedhe śvādigrahaṇamiti | uktarītyā vyarthaṃ sat śvādisūtraṃ śvangrahaṇena śvanśabdādyavayavakasya śabdasyāpi grahaṇamiti jñāpayati | tataśca śvabhastraśabapdasyāpi śvangrahaṇena grahaṇāt "dvārādīnāṃ ca' iti sūtreṇa aijāgame prāpte tanniṣedhārthaṃ "śvāderiñi' iti sūtram | jñāpanaphalaṃ tu--śauvahānam śauvādaṃṣṭra ityādāvaijāgamasiddhiḥ | tathā hi-jihate'sminnityarthe "o hāṅ gatau' iti dhātoḥ adhikaraṇe lyuṭ hānam | śunāṃ hānaṃ śvahānam | śvahānamasminnastītyarthe "tadasminnastīti deśe tannāmni (sū.4-2-67) iti sūtreṇāṇ | "dvārādīnāṃ ca' sūtreṇa vṛddhiniṣedhaḥ aijāgamaśca | śauvahānamiti rūpam | evaṃ śuno daṃṣṭrā śvādaṃṣṭrā | "śuno dantadaṃṣṭrā ' (vā.6-3-130) iti vārtikena dīrghaḥ | tatra bhava ityaṇ | dvārādīnāṃ ceti vṛddhiniṣedhaḥ aijāgamaśca | śauvādaṃṣṭra iti rūpam | atraubhayatrāpi śvanśabdena tadādergrahaṇe satyeva tatsūtraṃ pravarteteti tadādigrahaṇaphalamavaseyamiti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhaprācāṃ grāmanagarāṇām' (sū.7-3-14) prācāṃ grāmāṇāmityeva siddhe nagaragrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati grāmagrahaṇe nagaragrahaṇaṃ neti | tattu--"udīcyagrāmāt' (sū.4-2-109) "vāhīkagrāmebhyaśca' (4-2-117) "dikśabdā grāma' (sū.6-2-103)--iti sthalatraye nagaragrahaṇakartavyatāpacyā bhāṣye dūṣitam | "viśiṣṭaliṅga' (sū.2-4-7) ityatra tu nagarapratiṣedho'vaktavya eveti bhāṣye spaṣṭam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu janasamūhanivāsavācinau grāmanagaraśabdau | tatra grāmaśabdenaiva janasamūhanivāsatvāviśeṣāt nagarasyāpi grahaṇe siddhe "prācāṃ grāmanagarāṇām' iti sūtre nagarapadopādānaṃ vyarthamityāśaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakya, ata evātra vyākaraṇe grāmapadena nagaragrahaṇaṃ neti jñāpayatītyāha--prācāṃ grāmāṇāmityeveti | paraṃ tvevaṃ jñāpaṃna "udīcyagrāmāt' ityādisūtratraye grāmaśabdena nagaragrahaṇasyāpīṣṭatvāt tanna ghaṭeteti bhāṣye dūṣitamityāha--tattu iti | nanu grāmagrahaṇena nagarasyāpi grahaṇamiṣṭaṃ cet "viśiṣṭaliṅgo nadīdeśo'grāmāḥ' (sū.2-4-7) iti sūtre grāmapratiṣedhenaiva nagarapratiṣedhasyāpi lābhāt vārtikakāreṇa yaduktaṃ nagarasyāpi pratiṣedho vaktavya iti tadānavaśyakamityāśaṅkyoṣṭāpattyā pariharati--viśiṣṭaliṅga ityatra tu iti | tathā ca tatra sūtre nagarapratiṣedho na vaktavya evetyarthaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhasaṃkhyāyāssaṃ' (sū.7-3-15)|| parimāṇāntasyāsaṃjñeti siddhe saṃvatsaragrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati parimāṇagrahaṇena kālagrahaṇaṃ neti | tena dvivarṣetyādau"aparimāṇa' iti paryudāso na | jhrvi.ṭa nan "saṃkhyāyāḥ saṃvatsarasaṃkhyasya ca' (sū.7-3-15) iti sūtreṇa saṃkhyāvācakaśabdāt parasya saṃvatsaraśabdasya saṃkhyāvācakaśabdasya cottarapadavṛddhirvidhīyate ñidādau taddhite pare | dvisāṃvatsarikaḥ dviṣāṣṭika ityādikaṃ udāharaṇam | tatra saṃvatsaragrahaṇaṃ māstu, tadabhāve'pi saṃvatsaraśabdasya kālarūpaparimāṇavācakasya "parimāṇāntasyāsaṃjñāśāṇayoḥ' (sū 7-3-17) iti sūtreṇaivottarapadavṛddheḥ siddheḥ | kālo'pi hi kriyāparicchedahetutvātparimāṇamityāśaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍakya, ata eva parimāṇagrahaṇena kālarūpaparimāṇagrahaṇaṃ neti jñāpayatītyāha-- parimāṇāntasyāsaṃjñeti siddha iti | tathā ca "parimāṇāntasya' iti sūtre parimāṇaśabdena saṃvatsararūpakālaparimāṇasya grahaṇāsambhavāt dvisāṃvatsarika ityādau tena sūtreṇottarapadavṛddheraprāptau tatprāpaṇārthaṃ "saṃkhyāyāssaṃvatsara' iti sūtre
saṃvatsaragrahaṇamiti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu dvivarṣā ityādau dve varṣe bhṛtā ityarthe "tamadhīṣṭa' (sū.5-1-80) iti ṭhañi tasya "varṣālluk ca' (sū.5-1-88) iti luki "dvigoḥ' (sū.4-1-21) iti ṅīp prāptaḥ, sa ca "aparimāṇabistācitakambalyebhyo na taddhitaluki' (sū. 4-1-22) iti sūtreṇa pratiṣidhyate | yadi kālo'pi parimāṇaṃ syāt tadā dvivarṣaśabdasya parimāṇāntatvena aparimāṇeti paryudāsāt ṅīppratiṣedho na bhavet | kālasyāparimāṇatvenaiva ṅīpaḥ pratiṣedho bhavatīti | tadetadāha--tena dvivarṣetyādāviti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhapratyayasthāt' (sū.7-3-44)|| "na yāsayoḥ' (sū.7-3-45) iti pratiṣedhāt jñāpakāt vyavadhāne'pyetikāsvityādau pravṛttiḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanvetacchabdasya striyāṃ akaci jasi tyadādyatve ṭāpi itve ca etikā iti rūpamiṣyate | tatrākāreṇa vyavadhānāt kakārasya ṭāpparakatvaṃ nāstīti "pratyayasthātkātpūrvasya' iti itvaṃ na prāpnoti ityāśaṅkyāha--na yāsayoriti pratiṣedhāditi | yakā sakā ityādau itvāpratiṣedhārthaṃ "na yāsayoḥ' (sū.7-3-45) iti sūtramārabhyate | tatrāpi yacchabdasya tacchabdasya ca akaci tyadādyatve ṭāpi kakārasyākāreṇa vyavadhānāt ṭāpparakatvābhāvādeva ikārasyāprāpteḥ tatpratiṣedho'narthakaḥ syāt | ato jñāpayati akāravyavadhāne'pi itvaṃ bhavatīta ityāśayaḥ | atra kāditi varṇamātrasya grahaṇamiti pakṣe etikā hārikā iti sthaladvaye'pi yena nāvyavadhānanyāyenākāravyavadhāne'pītvāsiddhiḥ | saṃghātagrahaṇamiti pakṣe hāriketyādāvakāreṇa vyavadhānameva nāsti | etiketyatra tu saṃghātakakārābhāvāditvasya prāptireva neti "na yāsayoḥ' (sū.7-3-45) iti jñāpakena evaṃjātīyakānāmitvaṃ sādhitaṃ bhāṣye | evaṃjātīyakānāmityetat akajvatāmiti vyākhyātaṃ ca kaiyaṭena | evaṃ sthite jñāpakāt vyavadhāne'pītyuktiścintyā iti pārthasārathibhaṭṭācāryāḥ | jhrjña.ṭa ṭhaheracaṅi' (sū.7-3-56)|| acaṅīti pratiṣedho jñāpayati anyatra ṇyadhikasya kutvaṃ bhavatīti | tena prajighāyayiṣatīti | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "heracaṅi' iti sūtrasyāyamarthaḥ abhyāsātparasya hinoteḥ hasya kutvaṃ syānna tu caṅīti | "jighāya' ityādikamudāharaṇam | tatra acaṅīti ajīhayat ityatra hakārasya kutvavāraṇārthamupāttam | tadhdyartham | tathā hi yasmin pratyaye nimitte aḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagasaṃjñā tannimittako yo'bhyāsaḥ tannimittakadvayoḥ pūrva iti yāvat | tataḥ parasya hasya kutvamiti sūtraṃ vyākhyāyate | ṇau hinotiraṅgaṃ, caṅi abhyāsa iti aṅgatvābyāsatvayoḥ samānanimittakatvābhāvāt prājīhayadityatra kutvaṃ na bhaviṣyati | tathā ca acaṅītyetat ṇyantasyāpi kutvaṃ bhavatīti jñāpayati | aṅgatvasya ṇic nimittam abhyāsasya caṅ nimittamiti nimittabhede satyāpi kutvaṃ bhavatīti yāvat | tathā ca prājīhayadityatrāpi kutve prāpte tadvāraṇāya acaṅītyuktam | tena caṅo'nyasmin dvitvanimitte pare ṇyantasya kutvamabhyanujñāyate | tasya phalaṃ tuprajighāyayiṣati ityatra ṇijantāddhinoteḥ sani kutvasiddhiriti | "prakṛtigrahaṇe ṇyaghikasyāpi grahaṇam' iti paribhāṣārūpeṇeyaṃ paribhāṣenduśekhare paṭhitā | jhrjñādṛṭa ṭhajñājanorjñā' (sū 7-3-79) dīrghoccāraṇaṃ jñāpayatyaṅgavṛttaparibhāṣām | "ṇau caṅyu' (sū.7-4-1) oṇerṛditkaraṇāt jñāpakāt dvirvacanāddhrasvatvaṃ balīya iti | "nāglopi' (sū.7-4-2) iti sūtre agiti pratyāhāragrahaṇāt jñāpakāt vṛddherlopo balīyān | tasmāṇṇerṇicyupasaṃkhyānaṃ kāryam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu mā bhavānaṭiṭat ityatra aṭadhātorṇyantāt luṅi caṅi aṭ i at iti sthite upadhāvṛddhau āṭ i at iti jāte "ṇau caṅyupadhāyā hrasvaḥ' iti sūtreṇa ākārasya hrasve aṭi at iti jāte "adāderdvitīyasya
(sū.6-1-2) iti ṭiśabdasya dvitve aṭi ṭi at sthiteṇilope aṭiṭat iti rūpamiṣyate | tanna sidhyati | āṭi at ityasyāmavasthāyāṃ paramapyupadhāhrasvaṃ bādhitvā nityatvāt ṭiśabdasya dvitve āṭiṭi at ityatra prathamasya ṇicaḥ pareṇa ṇicā vyavadhānena caṅparakatvābhāvāt upadhāhrasvatvāpravṛtteḥ āṭiṭat ityeva rūpaprāpterityāśaṅkyāha--oṇerṛditkaraṇāditi | oṇerṛditkaraṇāt dvirvacanāt hrasvatvaṃ balīya iti jñāpyate | tathā ca hrasvatvasya balavattaratvāt ādāvupadhāhrasvatve kṛte tadanantaraṃ dvitve ca aṭiṭat ityeva rūpamiti bhāvaḥ | oṇṛghātoḥ ṇijantālluṅi caṅi oṇi at iti sthite upadhāyā okārasya "ṇau caṅyupadhāyā hrasvaḥ' (sū.7-4-1) itti hrasve prāpte tasya "nāglopiśāsvṛditām' (sū.7-4-2) iti sūtreṇa hrasvatvaniṣedhārthaṃ oṇṛ iti dhātuḥ ṛditkṛtaḥ | yadi ca nityatvāt ādau dvirvacanaṃ bhavet tadā ṇiśabdasya dvitve oṇiṇi at iti sthite prathamasya ṇicaḥ dvitīyena vyavadhānāt caṅūparakatvābhāvādeva "ṇau caṅi' iti hrasvo na bhaviṣyatīti kimṛditkaraṇena | kṛtaṃ ca tat dvirvavanāddhrasvatvaṃ balīya iti jñāpayati | tathā ca hrasve kṛte tato dvitve ca mā bhavānuṇiṇat ityeva bhavet mā bhavānoṇiṇat iti ceṣyate | tadarthaṃ oṇerṛdatkiraṇam | tena "nāglopi' iti hrasvaniṣeghāt iṣṭarūpasiddhiriti svāṃśe cāritārthyam | anyatra prayojanaṃ tu mā bhavānaṭiṭat iti pūrvoktam iti bhāvaḥ | "ṇau caṅi' iti sūtre "upadhāhrasve ṇorṇicyupasaṃkhyānaṃ' iti vārtikam | ṇijantāṇṇici tato luṅi caḍiḍḍatdh; ca upadhāhrasvatvaṃ bhavatīti vaktavyamiti tadarthaḥ | vāditavantaṃ prayojitavānavīvadadvīṇāṃ devadattena iti codāharaṇaṃ bhāṣye pradarśitam | vaderṇijantāt vādi ityasmāt ṇici luṅi caṅi aḍaḍḍatdh;āgame avād i i at ityatra prathamasya ṇicaḥ dvitīyena ṇicā vyavadhānāt caṅparakatvaṃ nāstīti upadhāhrasvatvamaprāptaṃ vārtikakāreṇa vidhīyate | nanvatra dvitīyasya ṇicaḥ "ṇeraniṭi' (sū.6-4-51) iti lope kṛte vyavadhānābhāvāt sūtreṇaiva hrasvatvaṃ setsyati | na ca ṇerlope'pi tasya sthānibadbhāvāt vyavadhānamastyeveti vācyam | caṅparanirhrāse "kvilugupadhātvacaṅparanirhrasakutveṣu' (vā.1-1-58) iti sthānivadbhāvapratiṣedhāt | na caivamapi prathamasyāpi ṇico'tra lopāt tasya ca ikārasya aktvena tallopānantaramaṅgamaglopi bhavatīti "nāglopiśāsvṛditām' (sū.7-4-2) iti niṣedhe prāpte pratiprasavarūpeṇa vārtikena hrasvatvaṃ vidhīyata iti vācyam | lopāpekṣayā paratvāt ṇicaḥvṛddhau aikāre kṛte tasya lopaḥ, aikāraśca nāk | tasmāt tallopavadaḍhaddhaṭhṭhaḍagaṃ nāglopi bhavatīti "nāglopi' iti niṣedhāpravṛtyā ṇau caṅīti sūtreṇaiva hrasvasiddhau vārtikārambho vyartha ityata āha--nāglopīti sūtra iti | ayamatrābhiprāyaḥ--vṛddherlopo balīyāniti jñāpyate | tatra ca nāglopīti sūtrameva jñāpakam | mālāmākhyat amamālat ityatra ākārarūpaṭerlopāt aglopitvāmastīti "nāglopi' iti sūtreṇopadhāhrasvatvaniṣedha iṣyate | tatra alopināṃ netyanenaiva niṣedhalābhe aglopināṃ neti pratyāhāragrahaṇam cāṭumākhyāt acacāṭat ityādau ukāralopayuktāṅgasyāpi upadhāhrasvapratiṣedhārthamiti vaktavyam | tatra ṇicaṃ nimittikṛtya ukārasya vṛddhau kṛtāyāṃ anantaraṃ ṭilope aukāralopitvamevāsti, nāglopitvamastīti pratyāhāragrahaṇe'pīṣṭāsiddhyā pratyāhāragrahaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt | kṛtaṃ ca pratyāhāragrahaṇaṃ vṛddherlopo balīyāniti jñāpayati | tataśca avīvadadityādau ṇilope aglopitvāt niṣedhe prāpte tadbādhanāya upadhāhrasvatve ṇerṇicyupasaṃkhyānaṃ kartavyameveti | tadāha --tasmāṇṇerṇicīti | jhrjñā.ṭa ṭhanāglopi' (sū.7-4-2)|| aglope
sthānivadbāvenaiva siddhe aglopigrahaṇam agvatāṃ lopo'pyaglopiśabdena gṛhyata ityevamartham | ita uttaraṃ sthānivattvābhāvajñāpakamaglopigrahaṇam iti bhāṣyaṃ tvekadeśyuktiḥ | jhrviṭa nanu "nāglopiśāsvṛditām' iti sūtrasya ṇici aglopinaḥ śāsterṛditāṃ copadhāyā hrasvo na syāccaṅpare ṇau parata ityarthaḥ | tatrāglopina udāharaṇam "amamālat' iti | mālāmākhyadityarthe mālāśabdāt "tatkaroti tadācaṣṭe' iti ṇici luṅi aṭi caṅi ca a mālā i at iti sthite "ṇāviṣṭhavat' iti ṭerākārasya lope māśabdasya dvitvādau ca a mamāl i at ityavasthāyām upadhāhrasvo niṣidhyate ākārarūpāglopavattvādaṅgasya | atra ṭilopasya "acaḥ parasmin' (sū.1-1-57) iti sthānivadbhāvena makārottarākārasyopadhātvābhāvādeva "ṇau caṅyupadhāyāḥ' (sū.7-4-1) iti hrasvasyāprāpteḥ nāglopīti pratiṣedho'narthaka ityata āha--aglopa iti | agvataḥ samudāyasya lopo'pyaglopagrahaṇena gṛhyata ityetadarthamaglopigrahaṇam | tena "atyararājat' ityatra rājānamatikrāntavānityarthe' iti ṇici luṅi caṅi ca "an' iti ṭerlepe ararāj at ityatropagāhrasvaniṣedhaḥ sidhyati | ana iti samudāyo hi nāk, atastallopo yathāśrute aglopaśabdena na gṛhyeta | agvato lopo'pyaglopaśabdena gṛhyata ityukte tu an iti samudāyasya akārarūpāgvattvāt tallopayuktamaṅgamaglopi bhavatīti "nāglopi' iti upadhāhrasvaniṣedhaḥ saddhaḥ | tathā ca amamālat ityādau sthānivadbhāvena nirvāhe'pi "atyararājat' ityatra ajjhalsamudāyādeśasya lopasyājādeśatvābhāvena sthanivattvāpravṛtteḥ upadhāhrasvaniṣedhalābhārthamaglopigrahaṇaṃ kṛtam | tacca agvatāṃ lopo'pyaglopagrahaṇena gṛhyata iti svīkṛta eva sārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | tadetat abhipretyoktaṃ kaiyaṭe--- "aglopagrahaṇasārmathyācca samudāyalopo'pyaglopa āśrīyate, kevalāglope pradiṣeghānarthakyāt' iti | "evaṃ tarhi siddhe sati yadaglopināṃ neti pratiṣedhaṃ śāsti tajjñāpayatyācāryaḥ ita uttaraṃ sthānivadbhāvo na bhavatīti |' iti nāglopīti pratiṣedhasyārthāntarajñāpakatvamuktam | tattu "acaḥ parasmin' iti sūtre halacorādeśo na sthānivaditi bhāṣye nirṇītatvena tata eva gatārthamityekadeśyuktirityāha--ita uttaramityādinā | spaṣṭaṃ caitatkaiyaṭodyotayoḥ | jhrjñādṛṭa rīṅṛtaḥ (sū.7-4-27) rīṅo dīrghoccāraṇam aṅgavṛttaparibhāṣājñāpakam | artipirpatyośca (sū.7-4-77)|| "bahulaṃ chandasi' ityeva siddhe artigrahaṇamiyarterbhāṣāyāṃ sādhutvajñāpakam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu ṛdhātoḥ pṝdhātośca juhotyādipaṭhitayorabhyāsasya ikārādeśo vidhīyate "artipirpatyośca' iti sūtreṇa | tatra ṛdhātoḥ dhāndasatvāt "bahulaṃ chandasi' (sū.7-4-78) ityanenaiva abhyāsasyekāraḥ syācchandasītyarthakena ikārādeśasiddheḥ artigrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkya ṛdhātorbhāṣāyāmapi prayogasya sādhutvajñāpanārthaṃ tadityāha--ityeva siddha iti | jhrjñā.ṭa guṇo yaṅ (sū. 7-4-82)|| "dīrgho'kitaḥ' (sū.7-4-83) ityakidgrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati abyāsavikāreṣu bādhakā na bādhanta iti | jhrvi.ṭa paribhāṣeyam | jhrjñā.ṭa dīrgho'kitaḥ (sū.7-4-83) hrasvatvātvetvaguṇeṣu abyāsasyeti acṛkārākārekāraviśeṣaṇam | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "dīrgho'kitaḥ' iti sūtre "akitaḥ' iti vyartham | na ca tadabhāve yaṃyamyate raṃramyata ityādau yam ram iti dhātvoḥ yaṅi dhātvordvitve halādiśeṣe "nugato'nunāsikāntasya' (sū.7-4-85) ityabyāsasya nuki tasyānusvāre ca yaṃyamyate raṃramyata iti rūpe | tatrābhyāsasya dīrghavāraṇāya akiṃta ityuktam | nuki sati kidantatvādabhyāsasyākidantatvaṃ nāstīti na doṣaḥ | tathā ca
sārthakamakidgrahaṇamiti vācyam | dīrghaśrutyā "acaḥ' (1-2-28) iti paribhāṣāyā upasthiteḥ aca ityasya cābhyāsaviśeṣaṇatvāt ajantasyābyāsasya dīrgha iti sūtrārthaḥ | nuki kṛte ca nakārānto'bhyāso nājanta ityeva dīrghāprasakterakidgrahaṇavairyathyāt | evaṃ chodhātoḥ "ādeca upadeśe' (sū.6-1-45) ityātve liṭi chāśabdasya dvitve'ntaraṅgatvāt tuki (sū.6-1-75) chāt chā atu rityatra "hrasva' (sū.4-4-59) iti sūtreṇābhyāsasya hrasvo vaktavyaḥ| tatrāpi hrasvaśrutyā "aca' hatyupasthiteḥ tasya cābhyāsaviśeṣaṇatvāt ajantābhyāsasya hrasva iti tatsūtrārthaḥ | tuki kṛte cābyāsasyājantatvābhāvena hrasvo na sidhyati | tataśca cacchadatuḥ iti rūpaṃ na prāpnoti | evaṃ cacchṛdattaḥ ityatra chṛdadhātordvivacane halādiśeṣe ca "che ca' (sū.6-1-73) iti tuki "urat' iti sūtreṇa ṛkārasyātvaṃ na syāt | urityasyābyāsaviśeṣaṇatvena ṛvarṇāntabhyāsasyātvavidhānāt tuki kṛte abhyāsasya ṛvarṇāntatvābhāvāt | tathā "cicchādayiṣati' ityatra chadadhātorṇijantāt sani dhātordvitve halādiśeṣe ca a chādayiṣati iti sthite "che ca' iti tuki "sanyataḥ' (sū.7-4-79) itītvaṃ na prāpnoti, abyāsasyādantatvābhāvāt | tathā "cecchidyate' ityatra chidadhātoryaṅi dvitve halādiśeṣe rcatve ci a chidyate iti sthite "che ca' iti tuki "guṇo yaṅlukoḥ' (sū.7-4-82) iti guṇo na syāt | guṇaśrutyā "ikaḥ' (sū.1-1-3) ityupasthiteḥ tasya cābhyāsaviśeṣaṇatvena igantābhyāsasyaiva guṇavidhānenakṛte|bhyāsasyegantatvābhāvādityāśaṅkyāha---hrasvatvetyādi | ayamāśayaḥ--pradarśiteṣu sūtreṣu viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve kāmacārāt "acaḥ' ityādikaṃ nābhyāsaviśeṣaṇam | api tu abyāsasyetyetadeva aca ityāderviśeṣaṇam | tena abhyāsasya yo'c tasya dīrghaḥ abhyāsasya ya ṛkārastasya atvam, abyāsasya yo'kārastasya ikārodeśaḥ, abyāsasya ya ik tasya guma iti pradarśitānāṃ sūtrāṇāmartha iti yathāyathaṃ hrasvādayaḥ sidhyanti | evaṃ ca yaṃyamyata ityatrāpi nuki kṛte'pi abhyāsāvayavācaḥ dīrghaprāptau tanniṣedhārtham akita ityuktamiti | iti saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
<8>
| atha aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ |
jhrjñā.ṭa sarvasya dve (sū.8-1-1)|| kaskādiṣu kautaskutaśabdapāṭhāt jñāpakāt dviḥ prayogapakṣe'pi paunaḥ punyamityādisiddhiḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "sarvasya dve' iti sūtreṇa vidhīyamānaṃ dvirvacanaṃ dviḥ prayoga iti pakṣe punaḥ śabdasya dvirvacane punaḥ punarbhavitari vartamānāt punaḥ punaḥ śabdāt bhāve ṣyañpratyaye paunaḥ punyamiti, bhavārthe "kālaṭṭhañ' (sū.4-3-11) iti ṭhañi paunaḥ punikamiti ca rūpamiṣyate, tanna sidhyet | samāsagrahaṇakṛtena niyamena arthavataḥ samāsarūpasamudāyasyaiva prātipadikasaṃjñābhyanujñānena punaḥ punariti samudāyasya prātipadikatvābhāvena prātipadikādhikārīyataddhitānupapatterityāśaṅkyāha-- kaskādiṣviti | kutaskutaḥ śabdasyāprātipadikatvena tato'ṇo durlabhatvena kautaskuta ityaṇantapāṭho'saṃgataḥ san dvirūktādaprātipadikādapi taddhitotpattirbhavatīti jñāpayati | ato na doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | jhrjñā.ṭa jātvapūrvam (sū.8-1-47)|| "āho utāho' (sū.8-1-49) ityatrānantaragrahaṇāt jñāpakāt apūrvagrahaṇaṃ jātuviśeṣaṇaṃ na tiṅantasya | tatrāpyapūrvamityanuvṛtteḥ | jhrvi.ṭa nanu "jātvapūrvam' ityatra apūrvamityasya avidyamānapūrvamityarthaḥ | tatrāpūrvamiti kiṃ jātuviśeṣaṇam uta tiṅantaviśeṣaṇam iti saṃdehaḥ | tatra prathamapakṣe avidyamānapūrvaṃ yajjātu tadyuktaṃ tiṅantaṃ nānudāttamityarthaḥ | jātu bhokṣyasa ityādikamudāharaṇam | dvitīyapakṣe jātuśabdādapūrvaṃ tiṅantaṃ nānudāttamityarthaḥ | tatra kataraḥ pakṣaḥ prāmāṇika ityāśaṅkyāha--āho utāho iti | ayamāśayaḥ- "āho utāho cānantaram' (sū.8-1-49) iti sūtreṇa āho utāho ityābhyāmanantaraṃ tiṅantaṃ na nihanyata iti bodhyate | āho bhuṅkye utāho bhuṅkte ityudāharaṇe | tatrāpi "jātvapūrvam' iti sūtrāt apūrvamityanuvartate | tadyadi tiṅantaviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt tarhi avidyamānapūrvaṃ tiṅntaṃ āho utāho ityābhyāmanantarameva bhaviṣyatīti "āho devadattaḥ pacati' ityatra sapūrvatvādeva tiṅantasya nighātaniṣedho na bhaviṣyatīti "āho utāho' iti sūtre anantaragrahaṇaṃ vyartham | kṛtaṃ ca tat, apūrvamityetanna tiṅantaviśeṣaṇam, api tu nimittaviśeṣaṇamiti jñāpayati | tathā ca apūrvāt āho ityasmāt utāho ityasmācca tiṅantaṃ nānudāttamityukte deva āho bhuṅkta iti sapūrvasya āhośabdasya vyāvartane'pi āho devadattaḥ pacatīti sapūrvaṃ tiṅantaṃ na vyāvartitaṃ bhavatīti tadhdyāvṛttaye anantaragrahaṇam | tena "jātvapūrvam' ityatrāpi apūrvamityetat nimittasya jātu ityasyaiva viśeṣaṇaṃ na tiṅantasyeti niścīyata iti | ityaṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ śrīveṃkaṭeśasya tathā gurūṇāṃ budhottamānāṃ karuṇārdradṛṣṭyā | śrīpārthasārathyabhimānataśca vyākhyeyamāpūri vinaiva vighnam || nāvalpāketi vikhyāte sūdhīgaṇavibhūṣite | agrahāre labdhajanmā catuśśāstraśiromaṇiḥ || rāmānujācāryanāmā kṛṣṇatātasudhīsutaḥ | bālānāṃ sukhabodhāya vivṛtiṃ kṛtavānimāma ||
